`README.en.md`:

```md
# Hyapk (English)

Hyapk is a protection and hardening packer for Android applications and
games, at both the native and the smali/dex level.

| | |
|---|---|
| Name | Hyapk |
| Version | 1.0.0 |
| Developers | beto2-dev (lead developer and BL Cyber lead) and ChapzoMods |
| License | AGPL-3.0-only (see [LICENSE](LICENSE)) |

> WARNING: Hyapk may stop being distributed, be shut down, or become a
> closed-source and/or commercial product at any time without prior notice.

> ADVERTENCIA: Hyapk puede dejar de distribuirse, cerrarse o pasar a ser un
> producto de codigo cerrado y/o comercial en cualquier momento y sin previo
> aviso.

> 警告：Hyapk 可能随时停止分发、关闭或转变为闭源和/或商业产品，恕不另行通知。

Languages: [Espanol](README.md) | English (this file) | [中文](README.zh.md)

## Features

- Advanced signature verification: the SHA-256 digest of the signing
  certificate is embedded encrypted in the native payload and validated at
  every start.
- Advanced Anti Tamper + Anti Debug: SHA-256 hashes of every dex, TracerPid
  detection, ptrace checks and periodic sweeps by a guard thread.
- Real per-method VMP: selected methods are converted into programs for the
  custom HyVm virtual machine with per-build opcode permutation and
  ChaCha20-Poly1305 encryption.
- Fully functional Dex2C: selected methods are translated to generated C,
  compiled into libhyapkrt.so and bound with RegisterNatives.
- Smali class renaming: identifier obfuscation with keep rules (manifest
  components, JNI, user patterns) plus mapping.txt generation.
- RES resource obfuscation: assets are encrypted with ChaCha20-Poly1305,
  renamed to hashes and decrypted at runtime through a JNI bridge.
- Modern cryptography: ChaCha20-Poly1305 (RFC 8439), SHA-256, HMAC-SHA256 and
  HKDF, with key material split between the native library and the payload.
- Advanced hardening: v1+v2+v3 signing, 16 KB alignment for Android 15+ page
  sizes, uncompressed stored dex for integrity checks, hardened compiler
  flags.
- Advanced AntiFrida/AntiRoot: /proc artifact scanning, Frida thread names,
  default ports, root binaries, Magisk, Zygisk and Riru detection.

## Quick start

Requirements: JDK 17+, Android SDK and NDK r27+, Gradle 8.10+, CMake and
Ninja.

```sh
# build the CLI
gradle :hyapk-cli:fatJar

# protect an APK
java -jar hyapk-cli/build/libs/hyapk-1.0.0-all.jar protect app.apk \
  -o app-protected.apk \
  --dex2c 'com.example.Calculator#*' \
  --vmp 'com.example.Core#verify' \
  --gen-keystore my-key.jks

# verify the result
java -jar hyapk-cli/build/libs/hyapk-1.0.0-all.jar verify app-protected.apk
```

## Caracteristicas

- Verificacion de firma avanzada: el digest SHA-256 del certificado firmante
  se incrusta cifrado en el payload nativo y se valida en cada arranque.
- Anti Tamper + Anti Debug avanzados: hashes SHA-256 de todos los dex,
  deteccion de TracerPid, ptrace y revisiones periodicas en un hilo guardián.
- VMP real por metodo: los metodos seleccionados se convierten a programas de
  maquina virtual propia (HyVm) con opcodes permutados por build y cifrado
  ChaCha20-Poly1305.
- Dex2C totalmente funcional: los metodos seleccionados se traducen a C
  autogenerado compilado dentro de libhyapkrt.so y se registran con
  RegisterNatives.
- Renombrado de clases smali: ofuscacion de identificadores con reglas de
  conservacion (manifest, JNI, patrones de usuario) y generacion de
  mapping.txt.
- Ofuscacion de recursos RES: los assets se cifran con ChaCha20-Poly1305, se
  renombran con hashes y se descifran en runtime mediante un puente JNI.
- Criptografia moderna: ChaCha20-Poly1305 (RFC 8439), SHA-256, HMAC-SHA256 y
  HKDF, con claves divididas entre la libreria nativa y el payload.
- Hardening avanzado: firma v1+v2+v3, alineacion de 16 KB para paginas de
  Android 15+, dex almacenados sin compresion para verificacion de
  integridad, flags de compilacion endurecidos.
- AntiFrida/AntiRoot avanzado: deteccion de artefactos en /proc, nombres de
  hilos de Frida, puertos por defecto, binarios de root, Magisk, Zygisk y
  Riru.

## 功能特性

- 高级签名校验：签名证书的 SHA-256 摘要以加密形式嵌入原生 payload，
  并在每次启动时校验。
- 高级防篡改与反调试：全部 dex 的 SHA-256 哈希、TracerPid 检测、
  ptrace 检查，以及守护线程的周期性巡查。
- 按方法的真实 VMP：被选中的方法被转换为自定义 HyVm 虚拟机程序，
  每次构建使用独立置换的操作码表并以 ChaCha20-Poly1305 加密。
- 完整可用的 Dex2C：被选中的方法被翻译为自动生成的 C 代码，
  编译进 libhyapkrt.so 并通过 RegisterNatives 绑定。
- smali 类重命名：带保留规则（manifest 组件、JNI、用户模式）的
  标识符混淆，并生成 mapping.txt。
- RES 资源混淆：assets 使用 ChaCha20-Poly1305 加密、更名为哈希名，
  并在运行时通过 JNI 桥解密。
- 现代密码学：ChaCha20-Poly1305（RFC 8439）、SHA-256、HMAC-SHA256
  与 HKDF，密钥材料在原生库与 payload 之间拆分。
- 高级加固：v1+v2+v3 签名、适配 Android 15+ 的 16 KB 页对齐、
  为完整性校验而采用存储不压缩的 dex、强化的编译选项。
- 高级反 Frida/反 Root：扫描 /proc 痕迹、Frida 线程名、默认端口、
  root 二进制、Magisk、Zygisk 与 Riru 检测。

`docs/en/architecture.md`:

```md
# Hyapk architecture

## Overview

Hyapk turns an input APK into a protected APK through a pass pipeline
that operates on the zip container, the dex bytecode and a per-build
generated native runtime.

```
input APK
   |
   v
[1] extraction and analysis (AXML manifest, dex inventory)
   |
   v
[2] dex passes:
    hardening -> bootstrap -> assets -> strings -> rename -> dex2c/vmp
   |
   v
[3] final dex write (stored uncompressed)
   |
   v
[4] encrypted payload assembly + C code generation
   |
   v
[5] libhyapkrt.so build per ABI (NDK + CMake)
   |
   v
[6] repackage (16 KB alignment, stable entry order)
   |
   v
[7] v1+v2+v3 signing and final verification
```

## Components

### hyapk-cli (Kotlin)

- `apk/ZipIO.kt`: zip reader and deterministic aligned writer; native
  libraries at 16 KB, resources.arsc at 4 bytes, dex stored uncompressed
  so the runtime can hash them.
- `apk/Axml.kt`: minimal binary AXML parser to read the package,
  application and launcher activities, and to flip manifest booleans
  without changing the file size.
- `dex/DexStore.kt`: loads and saves every classes*.dex through dexlib2.
- `passes/`: the pipeline passes and the payload builder.
- `ir/`: the dex to Hyir translator plus the C and HyVm emitters.

### hyapk-native (C)

- `crypto/`: SHA-256, HMAC, HKDF, ChaCha20-Poly1305 and the payload KDF.
- `vm/`: HyVm, a register virtual machine with 47 opcodes, per-build
  permuted opcode map and encrypted programs.
- `protect/`: anti-debug, anti-frida, anti-root and emulator heuristics.
- `jni/`: payload load and decryption, JNI bridge for the VM and the
  dex2c code, signature verification, dex hash verification and the
  guard thread.

## Payload format

The payload is a ChaCha20-Poly1305 encrypted blob embedded in the native
library. Its key derives from a library-held secret (XOR obfuscated)
combined with a random per-file salt stored in the blob header: holding
only the APK or only the library is not enough to recover the key.

```
header (72 bytes, cleartext):
  magic 'HPAY' | version | flags | plaintext size | nonce(12) | salt(32) | tag(16)
body: encrypted sections
```

Sections: strings (1), asset map (2), dex hashes (3), certificate digest
(4), VMP programs (5), method table (6), types (7), fields (8) and
configuration (9).

## HyVm (VMP)

Each converted method is translated to Hyir and emitted as a HyVm program:

```
header: magic 'HYVM' | version | code_size | reg_count | seed
        nonce(12) | key_half(32) | tag(16) | opcode_map(64)
code encrypted with ChaCha20-Poly1305 (AAD = first 16 bytes)
```

The interpreter validates bounds on every register, branch and operand
access, and any single byte tamper breaks the Poly1305 tag.

## Dex2C

The same Hyir translator feeds a C emitter. Generated functions use a
thin `hy_d2c_*` API over the same JNI bridge as the VM, compile into
libhyapkrt.so and register with RegisterNatives under the original
method name and signature.

## Bootstrap injection

Three runtime classes are injected (`Sec`, `HyapkBridge`, `Assets`) and
the Application class (or the launcher activity when no Application
exists) is modified to:

1. load libhyapkrt.so in `<clinit>`;
2. call `HyapkBridge.initNative(Context)` right after
   `super.attachBaseContext`.

`initNative` verifies the signature, checks the dex hashes and starts
the guard thread.

## Translation subset

The dex2c/VMP translators deliberately support an auditable subset of
bytecode: constants, moves, int/long/double arithmetic, comparisons,
branches, invocations (including range), primitive fields and arrays,
check-cast, instanceof, monitor, throw and returns. Methods with
try/catch, float, object arrays, object fields or rare instructions are
excluded and reported. Extending the subset only requires editing
`ir/Hyir.kt` and the emitters.

## Known limitations

- Object-typed fields are not translated (primitives are).
- Hash checking covers stored uncompressed dex; with `--compress-dex`
  the dex integrity check is disabled.
- Native library authenticity relies on the APK signature, not on a
  self-hash (which would be circular).

```

`docs/en/cli-reference.md`:

```md
# CLI reference

```
hyapk <command> [options]

Commands:
  protect <input.apk> -o <output.apk> [options]
  verify <apk>
  doctor
  version
```

## protect

| Option | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `-o, --output` | (required) | output APK |
| `--passes` | hardening,bootstrap,strings,assets,rename,protect | comma separated list; add dex2c,vmp |
| `--dex2c` | (none) | method selection pattern (repeatable) |
| `--vmp` | (none) | method selection pattern (repeatable) |
| `--keep` | (none) | rename keep rule (repeatable) |
| `--keystore` | (none) | existing PKCS12/JKS keystore |
| `--keystore-pass` | (none) | keystore password |
| `--key-alias` | hyapk | key alias |
| `--key-pass` | = keystore-pass | key password |
| `--gen-keystore` | (none) | generate a new keystore when none is given |
| `--abi` | arm64-v8a,armeabi-v7a,x86_64,x86 | ABIs to build |
| `--ndk` | env ANDROID_NDK_HOME | NDK path |
| `--runtime-src` | env HYAPK_RUNTIME_SRC | hyapk-native sources |
| `--min-api` | 21 | minimum supported API (Android 5.0+) |
| `--skip-root-check` | off | disable the runtime root heuristic |
| `--skip-emulator-check` | off | disable the runtime emulator heuristic |
| `--compress-dex` | off | compress dex (disables the dex hash check) |
| `--workdir` | tmp | working directory |
| `--report-dir` | workdir/report | report destination |
| `-v / -q` | off | verbose / quiet |

## Selection patterns

- `com.example.Calc` or `com.example.Calc#*`: the whole class.
- `com.example.Calc#fib`: a single method.
- `com.example.**`: the package tree.

## verify

Verifies signatures (v1/v2/v3) and container structure: stored entries,
4 byte alignment of resources.arsc and 16 KB alignment of native
libraries. Exits 1 on failure.

## doctor

Checks Java, the NDK and the native runtime sources.

## Environment variables

- `ANDROID_NDK_HOME` / `ANDROID_NDK` / `ANDROID_HOME`: NDK detection.
- `HYAPK_RUNTIME_SRC`: hyapk-native sources.

## Exit codes

- 0: success.
- 1: error (invalid input, build failure, failed verification).
- 2: usage error.

```

`docs/en/compatibility.md`:

```md
# Compatibility

## Android versions

| Version | API | Status |
|---|---|---|
| Android 9 Pie | 28 | supported |
| Android 10 | 29 | supported |
| Android 11 | 30 | supported |
| Android 12/12L | 31/32 | supported |
| Android 13 | 33 | supported |
| Android 14 | 34 | supported |
| Android 15 | 35 | supported (includes 16 KB pages) |
| Android 16 | 36 | supported |

The native runtime builds against `ANDROID_PLATFORM=android-21` and only
uses public NDK APIs, so it runs unchanged from Android 5.0 to current
releases.

## Relevant compatibility details

- Android 11+ (API 30): resources.arsc is stored uncompressed and aligned
  to 4 bytes as the installer requires.
- Android 15+ with 16 KB pages: native libraries are stored uncompressed
  and aligned to 16 KB (`-Wl,-z,max-page-size=16384`).
- Android 14 (API 34): no hidden APIs and no additional dynamic code
  loading; converted methods are replaced within the same dex and bound
  through RegisterNatives.
- v3 signing (key rotation) in addition to v1 and v2.

## Supported inputs

- Signed or unsigned APK input; the output is always signed.
- Multidex (classes.dex, classes2.dex, ...): every slot is processed and
  preserved.
- Assets: every file under `assets/` is encrypted and renamed;
  `AssetManager.open(String)` calls are redirected to the bridge.

## Tested build environments

- Ubuntu with OpenJDK 17/21, Gradle 8.10, NDK r27, CMake 3.18+.
- GitHub Actions `ubuntu-latest` (the `ci` workflow).

## Notes

- Apps reading assets through `openFd` or other methods besides
  `open(String)` will not have those reads redirected: use `open` only or
  adjust the code before protecting.
- Class renaming preserves manifest components, classes with native
  methods and common library prefixes; add `--keep` rules for classes
  accessed through reflection.

```

`docs/en/faq.md`:

```md
# FAQ

## The process dies at startup after protecting

Common causes:

1. The APK was re-signed with a key different from the one used during
   protection. The certificate digest is captured from the signing key;
   both must match.
2. `--compress-dex` was used and the APK was modified afterwards.
3. The root checks detect the environment (userdebug emulator images).
   Use `--skip-root-check --skip-emulator-check` for testing.

## A class stopped working after renaming

It is probably accessed through reflection (Class.forName, serialization,
injection). Add `--keep com.my.package.MyClass` and protect again.

## A method was not converted

The translator supports a bytecode subset (see architecture). Methods
with try/catch, float, object arrays or object fields are excluded and
listed in `protection-report.txt` with the reason.

## Can I protect apps with Google services or Firebase

Yes. Common library prefixes (androidx, kotlin, com/google, okhttp,
retrofit, etc.) are preserved by default. If you use manual reflection
over your own classes, add `--keep` rules.

## What does the closure notice mean

The project may stop being distributed or become commercial at any
time without prior notice. If you build critical products on Hyapk,
keep a copy of the version you use and consider supporting development.

## How do I contribute

Contributions are welcome. Open an issue or a pull request. By
contributing you agree that your code is distributed under
AGPL-3.0-only.

## Is there a warranty

No. The software is provided as is, without warranty of any kind, as
detailed in the AGPL-3.0 license.

```

`docs/en/getting-started.md`:

```md
# Hyapk getting started

## Requirements

- JDK 17 or newer.
- Android SDK with `platforms;android-35` and `build-tools;35.0.0`.
- Android NDK r27 or newer (required for the native runtime).
- Gradle 8.10 or newer.
- CMake 3.18+ and Ninja.

No paths are hardcoded: the NDK is located via `ANDROID_NDK_HOME`,
`ANDROID_NDK`, `ANDROID_HOME/ndk/*` or `--ndk`; the native sources via
`HYAPK_RUNTIME_SRC` or `--runtime-src`.

## Build

```sh
git clone <this repository>
cd Hyapk
gradle :hyapk-cli:fatJar
```

This produces `hyapk-cli/build/libs/hyapk-1.0.0-all.jar`, a self-contained
jar with all dependencies.

## Check the environment

```sh
java -jar hyapk-1.0.0-all.jar doctor
```

## Protect an APK

```sh
java -jar hyapk-1.0.0-all.jar protect input.apk -o output.apk [options]
```

By default the passes `hardening, bootstrap, strings, assets, rename,
protect` run. The `dex2c` and `vmp` passes activate when methods are
selected with `--dex2c` and `--vmp`.

### Signing

Without a keystore Hyapk generates an ephemeral RSA-4096 key and warns.
For reproducible signing use `--keystore` or generate a persistent one
with `--gen-keystore` on the first run and reuse it afterwards.

### Method selection

- `com.example.Calc` or `com.example.Calc#*`: every method of the class.
- `com.example.Calc#fib`: a single method.
- `com.example.**`: the whole package tree.

Methods using instructions outside the supported subset (try/catch,
float, object arrays, object fields) are skipped and reported.

## Verify a protected APK

```sh
java -jar hyapk-1.0.0-all.jar verify output.apk
```

Checks v1/v2/v3 signatures plus container structure (stored entries and
16 KB alignment of native libraries).

## Running the tests

```sh
cd hyapk-native
cmake -S . -B build-host && cmake --build build-host && ctest --test-dir build-host

gradle :hyapk-cli:test
```

The crypto tests share KAT vectors between C and Kotlin so both
implementations must match byte-for-byte.

## Reports

Every run writes `protection-report.txt` and, when renaming happened,
`mapping.txt` into the report directory (`--report-dir`).

```

`docs/en/threat-model.md`:

```md
# Threat model

## Goal

Measurably raise the cost of reverse engineering, fraudulent
repackaging, dynamic instrumentation and runtime tampering of an Android
application, while staying compatible with Android 9 through 16.

## Threat actors

| Actor | Capabilities | Main mitigations |
|---|---|---|
| Static reverser | jadx, apktool, ghidra | renaming, string encryption, encrypted assets, dex2c and VMP move logic out of the dex |
| Repackager | rebuild/repackage/re-sign | signature verification against the embedded digest, dex hashes, hard exit |
| Dynamic analyst | frida, xposed, debuggers | anti-debug, anti-frida, guard thread, logic outside the dex |
| Hostile device | root, magisk, emulators | anti-root, emulator heuristics, configurable policies |

## What each piece protects

1. Signature verification: the SHA-256 digest of the signing certificate
   is stored encrypted inside the payload. An APK re-signed with another
   key produces a different digest and the process dies in `initNative`.
2. Dex anti-tamper: every stored classes*.dex is hashed at build time;
   the runtime re-hashes at startup and terminates on mismatch.
3. Anti-debug: TracerPid reads and a probing PTRACE_TRACEME, both run
   periodically.
4. Anti-Frida: /proc/self/maps artifacts, agent thread names
   (gum-js-loop, gmain, pool-frida), default port probes and environment
   variables.
5. Anti-root: known binaries and paths (su, magisk), mount points,
   test-keys props, Zygisk/Riru artifacts.
6. VMP/dex2c: selected logic no longer exists as readable bytecode; it
   lives encrypted as a HyVm program or compiled to native C.
7. String and asset encryption: literals and resources resolve at
   runtime from the encrypted payload.

## Residual risks (honesty)

- No on-device protection is absolute: an attacker controlling the
  system can patch the native library, hook functions or dump memory.
  The goal is cost, not impossibility.
- The encryption key exists in process memory while in use; an attacker
  with root plus ptrace can extract it.
- The dex2c/VMP translation subset is deliberately conservative;
  excluded methods remain as obfuscated dex bytecode.
- Root/emulator checks are heuristics and occasionally produce false
  positives; that is why they are configurable
  (`--skip-root-check`, `--skip-emulator-check`).

## Reaction policy

The default reaction is immediate process termination (`_exit(1)`) with
a logcat record. The implementation is isolated in `hy_react` to allow
alternative policies in future versions.

```

`docs/es/architecture.md`:

```md
# Arquitectura de Hyapk

## Vision general

Hyapk transforma un APK de entrada en un APK protegido mediante un
pipeline de pasos que operan sobre el contenedor zip, el bytecode dex y un
runtime nativo generado por build.

```
APK de entrada
    |
    v
[1] extraccion y analisis (manifest AXML, inventario dex)
    |
    v
[2] pasos sobre dex:
    hardening -> bootstrap -> assets -> strings -> rename -> dex2c/vmp
    |
    v
[3] escritura de dex finales (almacenados sin compresion)
    |
    v
[4] construccion del payload cifrado + generacion de C
    |
    v
[5] compilacion de libhyapkrt.so por ABI (NDK + CMake)
    |
    v
[6] reempaquetado (alineacion de 16 KB, orden estable de entradas)
    |
    v
[7] firma v1+v2+v3 y verificacion final
```

## Componentes

### hyapk-cli (Kotlin)

- `apk/ZipIO.kt`: lector zip y escritor determinista con alineacion por
  entrada; bibliotecas nativas a 16 KB, resources.arsc a 4 bytes, dex
  almacenados sin compresion para que el runtime pueda hashearlos.
- `apk/Axml.kt`: parser binario AXML minimo para leer package, aplicacion,
  actividades lanzadoras y voltear booleanos del manifest sin cambiar el
  tamano del archivo.
- `dex/DexStore.kt`: carga/guarda de todos los classes*.dex via dexlib2 y
  utilidades para reemplazar clases.
- `passes/`: los pasos del pipeline y la construccion del payload.
- `ir/`: el traductor dex -> Hyir y los emisores a C y a bytecode HyVm.

### hyapk-native (C)

- `crypto/`: SHA-256, HMAC, HKDF, ChaCha20-Poly1305 y el KDF del payload.
- `vm/`: HyVm, una maquina virtual de registros con 47 opcodes, mapa de
  opcodes permutado por build y programas cifrados.
- `protect/`: anti-debug, anti-frida, anti-root y heuristica de emulador.
- `jni/`: carga y descifrado del payload, puente JNI para la VM y el
  codigo dex2c, verificacion de firma, verificacion de hashes dex y
  hilo guardián.

## Formato del payload

El payload es un blob cifrado con ChaCha20-Poly1305 incrustado en la
biblioteca nativa. La clave se deriva combinando un secreto guardado en la
libreria (ofuscado con XOR) y una sal aleatoria por archivo incluida en la
cabecera del blob: poseer solo el APK o solo la libreria no basta para
reconstruir la clave.

```
cabecera (72 bytes, en claro):
  magic 'HPAY' | version | flags | tamano plano | nonce(12) | sal(32) | tag(16)
cuerpo: secciones cifradas
```

Secciones: cadenas (1), mapa de assets (2), hashes de dex (3), digest del
certificado (4), programas VMP (5), tabla de metodos (6), tipos (7),
campos (8) y configuracion (9).

## HyVm (VMP)

Cada metodo convertido se traduce a Hyir y se emite como programa binario
para HyVm:

```
cabecera: magic 'HYVM' | version | code_size | reg_count | seed
          nonce(12) | key_half(32) | tag(16) | opcode_map(64)
codigo cifrado con ChaCha20-Poly1305 (AAD = primeros 16 bytes)
```

El interprete valida limites en cada acceso a registro, rama y operando,
y cualquier manipulacion de un byte del programa rompe el tag Poly1305.

## Dex2C

El mismo traductor Hyir alimenta un emisor de C. Las funciones generadas
usan un API fino (`hy_d2c_*`) sobre el mismo puente JNI de la VM, se
compilan dentro de libhyapkrt.so y se registran con RegisterNatives bajo
el nombre y firma originales.

## Inyeccion de arranque

Se inyectan tres clases runtime (`Sec`, `HyapkBridge`, `Assets`) y se
modifica la clase Application (o la actividad lanzadora si no existe
Application) para:

1. cargar libhyapkrt.so en `<clinit>`;
2. llamar `HyapkBridge.initNative(Context)` justo despues de
   `super.attachBaseContext`.

En `initNative` se verifica la firma, se comprueban los hashes de los dex
y arranca el hilo guardián.

## Subconjunto de traduccion

Los traductores dex2c/VMP soportan de forma deliberada un subconjunto
auditable del bytecode: constantes, movimientos, aritmetica int/long/double,
comparaciones, ramas, invocaciones (incluyendo range), campos y arrays
primitivos, check-cast, instanceof, monitor, throw y retornos. Los metodos
con try/catch, float, arrays de objetos, campos de objeto o instrucciones
raras se excluyen y se reportan. Ampliar el subconjunto solo requiere
editar `ir/Hyir.kt` y los emisores.

## Limitaciones conocidas

- Los campos de tipo objeto no se traducen (los primitivos si).
- La comprobacion de hashes cubre los dex almacenados sin compresion; con
  `--compress-dex` el chequeo de integridad de dex se desactiva.
- La autenticacion de la libreria nativa se apoya en la firma del APK, no
  en un auto-hash (evitar circularidad).

```

`docs/es/cli-reference.md`:

```md
# Referencia del CLI

```
hyapk <comando> [opciones]

Comandos:
  protect <input.apk> -o <output.apk> [opciones]
  verify <apk>
  doctor
  version
```

## protect

| Opcion | Valor por defecto | Descripcion |
|---|---|---|
| `-o, --output` | (obligatorio) | APK de salida |
| `--passes` | hardening,bootstrap,strings,assets,rename,protect | lista separada por comas; añadir dex2c,vmp |
| `--dex2c` | (ninguno) | patron de seleccion de metodos (repetible) |
| `--vmp` | (ninguno) | patron de seleccion de metodos (repetible) |
| `--keep` | (ninguno) | regla de conservacion para el renombrado (repetible) |
| `--keystore` | (ninguno) | keystore PKCS12/JKS existente |
| `--keystore-pass` | (ninguno) | contrasena del keystore |
| `--key-alias` | hyapk | alias de la clave |
| `--key-pass` | = keystore-pass | contrasena de la clave |
| `--gen-keystore` | (ninguno) | genera un keystore nuevo si no se entrega uno |
| `--abi` | arm64-v8a,armeabi-v7a,x86_64,x86 | ABIs a construir |
| `--ndk` | env ANDROID_NDK_HOME | ruta del NDK |
| `--runtime-src` | env HYAPK_RUNTIME_SRC | fuentes de hyapk-native |
| `--min-api` | 21 | API minimo soportado (Android 5.0+) |
| `--skip-root-check` | off | desactiva la heuristica de root en runtime |
| `--skip-emulator-check` | off | desactiva la heuristica de emulador |
| `--compress-dex` | off | dex comprimidos (desactiva el chequeo de hashes dex) |
| `--workdir` | tmp | directorio de trabajo |
| `--report-dir` | workdir/report | destino de informes |
| `-v / -q` | off | verboso / silencioso |

## Patrones de seleccion

- `com.ejemplo.Calc` o `com.ejemplo.Calc#*`: toda la clase.
- `com.ejemplo.Calc#fib`: un metodo.
- `com.ejemplo.**`: paquete completo con subpaquetes.

## verify

Verifica firmas (v1/v2/v3) y la estructura del contenedor: entradas
almacenadas, alineacion a 4 bytes de resources.arsc y alineacion de
16 KB de las bibliotecas nativas. Devuelve exit 1 ante fallos.

## doctor

Comprueba Java, NDK y fuentes nativas del runtime.

## Variables de entorno

- `ANDROID_NDK_HOME` / `ANDROID_NDK` / `ANDROID_HOME`: deteccion del NDK.
- `HYAPK_RUNTIME_SRC`: fuentes de hyapk-native.

## Codigos de salida

- 0: exito.
- 1: error (entrada invalida, compilacion fallida, verificacion fallida).
- 2: uso incorrecto.

```

`docs/es/compatibility.md`:

```md
# Compatibilidad

## Versiones de Android

| Version | API | Estado |
|---|---|---|
| Android 9 Pie | 28 | soportado |
| Android 10 | 29 | soportado |
| Android 11 | 30 | soportado |
| Android 12/12L | 31/32 | soportado |
| Android 13 | 33 | soportado |
| Android 14 | 34 | soportado |
| Android 15 | 35 | soportado (incluye paginas de 16 KB) |
| Android 16 | 36 | soportado |

El runtime nativo se construye con `ANDROID_PLATFORM=android-21` y usa
unicamente APIs publicas del NDK, por lo que se ejecuta sin cambios desde
Android 5.0 hasta las versiones actuales.

## Detalles de compatibilidad relevantes

- Android 11+ (API 30): `resources.arsc` se almacena sin comprimir y
  alineado a 4 bytes, como exige el instalador.
- Android 15+ con paginas de 16 KB: las bibliotecas nativas se almacenan
  sin comprimir y alineadas a 16 KB (`-Wl,-z,max-page-size=16384`).
- Android 14 (API 34): no se usan APIs ocultas ni dynamic code loading
  de dex adicionales; los metodos convertidos se sustituyen en el mismo
  dex y se enlazan via RegisterNatives.
- Firma v3 (rotacion de claves) ademas de v1 y v2.

## Formatos de entrada soportados

- APK firmados o sin firmar (entrada); la salida siempre se firma.
- Multidex (classes.dex, classes2.dex, ...): todos los slots se
  procesan y preservan.
- Assets: todos los ficheros bajo `assets/` se cifran y renombran;
  las llamadas a `AssetManager.open(String)` se redirigen al puente.

## Entornos de compilacion probados

- Ubuntu con OpenJDK 17/21, Gradle 8.10, NDK r27, CMake 3.18+.
- GitHub Actions `ubuntu-latest` (workflow `ci`).

## Notas

- Aplicaciones que leen assets con `openFd` u otros metodos distintos de
  `open(String)` no veran redirigidas esas lecturas: use solo `open` o
  ajuste el codigo antes de proteger.
- El renombrado de clases conserva componentes del manifest, clases con
  metodos nativos y prefijos habituales de librerias; añada reglas
  `--keep` para clases accedidas por reflexion.

```

`docs/es/faq.md`:

```md
# Preguntas frecuentes

## El proceso muere al arrancar tras proteger el APK

Las causas mas comunes:

1. El APK se re-firmo con una clave distinta a la usada al proteger.
   El digest del certificado se captura de la clave con la que se firman;
   ambos deben coincidir.
2. Se uso `--compress-dex` y luego se modifico el APK.
3. Los chequeos de root detectan el entorno (emuladores con imagenes
   userdebug). Use `--skip-root-check --skip-emulator-check` en pruebas.

## Una clase dejo de funcionar tras el renombrado

Probablemente se accede por reflexion (Class.forName, serializacion,
inyeccion). Añada `--keep com.mi.paquete.MiClase` y vuelva a proteger.

## Un metodo no aparece como convertido

El traductor soporta un subconjunto del bytecode (ver arquitectura).
Los metodos con try/catch, float, arrays de objetos o campos de objeto
se excluyen y se listan en `protection-report.txt` con el motivo.

## Puedo proteger apps con servicios de Google o Firebase

Si. Los prefijos de librerias comunes (androidx, kotlin, com/google,
okhttp, retrofit, etc.) se conservan por defecto. Si usa reflexion
manual sobre clases propias, añada reglas `--keep`.

## Que significa el aviso de cierre de codigo

Que el proyecto puede dejar de distribuirse o volverse comercial en
cualquier momento sin previo aviso. Si construye productos criticos
sobre Hyapk, mantenga una copia de la version que usa y evalue la
posibilidad de apoyar el desarrollo.

## Como contribuyo

Los aportes son bienvenidos. Abra un issue o un pull request. Al
contribuir acepta que su codigo se distribuya bajo AGPL-3.0-only.

## Hay garantia

No. El software se entrega tal cual, sin garantia de ningun tipo, como
detalla la licencia AGPL-3.0.

```

`docs/es/getting-started.md`:

```md
# Primeros pasos con Hyapk

## Requisitos

- JDK 17 o superior.
- Android SDK con `platforms;android-35` y `build-tools;35.0.0`.
- Android NDK r27 o superior (necesario para el runtime nativo).
- Gradle 8.10 o superior.
- CMake 3.18+ y Ninja (los instala el SDK Manager o el gestor de paquetes).

Ninguna ruta esta fijada en el codigo: el NDK se detecta con
`ANDROID_NDK_HOME`, `ANDROID_NDK`, `ANDROID_HOME/ndk/*` o con la opcion
`--ndk`, y las fuentes nativas con `HYAPK_RUNTIME_SRC` o `--runtime-src`.

## Compilacion

```sh
git clone <este repositorio>
cd Hyapk
gradle :hyapk-cli:fatJar
```

Esto produce `hyapk-cli/build/libs/hyapk-1.0.0-all.jar`, un jar
autocontenido con todas las dependencias.

## Comprobar el entorno

```sh
java -jar hyapk-1.0.0-all.jar doctor
```

El comando doctor revisa Java, el NDK y las fuentes nativas del runtime.

## Proteger un APK

```sh
java -jar hyapk-1.0.0-all.jar protect entrada.apk -o salida.apk [opciones]
```

Por defecto se aplican los pasos `hardening, bootstrap, strings, assets,
rename, protect`. Los pasos `dex2c` y `vmp` se activan seleccionando metodos
con `--dex2c` y `--vmp`.

### Firmado

Si no se entrega un keystore, Hyapk genera una clave efimera RSA-4096 y
avisa. Para firmados reproducibles use:

```sh
java -jar hyapk-1.0.0-all.jar protect entrada.apk -o salida.apk \
  --keystore mi-clave.jks --keystore-pass secreto --key-alias hyapk
```

o genere un keystore persistente con `--gen-keystore salida.jks` en la
primera ejecucion y reutilicelo en las siguientes.

### Seleccion de metodos

Los patrones admiten tres formas:

- `com.ejemplo.Calc` o `com.ejemplo.Calc#*`: todos los metodos de la clase.
- `com.ejemplo.Calc#fib`: un metodo concreto.
- `com.ejemplo.**`: todas las clases del paquete y subpaquetes.

Los metodos que usan instrucciones fuera del subconjunto soportado (por
ejemplo try/catch, float, arrays de objetos o campos de objeto) se omiten y
se listan en el informe con el motivo.

## Verificar un APK protegido

```sh
java -jar hyapk-1.0.0-all.jar verify salida.apk
```

Comprueba las firmas v1/v2/v3 con apksig y la estructura del contenedor
(entradas almacenadas y alineacion de 16 KB para bibliotecas nativas).

## Ejecutar los tests

```sh
# tests nativos (criptografia, VM, payload) en el host
cd hyapk-native
cmake -S . -B build-host && cmake --build build-host && ctest --test-dir build-host

# tests del CLI
gradle :hyapk-cli:test
```

Los tests de criptografia comparten vectores KAT entre C y Kotlin para
garantizar que ambas implementaciones produzcan exactamente los mismos
bytes.

## Informes

Tras cada ejecucion se escriben `protection-report.txt` y, si hubo
renombrados, `mapping.txt` (formato `descriptorViejo -> descriptorNuevo`)
en el directorio de informe (`--report-dir`).

```

`docs/es/threat-model.md`:

```md
# Modelo de amenazas

## Objetivo

Elevar de forma mensurable el costo de la ingenieria inversa, el
empaquetado fraudulento (repackaging), la instrumentacion dinamica y la
manipulacion en runtime de una aplicacion Android, manteniendo la
compatibilidad con Android 9 a 16.

## Actores de amenaza

| Actor | Capacidades | Mitigaciones principales |
|---|---|---|
| Reverser estatico | jadx, apktool, ghidra | renombrado, cifrado de cadenas, assets cifrados, dex2c y VMP sacan la logica del dex |
| Repackager | recompilar/reempaquetar/firmar de nuevo | verificacion de firma contra digest embebido, hashes de dex, exit duro |
| Analista dinamico | frida, xposed, depuracion | anti-debug, anti-frida, hilo guardian, excepciones fuera del dex |
| Dispositivo hostil | root, magisk, emuladores | anti-root, heuristica de emulador, politicas configurables |

## Que protege cada pieza

1. Verificacion de firma: el digest SHA-256 del certificado firmante se
   guarda cifrado dentro del payload. Un APK re-firmado con otra clave
   produce un digest distinto y el proceso termina en `initNative`.
2. Anti tamper de dex: cada classes*.dex almacenado se hashea en tiempo de
   construccion; el runtime re-hashea al arrancar y ante discrepancias
   termina el proceso.
3. Anti debug: lectura de TracerPid en /proc/self/status y
   PTRACE_TRACEME de sondeo; ambos se ejecutan periodicamente.
4. Anti Frida: artefactos en /proc/self/maps, nombres de hilo del agente
   (gum-js-loop, gmain, pool-frida), sondas a puertos por defecto y
   variables de entorno.
5. Anti root: binarios y rutas conocidas (su, magisk), puntos de montaje,
   props test-keys, artefactos Zygisk/Riru.
6. VMP/dex2c: la logica seleccionada deja de existir como bytecode legible;
   vive cifrada como programa HyVm o compilada a C nativo.
7. Cifrado de cadenas y assets: los literales y recursos se resuelven en
   el runtime desde el payload cifrado.

## Riesgos residuales (honestidad)

- Ninguna proteccion en dispositivo es absoluta: un atacante con el
  control del sistema puede parchear la biblioteca nativa, hookear
  funciones o volcar memoria. El objetivo es costo, no imposibilidad.
- La clave de cifrado existe en memoria del proceso mientras se usa; un
  atacante con root + ptrace puede extraerla.
- El subconjunto de traduccion dex2c/VMP es deliberadamente conservador;
   los metodos excluidos permanecen como bytecode dex ofuscado.
- Los chequeos de root/emulador son heuristicas y generan falsos
   positivos ocasionales; por eso son configurables
   (`--skip-root-check`, `--skip-emulator-check`).

## Politica de reaccion

La reaccion por defecto es terminacion inmediata del proceso
(`_exit(1)`) con registro en logcat. La implementacion esta aislada en
`hy_react` para permitir politicas alternativas en futuras versiones.

```

`docs/zh/architecture.md`:

```md
# Hyapk 架构

## 总览

Hyapk 通过作用于 zip 容器、dex 字节码以及按构建生成的原生运行时
的处理管线，把输入 APK 转换为受保护的 APK。

```
输入 APK
   |
   v
[1] 解包与分析（AXML manifest、dex 清单）
   |
   v
[2] dex 处理步骤：
    hardening -> bootstrap -> assets -> strings -> rename -> dex2c/vmp
   |
   v
[3] 写出最终 dex（不压缩存储）
   |
   v
[4] 组装加密 payload 并生成 C 代码
   |
   v
[5] 按 ABI 编译 libhyapkrt.so（NDK + CMake）
   |
   v
[6] 重新打包（16 KB 对齐、稳定条目顺序）
   |
   v
[7] v1+v2+v3 签名与最终校验
```

## 组件

### hyapk-cli（Kotlin）

- `apk/ZipIO.kt`：zip 读取与确定性的对齐写入器；原生库 16 KB
  对齐、resources.arsc 4 字节对齐、dex 不压缩存储以便运行时哈希。
- `apk/Axml.kt`：最小化二进制 AXML 解析器，读取包名、Application
  与启动 Activity，并在不改变文件大小的前提下翻转 manifest 布尔值。
- `dex/DexStore.kt`：通过 dexlib2 加载与保存全部 classes*.dex。
- `passes/`：处理管线与 payload 构建器。
- `ir/`：dex 到 Hyir 的翻译器，以及 C 与 HyVm 的发射器。

### hyapk-native（C）

- `crypto/`：SHA-256、HMAC、HKDF、ChaCha20-Poly1305 与 payload KDF。
- `vm/`：HyVm，47 个操作码的寄存器虚拟机，
  每次构建置换操作码表并加密程序。
- `protect/`：反调试、反 Frida、反 root 与模拟器启发式。
- `jni/`：payload 加载解密、面向 VM 与 dex2c 代码的 JNI 桥、
  签名校验、dex 哈希校验与守护线程。

## Payload 格式

payload 是嵌入原生库的 ChaCha20-Poly1305 加密块。密钥由保存在库内
的密钥（XOR 混淆）与 blob 头中按文件随机的盐共同派生：
只拿到 APK 或只拿到库都不足以还原密钥。

```
头（72 字节，明文）：
  magic 'HPAY' | 版本 | flags | 明文长度 | nonce(12) | 盐(32) | tag(16)
主体：加密的节
```

节包括：字符串(1)、asset 映射(2)、dex 哈希(3)、证书摘要(4)、
VMP 程序(5)、方法表(6)、类型(7)、字段(8) 与配置(9)。

## HyVm（VMP）

每个被转换的方法先翻译为 Hyir，再发射为 HyVm 程序：

```
头：magic 'HYVM' | 版本 | code_size | reg_count | seed
    nonce(12) | key_half(32) | tag(16) | opcode_map(64)
代码以 ChaCha20-Poly1305 加密（AAD = 前 16 字节）
```

解释器对每次寄存器、分支与操作数访问都做边界校验，
任何单字节篡改都会破坏 Poly1305 tag。

## Dex2C

同一个 Hyir 翻译器同时驱动 C 发射器。生成的函数通过精简的
`hy_d2c_*` API 复用与 VM 相同的 JNI 桥，编译进 libhyapkrt.so，
并以原始方法名和签名通过 RegisterNatives 注册。

## 启动注入

注入三个运行时类（`Sec`、`HyapkBridge`、`Assets`），并修改
Application 类（没有 Application 时修改启动 Activity）：

1. 在 `<clinit>` 中加载 libhyapkrt.so；
2. 在 `super.attachBaseContext` 之后立即调用
   `HyapkBridge.initNative(Context)`。

`initNative` 校验签名、检查 dex 哈希并启动守护线程。

## 翻译子集

dex2c/VMP 翻译器有意支持一个可审计的指令子集：常量、传送、
int/long/double 运算、比较、分支、调用（含 range）、基本类型字段
与数组、check-cast、instanceof、monitor、throw 与返回。包含
try/catch、float、对象数组、对象字段或罕见指令的方法会被排除并
报告。扩展子集只需修改 `ir/Hyir.kt` 与发射器。

## 已知限制

- 不翻译对象类型字段（基本类型可以）。
- 哈希校验覆盖不压缩存储的 dex；使用 `--compress-dex` 会关闭
  dex 完整性检查。
- 原生库的真实性依赖 APK 签名而非自哈希（后者存在循环依赖）。

```

`docs/zh/cli-reference.md`:

```md
# CLI 参考

```
hyapk <命令> [选项]

命令：
  protect <input.apk> -o <output.apk> [选项]
  verify <apk>
  doctor
  version
```

## protect

| 选项 | 默认值 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| `-o, --output` | （必填） | 输出 APK |
| `--passes` | hardening,bootstrap,strings,assets,rename,protect | 逗号分隔列表；可加 dex2c,vmp |
| `--dex2c` | （无） | 方法选择模式（可重复） |
| `--vmp` | （无） | 方法选择模式（可重复） |
| `--keep` | （无） | 重命名保留规则（可重复） |
| `--keystore` | （无） | 已有的 PKCS12/JKS keystore |
| `--keystore-pass` | （无） | keystore 密码 |
| `--key-alias` | hyapk | 密钥别名 |
| `--key-pass` | 同 keystore-pass | 密钥密码 |
| `--gen-keystore` | （无） | 未提供 keystore 时生成新的 |
| `--abi` | arm64-v8a,armeabi-v7a,x86_64,x86 | 构建的 ABI |
| `--ndk` | 环境变量 ANDROID_NDK_HOME | NDK 路径 |
| `--runtime-src` | 环境变量 HYAPK_RUNTIME_SRC | hyapk-native 源码 |
| `--min-api` | 21 | 最低支持 API（Android 5.0+） |
| `--skip-root-check` | 关 | 关闭运行时 root 启发式 |
| `--skip-emulator-check` | 关 | 关闭运行时模拟器启发式 |
| `--compress-dex` | 关 | 压缩 dex（关闭 dex 哈希检查） |
| `--workdir` | 临时目录 | 工作目录 |
| `--report-dir` | workdir/report | 报告目录 |
| `-v / -q` | 关 | 详细 / 安静 |

## 选择模式

- `com.example.Calc` 或 `com.example.Calc#*`：整类。
- `com.example.Calc#fib`：单个方法。
- `com.example.**`：整个包树。

## verify

校验签名（v1/v2/v3）与容器结构：存储条目、resources.arsc 的
4 字节对齐与原生库的 16 KB 对齐。失败时 exit 1。

## doctor

检查 Java、NDK 与原生运行时源码。

## 环境变量

- `ANDROID_NDK_HOME` / `ANDROID_NDK` / `ANDROID_HOME`：NDK 探测。
- `HYAPK_RUNTIME_SRC`：hyapk-native 源码位置。

## 退出码

- 0：成功。
- 1：错误（非法输入、构建失败、校验失败）。
- 2：用法错误。

```

`docs/zh/compatibility.md`:

```md
# 兼容性

## Android 版本

| 版本 | API | 状态 |
|---|---|---|
| Android 9 Pie | 28 | 支持 |
| Android 10 | 29 | 支持 |
| Android 11 | 30 | 支持 |
| Android 12/12L | 31/32 | 支持 |
| Android 13 | 33 | 支持 |
| Android 14 | 34 | 支持 |
| Android 15 | 35 | 支持（含 16 KB 页） |
| Android 16 | 36 | 支持 |

原生运行时以 `ANDROID_PLATFORM=android-21` 构建，仅使用公开 NDK
API，从 Android 5.0 到当前版本无需改动即可运行。

## 相关兼容细节

- Android 11+（API 30）：resources.arsc 不压缩存储并按 4 字节
  对齐，满足安装器要求。
- Android 15+ 16 KB 页：原生库不压缩存储并按 16 KB 对齐
  （`-Wl,-z,max-page-size=16384`）。
- Android 14（API 34）：不使用隐藏 API，也不额外加载动态 dex；
  被转换方法在原 dex 内替换并通过 RegisterNatives 绑定。
- 除 v1 与 v2 外支持 v3 签名（密钥轮换）。

## 支持的输入

- 有签名或无签名的 APK 输入；输出必定有签名。
- Multidex（classes.dex、classes2.dex 等）：全部槽位均处理保留。
- Assets：assets/ 下所有文件加密并重命名；
  `AssetManager.open(String)` 调用被重定向到桥。

## 已验证的构建环境

- Ubuntu，OpenJDK 17/21、Gradle 8.10、NDK r27、CMake 3.18+。
- GitHub Actions `ubuntu-latest`（`ci` 工作流）。

## 说明

- 通过 `openFd` 等非 `open(String)` 方法读取 assets 的应用，
  这些读取不会被重定向：请仅使用 `open`，或先调整代码。
- 类重命名保留 manifest 组件、含原生方法的类与常见库前缀；
  对反射访问的类请添加 `--keep` 规则。

```

`docs/zh/faq.md`:

```md
# 常见问题

## 保护后进程启动即退出

常见原因：

1. APK 在保护后被其他密钥重签。证书摘要在保护时取自签名密钥，
   两者必须一致。
2. 使用了 `--compress-dex` 且随后修改了 APK。
3. root 检查命中环境（userdebug 模拟器镜像）。
   测试时使用 `--skip-root-check --skip-emulator-check`。

## 某个类重命名后失效

很可能是通过反射访问（Class.forName、序列化、注入）。
添加 `--keep com.my.package.MyClass` 后重新保护。

## 某个方法没有被转换

翻译器支持一个字节码子集（见架构文档）。含 try/catch、float、
对象数组或对象字段的方法会被排除，原因见
`protection-report.txt`。

## 能保护带 Google 服务或 Firebase 的应用吗

可以。常见库前缀（androidx、kotlin、com/google、okhttp、
retrofit 等）默认保留。若对自己类使用手动反射，请添加
`--keep` 规则。

## 闭源警告是什么意思

本项目可能随时停止分发或转为商业产品，恕不另行通知。若在
Hyapk 上构建关键产品，请保留所用版本的副本并考虑支持开发。

## 如何贡献

欢迎贡献。请提交 issue 或 pull request。贡献即表示同意代码以
AGPL-3.0-only 分发。

## 有保修吗

没有。软件按原样提供，不附带任何形式的保修，
详见 AGPL-3.0 许可证。

```

`docs/zh/getting-started.md`:

```md
# Hyapk 快速上手

## 环境要求

- JDK 17 或更高版本。
- Android SDK（含 `platforms;android-35` 与 `build-tools;35.0.0`）。
- Android NDK r27 或更高版本（原生运行时所需）。
- Gradle 8.10 或更高版本。
- CMake 3.18+ 与 Ninja。

代码中没有写死任何路径：NDK 通过 `ANDROID_NDK_HOME`、`ANDROID_NDK`、
`ANDROID_HOME/ndk/*` 或 `--ndk` 定位；原生源码通过
`HYAPK_RUNTIME_SRC` 或 `--runtime-src` 定位。

## 构建

```sh
git clone <本仓库>
cd Hyapk
gradle :hyapk-cli:fatJar
```

产物为 `hyapk-cli/build/libs/hyapk-1.0.0-all.jar`，
包含全部依赖的自包含 jar。

## 检查环境

```sh
java -jar hyapk-1.0.0-all.jar doctor
```

## 保护一个 APK

```sh
java -jar hyapk-1.0.0-all.jar protect input.apk -o output.apk [选项]
```

默认执行 `hardening, bootstrap, strings, assets, rename, protect`
这些步骤；`dex2c` 与 `vmp` 步骤在通过 `--dex2c`、`--vmp`
选中方法后启用。

### 签名

未提供 keystore 时，Hyapk 会生成临时 RSA-4096 密钥并给出警告。
如需可复现的签名，请使用 `--keystore`，或在首次运行时用
`--gen-keystore` 生成持久 keystore 并复用。

### 方法选择

- `com.example.Calc` 或 `com.example.Calc#*`：该类的全部方法。
- `com.example.Calc#fib`：单个方法。
- `com.example.**`：整个包树。

使用了不支持指令（try/catch、float、对象数组、对象字段）的方法
会被跳过并在报告中说明原因。

## 校验受保护的 APK

```sh
java -jar hyapk-1.0.0-all.jar verify output.apk
```

校验 v1/v2/v3 签名以及容器结构（存储条目与原生库的 16 KB 对齐）。

## 运行测试

```sh
cd hyapk-native
cmake -S . -B build-host && cmake --build build-host && ctest --test-dir build-host

gradle :hyapk-cli:test
```

加密测试在 C 与 Kotlin 之间共享 KAT 向量，
保证两侧实现逐字节一致。

## 报告

每次运行都会在报告目录（`--report-dir`）写入
`protection-report.txt`，发生重命名时还会写入 `mapping.txt`。

```

`docs/zh/threat-model.md`:

```md
# 威胁模型

## 目标

显著提高对 Android 应用进行逆向、欺诈性重打包、动态插桩与运行时
篡改的成本，同时保持 Android 9 至 16 的兼容性。

## 威胁行为体

| 行为体 | 能力 | 主要缓解 |
|---|---|---|
| 静态逆向者 | jadx、apktool、ghidra | 重命名、字符串加密、assets 加密、dex2c 与 VMP 将逻辑移出 dex |
| 重打包者 | 重建/重打包/重签名 | 针对嵌入摘要的签名校验、dex 哈希、硬退出 |
| 动态分析者 | frida、xposed、调试器 | 反调试、反 Frida、守护线程、逻辑移出 dex |
| 恶意设备 | root、magisk、模拟器 | 反 root、模拟器启发式、可配置策略 |

## 各组件的保护作用

1. 签名校验：签名证书的 SHA-256 摘要以加密形式存于 payload。
   使用其他密钥重签名的 APK 摘要不同，进程在 `initNative` 中终止。
2. dex 防篡改：构建时对每个不压缩的 classes*.dex 计算哈希；
   运行时启动阶段重新哈希，不一致即终止进程。
3. 反调试：读取 TracerPid 与探测性 PTRACE_TRACEME，均周期执行。
4. 反 Frida：/proc/self/maps 痕迹、代理线程名
   （gum-js-loop、gmain、pool-frida）、默认端口探测与环境变量。
5. 反 root：已知二进制与路径（su、magisk）、挂载点、test-keys
   属性、Zygisk/Riru 痕迹。
6. VMP/dex2c：被选中的逻辑不再以可读字节码存在，
   而是以加密的 HyVm 程序或编译后的原生 C 存在。
7. 字符串与 asset 加密：字面量与资源在运行时从加密 payload 解析。

## 残余风险（诚实说明）

- 设备侧不存在绝对的保护：控制系统环境的攻击者可以修补原生库、
  钩住函数或转储内存。目标是提高成本，而非使其不可能。
- 加密密钥在使用期间存在于进程内存；具备 root 与 ptrace 的
  攻击者可以提取。
- dex2c/VMP 的翻译子集刻意保守；被排除的方法仍以混淆后的
  dex 字节码存在。
- root/模拟器检查属于启发式，偶尔误报，因此均可配置
  （`--skip-root-check`、`--skip-emulator-check`）。

## 响应策略

默认响应是立即终止进程（`_exit(1)`）并记录 logcat。
实现集中在 `hy_react`，便于未来支持其他策略。

```

`gradle.properties`:

```properties
org.gradle.jvmargs=-Xmx2g -Dfile.encoding=UTF-8
org.gradle.parallel=true
org.gradle.caching=true
kotlin.code.style=official

```

`hyapk-cli/build.gradle.kts`:

```kts
plugins {
    kotlin("jvm")
}

repositories {
    mavenCentral()
    google()
}

dependencies {
    implementation("org.smali:dexlib2:2.5.2")
    implementation("com.android.tools.build:apksig:8.7.3")
    implementation("org.bouncycastle:bcpkix-jdk18on:1.78.1")
    implementation("org.json:json:20240303")
    testImplementation("junit:junit:4.13.2")
}

kotlin {
    compilerOptions {
        jvmTarget.set(org.jetbrains.kotlin.gradle.dsl.JvmTarget.JVM_17)
    }
}

tasks.withType<JavaCompile> {
    sourceCompatibility = "17"
    targetCompatibility = "17"
}

tasks.test {
    useJUnit()
    testLogging {
        events("passed", "failed")
    }
}

val fatJar = tasks.register<Jar>("fatJar") {
    archiveBaseName.set("hyapk")
    archiveClassifier.set("all")
    duplicatesStrategy = DuplicatesStrategy.EXCLUDE
    manifest {
        attributes["Main-Class"] = "com.blcyber.hyapk.MainKt"
        attributes["Implementation-Version"] = project.version
    }
    from(sourceSets.main.get().output)
    dependsOn(configurations.runtimeClasspath)
    from({
        configurations.runtimeClasspath.get().filter { it.name.endsWith("jar") }.map { zipTree(it) }
    }) {
        exclude(
            "META-INF/*.SF", "META-INF/*.DSA", "META-INF/*.RSA",
            "module-info.class", "META-INF/versions/**/module-info.class",
            "META-INF/INDEX.LIST", "META-INF/DEPENDENCIES", "META-INF/LICENSE*", "META-INF/NOTICE*"
        )
    }
}

`hyapk-cli/src/main/kotlin/com/blcyber/hyapk/Log.kt`:

```kt
package com.blcyber.hyapk

import java.io.File

/** Minimal leveled logger. No emojis, no hardcoded paths. */
object Log {
    var verbose = false
    var quiet = false

    private fun line(level: String, msg: String) {
        if (quiet) return
        println("[hyapk][$level] $msg")
    }

    fun info(msg: String) = line("I", msg)
    fun warn(msg: String) = line("W", msg)
    fun error(msg: String) = line("E", msg)

    fun debug(msg: String) {
        if (verbose) line("D", msg)
    }

    fun die(msg: String, code: Int = 1): Nothing {
        error(msg)
        kotlin.system.exitProcess(code)
    }
}

const val VERSION = "1.0.0"
const val PROJECT = "Hyapk"
const val NOTICE =
    "NOTICE: $PROJECT may stop being distributed, be shut down, or become a " +
    "closed-source and/or commercial product at any time without prior notice."

fun banner() {
    if (Log.quiet) return
    println("$PROJECT $VERSION (AGPL-3.0-only) - Android application and game protection suite")
    println("Developers: beto2-dev (lead, BL Cyber) and ChapzoMods")
    println(NOTICE)
}

```

`hyapk-cli/src/main/kotlin/com/blcyber/hyapk/Main.kt`:

```kt
package com.blcyber.hyapk

import com.blcyber.hyapk.apk.AlignedZipWriter
import com.blcyber.hyapk.apk.ApkInfo
import com.blcyber.hyapk.apk.ZipSource
import com.blcyber.hyapk.apk.verifyStructure
import com.blcyber.hyapk.crypto.DerivedKeys
import com.blcyber.hyapk.dex.DexStore
import com.blcyber.hyapk.passes.PipelineContext
import com.blcyber.hyapk.passes.runPipeline
import com.blcyber.hyapk.signing.Signer
import java.io.File
import java.security.SecureRandom

fun main(args: Array<String>) {
    if (args.isEmpty()) {
        usage()
        kotlin.system.exitProcess(2)
    }
    when (args[0]) {
        "version" -> {
            banner()
            println("version: $VERSION")
        }
        "doctor" -> doctor()
        "protect" -> protect(args.drop(1))
        "verify" -> verify(args.drop(1))
        "help", "--help", "-h" -> usage()
        else -> {
            println("unknown command: ${args[0]}")
            usage()
            kotlin.system.exitProcess(2)
        }
    }
}

private fun usage() {
    banner()
    println(
        """
        Usage: hyapk <command> [options]

        Commands:
          protect <input.apk> -o <output.apk> [options]
              Apply the protection pipeline to an APK.

          verify <input.apk>
              Verify signatures and container alignment of a protected APK.

          doctor
              Check the local environment (Java, NDK, runtime sources).

          version
              Print version information.

        Protect options:
          -o, --output <file>          output APK path (required)
          --passes <a,b,c>             default: hardening,bootstrap,strings,assets,rename,protect
                                        extra: dex2c,vmp (need --dex2c/--vmp patterns)
          --dex2c <pattern>            select methods for dex2c (repeatable)
                                        pattern: com.example.Calc  |  com.example.Calc#*  |  com.example.**
          --vmp <pattern>              select methods for VMP (repeatable)
          --keep <pattern>             rename keep rules (repeatable)
          --keystore <file>            existing keystore (PKCS12/JKS)
          --keystore-pass <s>          keystore password
          --key-alias <s>              key alias (default: hyapk)
          --key-pass <s>               key password (default: keystore password)
          --gen-keystore <file>        generate a new RSA-4096 keystore if none given
          --abi <list>                 default: arm64-v8a,armeabi-v7a,x86_64,x86
          --ndk <path>                 NDK path (or env ANDROID_NDK_HOME)
          --runtime-src <path>         hyapk-native sources (or env HYAPK_RUNTIME_SRC)
          --min-api <n>                 minimum supported Android API (default 21 = Android 5.0)
          --skip-root-check            disable root heuristics at runtime (emulator testing)
          --skip-emulator-check         disable emulator heuristics at runtime
          --compress-dex                store dex compressed (tamper check then unavailable)
          --workdir <dir>               temporary working directory
          --report-dir <dir>            where to write mapping/report files
          -v / -q                      verbose / quiet

        Environment:
          ANDROID_NDK_HOME             default NDK location
          HYAPK_RUNTIME_SRC            default hyapk-native sources location
        """.trimIndent()
    )
}

private fun doctor() {
    banner()
    val rt = System.getProperty("java.version")
    println("java runtime: $rt")
    val ndk = findNdk(null)
    if (ndk != null) {
        println("ndk: $ndk")
    } else {
        println("ndk: NOT FOUND (pass --ndk or set ANDROID_NDK_HOME; required for protect)")
    }
    val src = findRuntimeSrc(null)
    if (src != null) {
        println("runtime sources: $src")
    } else {
        println("runtime sources: NOT FOUND (pass --runtime-src or set HYAPK_RUNTIME_SRC)")
    }
    println("opcodes: ${com.blcyber.hyapk.ir.VmOp.values().size} VM opcodes registered")
}

fun findNdk(explicit: String?): File? {
    explicit?.let { return File(it).takeIf { it.isDirectory } }
    System.getenv("ANDROID_NDK_HOME")?.let { return File(it).takeIf { it.isDirectory } }
    System.getenv("ANDROID_NDK")?.let { return File(it).takeIf { it.isDirectory } }
    System.getenv("ANDROID_HOME")?.let { home ->
        val ndkDir = File(home, "ndk")
        if (ndkDir.isDirectory) {
            return ndkDir.listFiles()?.filter { it.isDirectory }?.maxByOrNull { it.name }
        }
    }
    return null
}

fun findRuntimeSrc(explicit: String?): File? {
    explicit?.let { return File(it).takeIf { File(it, "CMakeLists.txt").isFile } }
    System.getenv("HYAPK_RUNTIME_SRC")?.let { return File(it).takeIf { File(it, "CMakeLists.txt").isFile } }
    // relative to the jar location: ../hyapk-native
    val jar = File(ProtectOptions::class.java.protectionDomain.codeSource.location.toURI())
    val candidates = listOf(
        File(jar.parentFile, "hyapk-native"),
        File(jar.parentFile?.parentFile, "hyapk-native"),
        File(jar.parentFile?.parentFile?.parentFile, "hyapk-native"),
        File("hyapk-native")
    )
    return candidates.firstOrNull { File(it, "CMakeLists.txt").isFile }
}

private fun protect(args: List<String>) {
    banner()
    val opts = ProtectOptions.parse(args)
    val input = opts.input!!
    val output = opts.output!!
    if (!input.isFile) Log.die("input APK not found: $input")

    val work = opts.workdir ?: File(System.getProperty("java.io.tmpdir"), "hyapk-${System.currentTimeMillis()}")
    work.mkdirs()
    Log.info("workdir: $work")

    val source = ZipSource.open(input)
    val apkInfo = ApkInfo.read(source)
    Log.info("package: ${apkInfo.packageName ?: "?"}")

    val dexStore = DexStore.load(source, opts.minApi)

    val ctx = PipelineContext(
        opts = opts,
        source = source,
        apkInfo = apkInfo,
        dexStore = dexStore,
        random = java.security.SecureRandom(),
        keys = DerivedKeys.generate(java.security.SecureRandom())
    )
    if (opts.workdir == null) ctx.opts.workdir = work

    runPipeline(ctx)

    // rebuild the zip container
    val unsigned = File(work, "unsigned.apk")
    AlignedZipWriter.write(unsigned, source)
    source.close()

    // signing (reuses the material prepared by the native pass, or prepares one)
    val signer = ctx.signingMaterial ?: com.blcyber.hyapk.signing.Signer.prepare(opts)
    Signer.sign(signer, unsigned, output)
    unsigned.delete()

    Log.info("protected APK: $output")
    ctx.report.writeTo(opts.reportDir ?: File(work, "report"))
}

private fun verify(args: List<String>) {
    banner()
    if (args.isEmpty()) Log.die("usage: hyapk verify <apk>")
    val f = File(args[0])
    if (!f.isFile) Log.die("APK not found: $f")
    var ok = true
    if (!com.blcyber.hyapk.signing.Signer.verifySignatures(f)) {
        Log.error("signature verification FAILED")
        ok = false
    } else {
        Log.info("signatures: OK (v1/v2/v3)")
    }
    val problems = verifyStructure(f)
    if (problems.isEmpty()) {
        Log.info("container: OK (alignment and stored entries)")
    } else {
        problems.forEach { Log.warn(it) }
        if (problems.any { it.startsWith("ERROR") }) ok = false
    }
    kotlin.system.exitProcess(if (ok) 0 else 1)
}

private fun createTempDirectory(prefix: String): File {
    val dir = File.createTempFile(prefix, "").let {
        it.delete()
        it
    }
    dir.mkdirs()
    return dir
}

`hyapk-cli/src/main/kotlin/com/blcyber/hyapk/Options.kt`:

```kt
package com.blcyber.hyapk

import java.io.File

/**
 * Command line options for the protect verb.
 * All paths are user supplied; nothing is hardcoded.
 */
class ProtectOptions {
    var input: File? = null
    var output: File? = null
    var workdir: File? = null
    var reportDir: File? = null
    var keystore: File? = null
    var keystorePass: String? = null
    var keyAlias: String? = null
    var keyPass: String? = null
    var genKeystore: File? = null
    val passes = mutableListOf(
        "hardening", "bootstrap", "strings", "assets", "rename", "protect"
    )
    var dex2cPatterns = mutableListOf<String>()
    var vmpPatterns = mutableListOf<String>()
    var keepPatterns = mutableListOf<String>()
    var abis = mutableListOf("arm64-v8a", "armeabi-v7a", "x86_64", "x86")
    var ndkPath: String? = null
    var runtimeSrc: String? = null
    var minApi: Int = 21
    var skipRootCheck = false
    var skipEmulatorCheck = false
    var noStrings: Boolean get() = !passes.contains("strings")
        set(_) {}
    var compressDex = false

    fun hasPass(name: String) = passes.contains(name)

    companion object {
        val PASS_NAMES = setOf(
            "hardening", "bootstrap", "strings", "assets", "rename",
            "protect", "dex2c", "vmp"
        )

        fun parse(args: List<String>): ProtectOptions {
            val o = ProtectOptions()
            var i = 0
            while (i < args.size) {
                val a = args[i]
                fun value(): String {
                    if (i + 1 >= args.size) Log.die("missing value for $a")
                    i++
                    return args[i]
                }
                when (a) {
                    "-o", "--output" -> o.output = File(value())
                    "--workdir" -> o.workdir = File(value())
                    "--report-dir" -> o.reportDir = File(value())
                    "--keystore" -> o.keystore = File(value())
                    "--keystore-pass" -> o.keystorePass = value()
                    "--key-alias" -> o.keyAlias = value()
                    "--key-pass" -> o.keyPass = value()
                    "--gen-keystore" -> o.genKeystore = File(value())
                    "--passes" -> {
                        o.passes.clear()
                        value().split(',').forEach { p ->
                            val t = p.trim()
                            if (t.isNotEmpty()) o.passes.add(t)
                        }
                    }
                    "--dex2c" -> o.dex2cPatterns.add(value())
                    "--vmp" -> o.vmpPatterns.add(value())
                    "--keep" -> o.keepPatterns.add(value())
                    "--abi" -> {
                        o.abis.clear()
                        value().split(',').filter { it.isNotBlank() }.forEach { o.abis.add(it.trim()) }
                    }
                    "--ndk" -> o.ndkPath = value()
                    "--runtime-src" -> o.runtimeSrc = value()
                    "--min-api" -> o.minApi = value().toIntOrNull() ?: Log.die("--min-api must be an integer")
                    "--skip-root-check" -> o.skipRootCheck = true
                    "--skip-emulator-check" -> o.skipEmulatorCheck = true
                    "--compress-dex" -> o.compressDex = true
                    "-v", "--verbose" -> Log.verbose = true
                    "-q", "--quiet" -> Log.quiet = true
                    else -> {
                        if (o.input == null && !a.startsWith("-")) o.input = File(a)
                        else Log.die("unknown option: $a")
                    }
                }
                i++
            }
            for (p in o.passes) {
                if (p !in PASS_NAMES) Log.die("unknown pass: $p (valid: ${PASS_NAMES.sorted()})")
            }
            if (o.input == null) Log.die("no input APK given")
            if (o.output == null) Log.die("no output path given (-o)")
            return o
        }
    }
}

`hyapk-cli/src/main/kotlin/com/blcyber/hyapk/apk/Axml.kt`:

```kt
package com.blcyber.hyapk.apk

import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream

/**
 * Minimal binary XML (AXML) reader/patcher for AndroidManifest.xml.
 * Supports: element/attribute walking, boolean attribute flipping in place
 * (no size change), and extraction of package/application/launcher info.
 */
object Axml {

    private const val RES_STRING_POOL_TYPE = 0x0001
    private const val RES_XML_START_ELEMENT_TYPE = 0x0102
    private const val RES_XML_END_ELEMENT_TYPE = 0x0103
    private const val RES_START_NAMESPACE = 0x0100
    private const val RES_END_NAMESPACE = 0x0101

    class Ctx(
        val data: ByteArray
    ) {
        var strings: List<String> = emptyList()
    }

    class Attr(val name: String, val ns: String?, val rawValue: String?, val dataType: Int, val data: Int)

    class Node(val name: String, val attrs: List<Attr>, val lineNumber: Int)

    /** Parsed document: start and end events, nesting tracked via depth. */
    class Document(val events: List<Event>) {
        class Event(val depth: Int, val name: String, val node: Node?, val isEnd: Boolean)
    }

    fun parse(data: ByteArray): Document {
        val events = mutableListOf<Document.Event>()
        val strings = mutableListOf<String>()
        var p = 0
        var depth = 0

        fun u16(off: Int) = (data[off].toInt() and 0xff) or ((data[off + 1].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8)
        fun u32(off: Int): Int =
            (data[off].toInt() and 0xff) or
                ((data[off + 1].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or
                ((data[off + 2].toInt() and 0xff) shl 16) or
                ((data[off + 3].toInt() and 0xff) shl 24)

        if (data.size < 8 || u16(0) != 0x0003) {
            throw IllegalArgumentException("not a binary XML document")
        }

        while (p + 8 <= data.size) {
            val type = u16(p)
            val headerSize = u16(p + 2)
            val size = u32(p + 4).toInt()
            if (size < 8 || p + size > data.size) break

            when (type) {
                RES_STRING_POOL_TYPE -> {
                    val count = u32(p + 8).toInt()
                    val flags = u32(p + 16).toInt()
                    val stringsStart = u32(p + 20).toInt()
                    val isUtf8 = (flags and (1 shl 8)) != 0
                    strings.clear()
                    for (i in 0 until count) {
                        val strOff = u32(p + 28 + i * 4).toInt()
                        val sp = p + stringsStart + strOff
                        if (sp < 0 || sp >= data.size) {
                            strings.add("")
                            continue
                        }
                        strings.add(readString(data, sp, isUtf8))
                    }
                }
                RES_XML_START_ELEMENT_TYPE -> {
                    val nameIdx = u32(p + 20)
                    val attrStart = u16(p + 24)  // offset from the attrExt struct
                    val attrCount = u16(p + 28)
                    val attrs = mutableListOf<Attr>()
                    val attrBase = p + 16 + attrStart
                    for (i in 0 until attrCount) {
                        val ap = attrBase + i * 20
                        if (ap + 20 > data.size) break
                        val nsIdx = u32(ap).toInt()
                        val nameI = u32(ap + 4).toInt()
                        val rawIdx = u32(ap + 8).toInt()
                        val vType = data[ap + 15].toInt() and 0xff
                        val vData = (data[ap + 16].toInt() and 0xff) or
                            ((data[ap + 17].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or
                            ((data[ap + 18].toInt() and 0xff) shl 16) or
                            ((data[ap + 19].toInt() and 0xff) shl 24)
                        attrs.add(
                            Attr(
                                name = strings.getOrNull(nameI) ?: "?",
                                ns = strings.getOrNull(nsIdx),
                                rawValue = strings.getOrNull(rawIdx),
                                dataType = vType,
                                data = vData
                            )
                        )
                    }
                    val node = Node(strings.getOrNull(nameIdx) ?: "?", attrs, u32(p + 8).toInt())
                    events.add(Document.Event(depth, node.name, node, false))
                    depth++
                }
                RES_XML_END_ELEMENT_TYPE -> {
                    val nameIdx = u32(p + 20)
                    depth--
                    events.add(Document.Event(depth, strings.getOrNull(nameIdx) ?: "?", null, true))
                }
                else -> {}
            }
            p += size
        }
        return Document(events)
    }

    private fun readString(d: ByteArray, p: Int, utf8: Boolean): String {
        if (utf8) {
            var off = p
            val u16len = d[off].toInt() and 0xff
            off++
            if (u16len and 0x80 != 0) off++
            var blen = d[off].toInt() and 0xff
            off++
            if (blen and 0x80 != 0) {
                blen = ((blen and 0x7f) shl 8) or (d[off].toInt() and 0xff)
                off++
            }
            return String(d, off, blen, Charsets.UTF_8)
        } else {
            var off = p
            var len = d[off].toInt() and 0xff or ((d[off + 1].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8)
            off += 2
            if (len and 0x8000 != 0) {
                len = ((len and 0x7fff) shl 16) or
                    (d[off].toInt() and 0xff) or ((d[off + 1].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8)
                off += 2
            }
            return String(d, off, len * 2, Charsets.UTF_16LE)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Flips a boolean attribute to the given value in place.
     * Only touches the 4-byte data field; document size is unchanged.
     */
    fun flipBoolean(manifest: ByteArray, element: String, attrName: String, value: Boolean): Boolean {
        val data = manifest
        val doc = parse(data)
        // Find the string index for the attribute name within the string pool
        val attrIdx = findStringIndex(data, attrName) ?: return false
        val elementIdx = findStringIndex(data, element)

        var p = 0
        fun u16(off: Int) = (data[off].toInt() and 0xff) or ((data[off + 1].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8)
        fun u32(off: Int): Int =
            (data[off].toInt() and 0xff) or
                ((data[off + 1].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or
                ((data[off + 2].toInt() and 0xff) shl 16) or
                ((data[off + 3].toInt() and 0xff) shl 24)

        while (p + 8 <= data.size) {
            val type = u16(p)
            val size = u32(p + 4).toInt()
            if (size < 8 || p + size > data.size) break
            if (type == RES_XML_START_ELEMENT_TYPE) {
                val nameIdx = u32(p + 20).toInt()
                val attrStart = u16(p + 24)
                val attrCount = u16(p + 28)
                val attrBase = p + 16 + attrStart
                if (elementIdx == null || nameIdx == elementIdx) {
                    for (i in 0 until attrCount) {
                        val ap = attrBase + i * 20
                        if (ap + 20 > data.size) break
                        val nameI = u32(ap + 4).toInt()
                        if (nameI == attrIdx) {
                            // TYPE_INT_BOOLEAN = 0x12
                            if (data[ap + 15].toInt() and 0xff == 0x12) {
                                val newV = if (value) -1 else 0
                                data[ap + 16] = (newV and 0xff).toByte()
                                data[ap + 17] = ((newV shr 8) and 0xff).toByte()
                                data[ap + 18] = ((newV shr 16) and 0xff).toByte()
                                data[ap + 19] = ((newV shr 24) and 0xff).toByte()
                                return true
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            p += size
        }
        return false
    }

    private fun findStringIndex(data: ByteArray, s: String): Int? {
        var p = 0
        fun u16(off: Int) = (data[off].toInt() and 0xff) or ((data[off + 1].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8)
        fun u32(off: Int): Int =
            (data[off].toInt() and 0xff) or
                ((data[off + 1].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or
                ((data[off + 2].toInt() and 0xff) shl 16) or
                ((data[off + 3].toInt() and 0xff) shl 24)
        while (p + 28 <= data.size) {
            val type = u16(p)
            val size = u32(p + 4).toInt()
            if (size < 8 || p + size > data.size) break
            if (type == RES_STRING_POOL_TYPE) {
                val count = u32(p + 8).toInt()
                val flags = u32(p + 16).toInt()
                val stringsStart = u32(p + 20).toInt()
                val isUtf8 = (flags and (1 shl 8)) != 0
                for (i in 0 until count) {
                    val strOff = u32(p + 28 + i * 4).toInt()
                    val sp = p + stringsStart + strOff
                    if (sp in 0 until data.size && readString(data, sp, isUtf8) == s) return i
                }
                return null
            }
            p += size
        }
        return null
    }
}

/** Manifest-level information needed by the pipeline. */
class ApkInfo private constructor(
    val packageName: String?,
    val applicationClass: String?, // type descriptor or null
    val launcherActivities: List<String>, // type descriptors
    val hasDebuggable: Boolean,
    val hasAllowBackup: Boolean
) {
    companion object {
        fun read(source: ZipSource): ApkInfo {
            val manifest = source["AndroidManifest.xml"]?.data
                ?: throw IllegalArgumentException("AndroidManifest.xml missing")
            val doc = Axml.parse(manifest)
            var pkg: String? = null
            var app: String? = null
            val launchers = mutableListOf<String>()
            var debuggable = false
            var allowBackup = false

            var currentActivity: String? = null
            var filterDepth = -1
            var filterHasMain = false
            var filterHasLauncher = false

            for (ev in doc.events) {
                if (ev.isEnd) {
                    if (filterDepth >= 0 && ev.depth < filterDepth) {
                        // filter closed: evaluate
                        if (currentActivity != null && filterHasMain && filterHasLauncher) {
                            launchers.add(toDescriptor(currentActivity))
                        }
                        filterDepth = -1
                        filterHasMain = false
                        filterHasLauncher = false
                    }
                    continue
                }
                val node = ev.node!!
                when (node.name) {
                    "manifest" -> pkg = node.attrs.firstOrNull { it.name == "package" }?.rawValue
                    "application" -> {
                        app = node.attrs
                            .firstOrNull { it.name == "name" && it.ns?.contains("res/android") == true }
                            ?.rawValue
                        debuggable = node.attrs
                            .firstOrNull { it.name == "debuggable" }?.data != 0
                        allowBackup = node.attrs
                            .firstOrNull { it.name == "allowBackup" }?.data != 0
                    }
                    "activity", "activity-alias" -> {
                        currentActivity = node.attrs
                            .firstOrNull { it.name == "name" && it.ns?.contains("res/android") == true }
                            ?.rawValue
                    }
                    "intent-filter" -> filterDepth = ev.depth + 1
                    "action", "category" -> if (filterDepth >= 0 && ev.depth >= filterDepth) {
                        val a = node.attrs.firstOrNull { it.name == "name" }?.rawValue
                        if (node.name == "action" && a == "android.intent.action.MAIN") filterHasMain = true
                        if (node.name == "category" && a == "android.intent.category.LAUNCHER") filterHasLauncher = true
                    }
                }
            }

            return ApkInfo(
                packageName = pkg,
                applicationClass = app?.let { toDescriptor(it) },
                launcherActivities = launchers,
                hasDebuggable = debuggable,
                hasAllowBackup = allowBackup
            )
        }

        fun toDescriptor(className: String): String {
            val c = className.removePrefix(".").replace('.', '/')
            return if (c.startsWith("L")) c else "L$c;"
        }
    }
}

`hyapk-cli/src/main/kotlin/com/blcyber/hyapk/apk/ZipIO.kt`:

```kt
package com.blcyber.hyapk.apk

import java.io.BufferedInputStream
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream
import java.io.Closeable
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileInputStream
import java.io.FileOutputStream
import java.io.InputStream
import java.io.OutputStream
import java.util.zip.CRC32
import java.util.zip.Deflater
import java.util.zip.ZipEntry
import java.util.zip.ZipInputStream

/**
 * Read/write model over an APK zip container.
 * ZipSource keeps the original entries in memory (smaller than the pipeline
 * budget for protected apps) and lets passes replace, add or delete entries.
 */
class ZipSource private constructor(
    val entries: LinkedHashMap<String, Entry>
) : Closeable {

    /** Entry payload and metadata. */
    data class Entry(
        val name: String,
        var data: ByteArray,
        var method: Int, // ZipEntry.STORED or DEFLATED
        var align: Int = 4
    )

    companion object {
        fun open(file: File): ZipSource {
            ZipInputStream(BufferedInputStream(FileInputStream(file), 1 shl 16)).use { zin ->
                val map = LinkedHashMap<String, Entry>()
                var e: ZipEntry? = zin.nextEntry
                while (e != null) {
                    if (!e.isDirectory) {
                        val data = zin.readBytes()
                        val name = e.name
                        // ZipInputStream may report unknown compression for some entries;
                        // we always re-encode on write, so normalize to DEFLATED/STORED here.
                        val method = if (e.method == ZipEntry.STORED) ZipEntry.STORED else ZipEntry.DEFLATED
                        map[name] = Entry(name, data, method)
                    }
                    e = zin.nextEntry
                }
                return ZipSource(map)
            }
        }
    }

    fun names(): Set<String> = entries.keys.toSet()

    operator fun get(name: String): Entry? = entries[name]

    fun replace(entry: Entry) {
        entries[entry.name] = entry
    }

    fun put(name: String, data: ByteArray, method: Int, align: Int = 4) {
        entries[name] = Entry(name, data, method, align)
    }

    fun remove(name: String): Entry? = entries.remove(name)

    fun removeIf(pred: (String) -> Boolean): List<String> {
        val removed = mutableListOf<String>()
        val it = entries.entries.iterator()
        while (it.hasNext()) {
            val n = it.next().key
            if (pred(n)) {
                it.remove()
                removed.add(n)
            }
        }
        return removed
    }

    override fun close() {
        // in-memory; nothing to release
    }
}

/**
 * Deterministic zip writer with alignment support for stored entries.
 * - AndroidManifest.xml first
 * - resources.arsc stored and 4-byte aligned (required on API 30+)
 * - native libraries stored and 16KB aligned (Android 15+ page size)
 * - everything else deflated or stored per entry metadata
 */
object AlignedZipWriter {

    fun write(out: File, source: ZipSource, ctx: Any? = null) {
        val order = entryOrder(source)
        FileOutputStream(out).use { fos ->
            writeStream(fos) { w ->
                for (name in order) {
                    val e = source[name] ?: continue
                    var align = e.align
                    var method = e.method
                    if (name == "resources.arsc") {
                        method = ZipEntry.STORED
                        align = 4
                    } else if (name.startsWith("lib/") && name.endsWith(".so")) {
                        method = ZipEntry.STORED
                        align = 16384
                    } else if (name.endsWith(".dex")) {
                        align = 4
                    }
                    w.writeEntry(name, e.data, method, align)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun entryOrder(source: ZipSource): List<String> {
        val manifest = mutableListOf<String>()
        val dex = mutableListOf<String>()
        val libs = mutableListOf<String>()
        val arsc = mutableListOf<String>()
        val assets = mutableListOf<String>()
        val res = mutableListOf<String>()
        val other = mutableListOf<String>()
        for (n in source.names()) {
            when {
                n == "AndroidManifest.xml" -> manifest.add(n)
                n.endsWith(".dex") -> dex.add(n)
                n.startsWith("lib/") -> libs.add(n)
                n == "resources.arsc" -> arsc.add(n)
                n.startsWith("assets/") -> assets.add(n)
                n.startsWith("res/") -> res.add(n)
                else -> other.add(n)
            }
        }
        fun sortedCase(l: MutableList<String>) = l.sorted()
        return manifest + dex.sorted() + libs.sorted() + arsc + assets.sorted() + res.sorted() + other.sorted()
    }

    interface EntryWriter {
        fun writeEntry(name: String, data: ByteArray, method: Int, align: Int)
    }

    fun writeStream(out: OutputStream, body: (EntryWriter) -> Unit) {
        val records = mutableListOf<Record>()
        val cdRecords = mutableListOf<ByteArray>()
        val cdNames = mutableListOf<ByteArray>()

        class W : EntryWriter {
            override fun writeEntry(name: String, data: ByteArray, method: Int, align: Int) {
                val crc = CRC32().apply { update(data) }
                records.add(Record(name, data, method, align, crc.value))
            }
        }
        body(W())

        var offset = 0L

        for (r in records) {
            val nameBytes = r.name.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)
            val utfFlag = if (r.name.any { it.code > 0x7f }) 1 shl 11 else 0

            val payload: ByteArray
            val methodField: Int
            if (r.method == ZipEntry.STORED) {
                payload = r.data
                methodField = 0
            } else {
                payload = deflate(r.data)
                methodField = 8
            }

            var extraLen = 0
            if (methodField == 0 && r.align > 1) {
                val headerEnd = offset + 30L + nameBytes.size
                var pad = (r.align - (headerEnd % r.align)).toInt()
                if (pad == r.align) pad = 0
                extraLen = pad
            }

            val dataStart = offset + 30L + nameBytes.size + extraLen
            val aligned = methodField != 0 ||
                r.align <= 1 || (dataStart % r.align == 0L)
            require(aligned) { "alignment computation failed for ${r.name}" }

            // local file header
            val lh = ByteArrayOutputStream()
            le32(lh, 0x04034b50)
            le16(lh, 20) // version needed
            le16(lh, utfFlag) // general purpose flags
            le16(lh, methodField)
            le16(lh, 0) // time (fixed for reproducibility)
            le16(lh, 0x21) // date 1980-01-01
            le32(lh, r.crc)
            le32(lh, payload.size)
            le32(lh, r.data.size)
            le16(lh, nameBytes.size)
            le16(lh, extraLen)
            out.write(lh.toByteArray())
            out.write(nameBytes)
            if (extraLen > 0) {
                out.write(ByteArray(extraLen))
            }
            out.write(payload)

            // central directory entry
            val ce = ByteArrayOutputStream()
            le32(ce, 0x02014b50)
            le16(ce, 0x0014) // version made by
            le16(ce, 20)     // version needed
            le16(ce, utfFlag)
            le16(ce, methodField)
            le16(ce, 0)
            le16(ce, 0x21)
            le32(ce, r.crc)
            le32(ce, payload.size)
            le32(ce, r.data.size)
            le16(ce, nameBytes.size)
            le16(ce, 0)
            le16(ce, 0)
            le16(ce, 0)
            le16(ce, 0)
            le32(ce, 0)
            le32(ce, offset.toInt())
            cdRecords.add(ce.toByteArray())
            cdNames.add(nameBytes)

            offset = dataStart + payload.size
        }

        val cdOffset = offset
        for (i in records.indices) {
            out.write(cdRecords[i])
            out.write(cdNames[i])
        }
        val cdSize = cdRecords.sumOf { it.size } + cdNames.sumOf { it.size }

        val eocd = ByteArrayOutputStream()
        le32(eocd, 0x06054b50)
        le16(eocd, 0)
        le16(eocd, 0)
        le16(eocd, records.size)
        le16(eocd, records.size)
        le32(eocd, cdSize)
        le32(eocd, cdOffset.toInt())
        le16(eocd, 0)
        out.write(eocd.toByteArray())
    }

    private fun deflate(data: ByteArray): ByteArray {
        val def = Deflater(Deflater.BEST_COMPRESSION, true)
        def.setInput(data)
        def.finish()
        val bos = ByteArrayOutputStream()
        val buf = ByteArray(1 shl 14)
        while (!def.finished()) {
            val n = def.deflate(buf)
