```kt
            if (n > 0) bos.write(buf, 0, n)
        }
        def.end()
        return bos.toByteArray()
    }

    private data class Record(
        val name: String,
        val data: ByteArray,
        val method: Int,
        val align: Int,
        val crc: Long
    )

    private fun le16(o: ByteArrayOutputStream, v: Int) {
        o.write(v and 0xff)
        o.write((v shr 8) and 0xff)
    }

    private fun le32(o: ByteArrayOutputStream, v: Long) {
        o.write((v and 0xff).toInt())
        o.write(((v shr 8) and 0xff).toInt())
        o.write(((v shr 16) and 0xff).toInt())
        o.write(((v shr 24) and 0xff).toInt())
    }

    private fun le32(o: ByteArrayOutputStream, v: Int) = le32(o, v.toLong())
}

/** Structural checks for a protected APK: alignment + stored rules. */
fun verifyStructure(apk: File): List<String> {
    val problems = mutableListOf<String>()
    val data = apk.readBytes()
    val entries = readCentralDirectory(data)

    if (entries.isEmpty()) {
        problems.add("ERROR: no central directory found")
        return problems
    }
    for ((name, e) in entries) {
        if (name == "resources.arsc") {
            if (e.method != 0) problems.add("ERROR: resources.arsc must be stored")
            if (e.dataOffset % 4L != 0L) problems.add("ERROR: resources.arsc not 4-byte aligned")
        }
        if (name.startsWith("lib/") && name.endsWith(".so")) {
            if (e.method != 0) problems.add("ERROR: $name must be stored")
            if (e.dataOffset % 16384L != 0L) {
                problems.add("ERROR: $name not aligned to 16K pages (offset ${e.dataOffset})")
            }
        }
    }
    return problems
}

data class ZipEntryInfo(
    val name: String,
    val method: Int,
    val dataOffset: Long,
    val size: Long
)

fun readCentralDirectory(data: ByteArray): Map<String, ZipEntryInfo> {
    val map = LinkedHashMap<String, ZipEntryInfo>()
    if (data.size < 22) return map
    var eocd = -1
    for (i in data.size - 22 downTo maxOf(0, data.size - 22 - 65536)) {
        if (data[i].toInt() and 0xff == 0x50 && data[i + 1].toInt() and 0xff == 0x4b &&
            data[i + 2].toInt() and 0xff == 0x05 && data[i + 3].toInt() and 0xff == 0x06
        ) {
            eocd = i
            break
        }
    }
    if (eocd < 0) return map
    val count = le16(data, eocd + 10)
    val cdOffset = le32(data, eocd + 16)
    var p = cdOffset
    repeat(count) {
        if (p + 46 > data.size) return map
        val nameLen = le16(data, p + 28)
        val extraLen = le16(data, p + 30)
        val commentLen = le16(data, p + 32)
        val method = le16(data, p + 10)
        val size = le32(data, p + 24)
        val localOff = le32(data, p + 42)
        val name = String(data, p + 46, nameLen, Charsets.UTF_8)
        // local header to compute data offset
        if (localOff + 30 <= data.size) {
            val lhName = le16(data, localOff + 26)
            val lhExtra = le16(data, localOff + 28)
            val dataOff = localOff.toLong() + 30L + lhName + lhExtra
            map[name] = ZipEntryInfo(name, method, dataOff, size.toLong())
        }
        p += 46 + nameLen + extraLen + commentLen
    }
    return map
}

private fun le16(b: ByteArray, o: Int): Int =
    (b[o].toInt() and 0xff) or ((b[o + 1].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8)

private fun le32(b: ByteArray, o: Int): Int =
    (b[o].toInt() and 0xff) or ((b[o + 1].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or
        ((b[o + 2].toInt() and 0xff) shl 16) or ((b[o + 3].toInt() and 0xff) shl 24)

```

`hyapk-cli/src/main/kotlin/com/blcyber/hyapk/crypto/Crypto.kt`:

```kt
package com.blcyber.hyapk.crypto

import java.security.MessageDigest
import java.security.SecureRandom
import javax.crypto.Mac
import javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec

/**
 * ChaCha20-Poly1305 AEAD and the Hyapk payload KDF, mirroring the C runtime
 * implementation byte-for-byte. Cross-language consistency is enforced by the
 * shared known-answer tests in both codebases.
 */
object Crypto {
    fun sha256(data: ByteArray): ByteArray =
        MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(data)

    fun hmacSha256(key: ByteArray, data: ByteArray): ByteArray {
        val mac = Mac.getInstance("HmacSHA256")
        mac.init(SecretKeySpec(key, "HmacSHA256"))
        return mac.doFinal(data)
    }

    private fun le32(v: Int): ByteArray = byteArrayOf(
        v.toByte(), (v ushr 8).toByte(), (v ushr 16).toByte(), (v ushr 24).toByte()
    )

    private fun rotl(v: Int, c: Int): Int = (v shl c) or (v ushr (32 - c))

    private fun quarter(s: IntArray, a: Int, b: Int, c: Int, d: Int) {
        s[a] = s[a] + s[b]; s[d] = s[d] xor s[a]; s[d] = rotl(s[d], 16)
        s[c] = s[c] + s[d]; s[b] = s[b] xor s[c]; s[b] = rotl(s[b], 12)
        s[a] = s[a] + s[b]; s[d] = s[d] xor s[a]; s[d] = rotl(s[d], 8)
        s[c] = s[c] + s[d]; s[b] = s[b] xor s[c]; s[b] = rotl(s[b], 7)
    }

    fun chacha20Block(key: ByteArray, counter: Int, nonce: ByteArray): ByteArray {
        require(key.size == 32) { "key must be 32 bytes" }
        require(nonce.size == 12) { "nonce must be 12 bytes" }
        val s = IntArray(16)
        s[0] = 0x61707865; s[1] = 0x3320646e; s[2] = 0x79622d32; s[3] = 0x6b206574
        for (i in 0 until 8) {
            s[4 + i] = (key[4 * i].toInt() and 0xff) or
                ((key[4 * i + 1].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or
                ((key[4 * i + 2].toInt() and 0xff) shl 16) or
                ((key[4 * i + 3].toInt() and 0xff) shl 24)
        }
        s[12] = counter
        for (i in 0 until 3) {
            s[13 + i] = (nonce[4 * i].toInt() and 0xff) or
                ((nonce[4 * i + 1].toInt() and 0xff) shl 8) or
                ((nonce[4 * i + 2].toInt() and 0xff) shl 16) or
                ((nonce[4 * i + 3].toInt() and 0xff) shl 24)
        }
        val w = s.copyOf()
        repeat(10) {
            quarter(w, 0, 4, 8, 12); quarter(w, 1, 5, 9, 13)
            quarter(w, 2, 6, 10, 14); quarter(w, 3, 7, 11, 15)
            quarter(w, 0, 5, 10, 15); quarter(w, 1, 6, 11, 12)
            quarter(w, 2, 7, 8, 13); quarter(w, 3, 4, 9, 14)
        }
        val out = ByteArray(64)
        for (i in 0 until 16) {
            val v = w[i] + s[i]
            out[4 * i] = v.toByte()
            out[4 * i + 1] = (v ushr 8).toByte()
            out[4 * i + 2] = (v ushr 16).toByte()
            out[4 * i + 3] = (v ushr 24).toByte()
        }
        return out
    }

    fun chacha20Xor(key: ByteArray, counter: Int, nonce: ByteArray, data: ByteArray): ByteArray {
        val out = ByteArray(data.size)
        var off = 0
        var ctr = counter
        while (off < data.size) {
            val ks = chacha20Block(key, ctr, nonce)
            val take = minOf(64, data.size - off)
            for (i in 0 until take) out[off + i] = (data[off + i].toInt() xor ks[i].toInt()).toByte()
            off += take
            ctr++
        }
        return out
    }

    /** Poly1305 (RFC 8439 section 2.5) using big integers: spec-direct. */
    fun poly1305(key: ByteArray, message: ByteArray): ByteArray {
        require(key.size == 32)
        val r = BigIntegerPos(key.copyOfRange(0, 16))
            .and(CLAMP)
        val s = BigIntegerPos(key.copyOfRange(16, 32))
        val p = java.math.BigInteger.TWO.pow(130).subtract(java.math.BigInteger.valueOf(5))
        var h = java.math.BigInteger.ZERO
        var off = 0
        while (off < message.size) {
            val take = minOf(16, message.size - off)
            val n = BigIntegerPos(message.copyOfRange(off, off + take))
                .add(java.math.BigInteger.ONE.shiftLeft(8 * take))
            h = h.add(n).multiply(r).mod(p)
            off += take
        }
        val tag = h.add(s).and(M128)
        return tag.toByteArray().let { tb ->
            // toByteArray is big-endian; the tag is little-endian
            val out = ByteArray(16)
            for (i in 0 until 16) {
                val idx = tb.size - 1 - i
                out[i] = if (idx >= 0) tb[idx] else 0
            }
            out
        }
    }

    private val CLAMP = java.math.BigInteger("0ffffffc0ffffffc0ffffffc0fffffff", 16)
    private val M128 = java.math.BigInteger.ONE.shiftLeft(128).subtract(java.math.BigInteger.ONE)

    private fun BigIntegerPos(b: ByteArray) = java.math.BigInteger(1, b.reversedArray())

    private fun pad16(data: ByteArray): ByteArray =
        if (data.size % 16 == 0) ByteArray(0) else ByteArray(16 - data.size % 16)

    data class AeadResult(val ciphertext: ByteArray, val tag: ByteArray)

    fun aeadEncrypt(key: ByteArray, nonce: ByteArray, aad: ByteArray, plaintext: ByteArray): AeadResult {
        require(key.size == 32 && nonce.size == 12)
        val block0 = chacha20Block(key, 0, nonce)
        val polyKey = block0.copyOfRange(0, 32)
        val ct = chacha20Xor(key, 1, nonce, plaintext)
        val mac = aad + pad16(aad) + ct + pad16(ct) +
            le32(aad.size) + ByteArray(4) + le32(ct.size) + ByteArray(4)
        return AeadResult(ct, poly1305(polyKey, mac))
    }

    fun aeadDecrypt(key: ByteArray, nonce: ByteArray, aad: ByteArray, ciphertext: ByteArray, tag: ByteArray): ByteArray? {
        require(key.size == 32 && nonce.size == 12 && tag.size == 16)
        val block0 = chacha20Block(key, 0, nonce)
        val polyKey = block0.copyOfRange(0, 32)
        val mac = aad + pad16(aad) + ciphertext + pad16(ciphertext) +
            le32(aad.size) + ByteArray(4) + le32(ciphertext.size) + ByteArray(4)
        val computed = poly1305(polyKey, mac)
        var diff = 0
        for (i in 0 until 16) diff = diff or (computed[i].toInt() xor tag[i].toInt())
        if (diff != 0) return null
        return chacha20Xor(key, 1, nonce, ciphertext)
    }

    fun derivePayloadKey(libSecret: ByteArray, fileSalt: ByteArray): ByteArray {
        require(libSecret.size == 32 && fileSalt.size == 32)
        val master = sha256("HYAPK-KDF1".toByteArray() + libSecret + fileSalt)
        return hmacSha256(master, "hyapk-payload-aead-v1".toByteArray())
    }
}

/** Per-build key material generated by the packer. */
data class DerivedKeys(
    val libSecret: ByteArray,
    /** random file salts, one per encrypted artifact */
    val salts: MutableList<ByteArray> = mutableListOf()
) {
    companion object {
        fun generate(random: SecureRandom): DerivedKeys {
            val secret = ByteArray(32)
            random.nextBytes(secret)
            return DerivedKeys(secret)
        }
    }
}

```

`hyapk-cli/src/main/kotlin/com/blcyber/hyapk/dex/DexStore.kt`:

```kt
package com.blcyber.hyapk.dex

import com.blcyber.hyapk.Log
import com.blcyber.hyapk.apk.ZipSource
import org.jf.dexlib2.Opcodes
import org.jf.dexlib2.builder.MutableMethodImplementation
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.ClassDef
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.Method
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.instruction.Instruction
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.reference.Reference
import org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.ImmutableClassDef
import org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.ImmutableMethod
import org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.ImmutableMethodImplementation
import org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.reference.ImmutableMethodReference
import org.jf.dexlib2.writer.io.FileDataStore
import org.jf.dexlib2.writer.pool.DexPool
import java.io.File

/**
 * Loads all dex files of an APK into dexlib2 objects, tracks per-slot class
 * lists so passes can replace classes, and writes everything back.
 */
class DexStore private constructor(
    val slots: MutableList<Slot>
) {
    /** A dex slot: the zip entry name and its current class list. */
    class Slot(val name: String, val classes: MutableList<ClassDef>)

    var opcodes: Opcodes = Opcodes.forApi(35)
        private set

    companion object {
        fun load(source: ZipSource, minApi: Int): DexStore {
            val slots = mutableListOf<Slot>()
            val names = source.names().filter { it.matches(Regex("classes\\d*\\.dex")) }.sorted()
            if (names.isEmpty()) throw IllegalArgumentException("no dex files found in APK")
            val tmp = createTempDir()
            val ops = try {
                Opcodes.forApi(34)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Opcodes.forApi(32)
            }
            for (name in names) {
                val f = File(tmp, name)
                f.parentFile.mkdirs()
                f.writeBytes(source[name]!!.data)
                val dexFile = org.jf.dexlib2.DexFileFactory.loadDexFile(f, ops)
                val classes = dexFile.classes.toList()
                slots.add(Slot(name, classes.toMutableList()))
                Log.debug("loaded ${classes.size} classes from $name")
            }
            val store = DexStore(slots)
            store.opcodes = ops
            return store
        }

        private fun createTempDir(): File {
            val d = File.createTempFile("hyapk-dex", "")
            d.delete()
            d.mkdirs()
            return d
        }
    }

    fun allClasses(): Sequence<Pair<Slot, ClassDef>> =
        slots.asSequence().flatMap { s -> s.classes.asSequence().map { s to it } }

    fun findClass(type: String): Pair<Slot, ClassDef>? =
        allClasses().firstOrNull { it.second.type == type }

    fun replaceClass(slot: Slot, original: ClassDef, new: ClassDef) {
        val idx = slot.classes.indexOfFirst { it === original || it.type == original.type }
        if (idx >= 0) slot.classes[idx] = new
    }

    fun addClass(slot: Slot, cls: ClassDef) {
        slot.classes.add(cls)
    }

    fun writeAll(onDex: (String, ByteArray, Boolean) -> Unit, compress: Boolean) {
        val tmp = createTempDir()
        for (slot in slots) {
            val f = File(tmp, slot.name)
            val pool = DexPool(opcodes)
            for (c in slot.classes) {
                try {
                    pool.internClass(c)
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    throw IllegalStateException("failed to intern class ${c.type}: ${e.message}", e)
                }
            }
            pool.writeTo(FileDataStore(f))
        }
        for (slot in slots) {
            val f = File(tmp, slot.name)
            onDex(slot.name, f.readBytes(), compress)
        }
    }

    /** Builds an immutable copy of a mutable implementation. */
    fun immutableOf(m: MutableMethodImplementation): ImmutableMethodImplementation =
        ImmutableMethodImplementation(m.registerCount, m.instructions, m.tryBlocks, m.debugItems)

    /** Replaces methods inside a class; returns the new class def. */
    fun withMethods(slot: Slot, cls: ClassDef, transform: (Method) -> Method?): ClassDef {
        val direct = cls.directMethods.map { transform(it) ?: it }
        val virtual = cls.virtualMethods.map { transform(it) ?: it }
        return ImmutableClassDef(
            cls.type,
            cls.accessFlags,
            cls.superclass,
            cls.interfaces,
            cls.sourceFile,
            null,
            cls.staticFields,
            cls.instanceFields,
            direct,
            virtual
        )
    }
}

/** Identifies a method uniquely across passes. */
data class MethodKey(val cls: String, val name: String, val proto: String) {
    override fun toString() = "$cls->$name$proto"
}

fun Method.key(): MethodKey = MethodKey(definingClass, name, "(" + parameterTypes.joinToString("") + ")" + returnType)

/** Creates a method reference pointing at the injected Sec class. */
fun secStringRef(): ImmutableMethodReference =
    ImmutableMethodReference("Lcom/hyapk/runtime/Sec;", "s", listOf("I"), "Ljava/lang/String;")

```

`hyapk-cli/src/main/kotlin/com/blcyber/hyapk/ir/Emitters.kt`:

```kt
package com.blcyber.hyapk.ir

import com.blcyber.hyapk.crypto.Crypto
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream
import java.security.SecureRandom

/**
 * Emitters for the translated IR: encrypted HyVm programs (VMP backend) and
 * C sources (dex2c backend). Both share the payload tables.
 */
object Emitters {

    /* ---------------- VM program emitter ---------------- */

    class VmEmitResult(val program: ByteArray, val perm: IntArray)

    private fun le16(v: Int) = byteArrayOf(v.toByte(), (v shr 8).toByte())
    private fun le32(v: Int) = byteArrayOf(
        v.toByte(), (v shr 8).toByte(), (v shr 16).toByte(), (v shr 24).toByte()
    )
    private fun le64(v: Long) = le32(v.toInt()) + le32((v ushr 32).toInt())

    fun emitVmProgram(ir: Hyir, paramRegs: List<Int>, random: SecureRandom, libSecret: ByteArray): VmEmitResult {
        // opcode permutation: stored = perm[real], map[stored] = real
        val perm = IntArray(64) { it }
        for (i in 63 downTo 1) {
            val j = random.nextInt(i + 1)
            val t = perm[i]; perm[i] = perm[j]; perm[j] = t
        }

        val regCount = ir.registerCount + ir.scratchCount + ir.paramCount + 1
        val argBase = regCount - ir.paramCount // first staging register

        class Buf {
            val bytes = ByteArrayOutputStream()
            val fixups = mutableListOf<Triple<Int, Int, Int>>() // pos, label
            val labelPos = HashMap<Int, Int>()
            fun op(o: VmOp) = bytes.write(perm[o.id])
            fun u8(v: Int) = bytes.write(v and 0xff)
            fun u32(v: Int) = bytes.write(le32(v))
            fun i64(v: Long) = bytes.write(le64(v))
            fun branch32(label: Int) {
                fixups.add(Triple(bytes.size(), label, 0))
                bytes.write(le32(0))
            }
            fun markLabel(id: Int) {
                labelPos[id] = bytes.size()
            }
            val size: Int get() = bytes.size()
        }

        val b = Buf()
        fun emit(hi: HI) {
            when (hi) {
                is HI.Nop -> b.op(VmOp.NOP)
                is HI.Ldi -> { b.op(VmOp.LDI); b.u8(hi.dst); b.i64(hi.imm) }
                is HI.Ldd -> { b.op(VmOp.LDD); b.u8(hi.dst); b.i64(hi.bits) }
                is HI.Move -> { b.op(VmOp.MOVE); b.u8(hi.dst); b.u8(hi.src) }
                is HI.Bin -> {
                    b.op(hi.op); b.u8(hi.dst); b.u8(hi.a); b.u8(hi.b)
                    // division guard: Java throws on zero divisor
                    if (hi.op == VmOp.IDIV || hi.op == VmOp.IREM) {
                        // handled by the interpreter returning failure
                    }
                }
                is HI.Neg -> { b.op(hi.op); b.u8(hi.dst); b.u8(hi.a) }
                is HI.Conv -> { b.op(hi.op); b.u8(hi.dst); b.u8(hi.a) }
                is HI.Cmp -> { b.op(VmOp.ICMP); b.u8(hi.dst); b.u8(hi.a); b.u8(hi.b); b.u8(hi.cond) }
                is HI.Br -> { b.op(VmOp.BR); b.branch32(hi.label) }
                is HI.Brt -> { b.op(VmOp.BRT); b.u8(hi.reg); b.branch32(hi.label) }
                is HI.Brf -> { b.op(VmOp.BRF); b.u8(hi.reg); b.branch32(hi.label) }
                is HI.Str -> { b.op(VmOp.STR); b.u8(hi.dst); b.u32(hi.stringId) }
                is HI.New -> { b.op(VmOp.NEW); b.u8(hi.dst); b.u32(hi.typeIdx) }
                is HI.IGet -> { b.op(VmOp.IGET); b.u8(hi.dst); b.u8(hi.obj); b.u32(hi.fieldIdx) }
                is HI.IPut -> { b.op(VmOp.IPUT); b.u8(hi.obj); b.u8(hi.src); b.u32(hi.fieldIdx) }
                is HI.SGet -> { b.op(VmOp.SGET); b.u8(hi.dst); b.u32(hi.fieldIdx) }
                is HI.SPut -> { b.op(VmOp.SPUT); b.u8(hi.src); b.u32(hi.fieldIdx) }
                is HI.ALen -> { b.op(VmOp.ALEN); b.u8(hi.dst); b.u8(hi.arr) }
                is HI.ANew -> { b.op(VmOp.ANEW); b.u8(hi.dst); b.u8(hi.elemTag); b.u8(hi.lenReg) }
                is HI.AGet -> { b.op(VmOp.AGET); b.u8(hi.dst); b.u8(hi.arr); b.u8(hi.idx) }
                is HI.APut -> { b.op(VmOp.APUT); b.u8(hi.arr); b.u8(hi.idx); b.u8(hi.src) }
                is HI.Invk -> {
                    b.op(VmOp.INVK); b.u8(hi.kind); b.u32(hi.methodIdx); b.u8(hi.args.size)
                    for (a in hi.args) b.u8(a)
                    b.u8(hi.dst ?: 0xFF)
                }
                is HI.Ret -> { b.op(VmOp.RET); b.u8(hi.reg ?: 0) }
                is HI.RetV -> b.op(VmOp.RETV)
                is HI.ChkCast -> { b.op(VmOp.CHKCAST); b.u8(hi.reg); b.u32(hi.typeIdx) }
                is HI.InstOf -> { b.op(VmOp.INSTOF); b.u8(hi.dst); b.u8(hi.reg); b.u32(hi.typeIdx) }
                is HI.Throw -> { b.op(VmOp.THROW); b.u8(hi.reg) }
                is HI.Monitor -> { b.op(VmOp.MONITOR); b.u8(hi.reg); b.u8(hi.mode) }
                is HI.Label -> b.markLabel(hi.id)
            }
        }

        // prologue: move staged args into their dalvik parameter registers
        for ((i, pre) in paramRegs.withIndex()) {
            b.op(VmOp.MOVE); b.u8(pre); b.u8(argBase + i)
        }
        for (hi in ir.instructions) emit(hi)

        val code = b.bytes.toByteArray()

        // resolve label fixups
        for ((pos, label, _) in b.fixups) {
            val target = b.labelPos[label] ?: 0
            val fb = le32(target)
            code[pos] = fb[0]; code[pos + 1] = fb[1]
            code[pos + 2] = fb[2]; code[pos + 3] = fb[3]
        }

        // serialize + encrypt
        val keyHalf = ByteArray(32)
        random.nextBytes(keyHalf)
        val nonce = ByteArray(12)
        random.nextBytes(nonce)
        val key = Crypto.derivePayloadKey(libSecret, keyHalf)

        val header = ByteArrayOutputStream()
        header.write(byteArrayOf(0x48, 0x59, 0x56, 0x4D)) // 'HYVM'
        header.write(le16(1)) // version
        header.write(le16(0)) // reserved
        header.write(le32(code.size))
        header.write(le32(regCount))
        header.write(le16(random.nextInt(0x10000))) // seed
        header.write(le16(0)) // pad
        header.write(nonce)
        header.write(keyHalf)
        header.write(ByteArray(16)) // tag placeholder
        val mapBytes = ByteArray(64)
        for (stored in 0 until 64) {
            mapBytes[stored] = perm[stored].toByte()
        }
        // careful: perm[real] = stored, so map[stored] = real means map[perm[r]] = r
        val inverse = IntArray(64)
        for (real in 0 until 64) inverse[perm[real]] = real
        for (stored in 0 until 64) mapBytes[stored] = inverse[stored].toByte()
        header.write(mapBytes)
        val headerBytes = header.toByteArray()

        val aead = Crypto.aeadEncrypt(key, nonce, headerBytes.copyOf(16), code)
        val out = headerBytes.copyOf()
        // tag at offset 64..79
        System.arraycopy(aead.tag, 0, out, 64, 16)
        return VmEmitResult(out + aead.ciphertext, perm)
    }

    /* ---------------- C emitter (dex2c) ---------------- */

    /** JNI type for a descriptor char. */
    fun jniType(t: String): String = when (t) {
        "V" -> "void"
        "Z" -> "jboolean"
        "B" -> "jbyte"
        "C" -> "jchar"
        "S" -> "jshort"
        "I" -> "jint"
        "J" -> "jlong"
        "F" -> "jfloat"
        "D" -> "jdouble"
        else -> "jobject"
    }

    fun convertIn(t: String, expr: String): String = when (t) {
        "Z", "B", "C", "S", "I", "J" -> "(int64_t)($expr)"
        "F", "D" -> "(double)($expr)"
        else -> "(void *)($expr)"
    }

    fun convertOut(t: String, expr: String): String = when (t) {
        "V" -> expr
        "Z" -> "(jboolean)($expr)"
        "B" -> "(jbyte)($expr)"
        "C" -> "(jchar)($expr)"
        "S" -> "(jshort)($expr)"
        "I" -> "(jint)($expr)"
        "J" -> "(jlong)($expr)"
        "F" -> "(jfloat)($expr)"
        "D" -> "(jdouble)($expr)"
        else -> "(jobject)($expr)"
    }

    /**
     * Emits a dex2c function for [ir]. [paramRegs] holds the dalvik register
     * of each parameter (including this for instance methods).
     */
    fun emitCFunction(ir: Hyir, paramRegs: List<Int>, paramTypes: List<String>, fnName: String): String {
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        val ret = jniType(ir.returnType)
        sb.append("static $ret $fnName(JNIEnv *env")
        sb.append(if (ir.isStatic) ", jclass cls" else ", jobject thiz")
        paramTypes.forEachIndexed { i, t ->
            sb.append(", ${jniType(t)} p$i")
        }
        sb.append(") {\n")
        sb.append("    (void)env; (void)${if (ir.isStatic) "cls" else "thiz"};\n")
        sb.append("    hy_d2c_set_env(env);\n")
        sb.append("    hyvm_val r[${ir.registerCount + ir.scratchCount + 1}];\n")
        sb.append("    int hy_ok = 1;\n")
        sb.append("    memset(r, 0, sizeof(r));\n")
        paramRegs.forEachIndexed { i, pre ->
            sb.append("    r[$pre].i = ${convertIn(paramTypes[i], "p$i")};\n")
        }

        for (hi in ir.instructions) {
            when (hi) {
                is HI.Nop -> {}
                is HI.Ldi -> sb.append("    r[${hi.dst}].i = ${hi.imm}LL;\n")
                is HI.Ldd -> sb.append("    r[${hi.dst}].i = (int64_t)0x${java.lang.Long.toUnsignedString(hi.bits, 16)}ULL;\n")
                is HI.Move -> sb.append("    r[${hi.dst}] = r[${hi.src}];\n")
                is HI.Bin -> {
                    val op = when (hi.op) {
                        VmOp.IADD -> "+"; VmOp.ISUB -> "-"; VmOp.IMUL -> "*"
                        VmOp.IDIV -> "/"; VmOp.IREM -> "%"
                        VmOp.IAND -> "&"; VmOp.IOR -> "|"; VmOp.IXOR -> "^"
                        VmOp.ISHL -> "<<"; VmOp.ISHR -> ">>"; VmOp.IUSHR -> ">>"
                        else -> "+"
                    }
                    if (hi.op == VmOp.IDIV || hi.op == VmOp.IREM) {
                        sb.append("    if (r[${hi.b}].i == 0) { hy_d2c_throw_arithmetic(); return ${defaultRet(ir.returnType)}; }\n")
                        if (hi.op == VmOp.IUSHR) {
                            sb.append("    r[${hi.dst}].i = (int64_t)((uint64_t)r[${hi.a}].i ${op} ((uint64_t)r[${hi.b}].i & 63));\n")
                        } else if (hi.op == VmOp.ISHL || hi.op == VmOp.ISHR) {
                            sb.append("    r[${hi.dst}].i = r[${hi.a}].i ${op} (r[${hi.b}].i & 63);\n")
                        } else {
                            sb.append("    r[${hi.dst}].i = r[${hi.a}].i ${op} r[${hi.b}].i;\n")
                        }
                    } else if (hi.op == VmOp.IUSHR) {
                        sb.append("    r[${hi.dst}].i = (int64_t)((uint64_t)r[${hi.a}].i >> ((uint64_t)r[${hi.b}].i & 63));\n")
                    } else if (hi.op == VmOp.FADD || hi.op == VmOp.FSUB || hi.op == VmOp.FMUL || hi.op == VmOp.FDIV) {
                        val opd = when (hi.op) {
                            VmOp.FADD -> "+"; VmOp.FSUB -> "-"; VmOp.FMUL -> "*"
                            else -> "/"
                        }
                        sb.append("    r[${hi.dst}].d = r[${hi.a}].d $opd r[${hi.b}].d;\n")
                    } else if (hi.op == VmOp.ISHL || hi.op == VmOp.ISHR) {
                        sb.append("    r[${hi.dst}].i = r[${hi.a}].i ${op} (r[${hi.b}].i & 63);\n")
                    } else {
                        sb.append("    r[${hi.dst}].i = r[${hi.a}].i ${op} r[${hi.b}].i;\n")
                    }
                }
                is HI.Neg -> sb.append(
                    if (hi.op == VmOp.FNEG) "    r[${hi.dst}].d = -r[${hi.a}].d;\n"
                    else "    r[${hi.dst}].i = (int64_t)(0ULL - (uint64_t)r[${hi.a}].i);\n"
                )
                is HI.Conv -> sb.append(
                    if (hi.op == VmOp.F2I) "    r[${hi.dst}].i = hy_d2c_d2i(r[${hi.a}].d);\n"
                    else "    r[${hi.dst}].d = (double)r[${hi.a}].i;\n"
                )
                is HI.Cmp -> {
                    val cond = when (hi.cond) {
                        Cond.EQ -> "=="; Cond.NE -> "!="; Cond.LT -> "<"
                        Cond.GE -> ">="; Cond.GT -> ">"; else -> "<="
                    }
                    sb.append("    r[${hi.dst}].i = (r[${hi.a}].i ${cond} r[${hi.b}].i);\n")
                }
                is HI.Br -> { sb.append("    goto hy_L${hi.label};\n") }
                is HI.Brt -> sb.append("    if (r[${hi.reg}].i) goto hy_L${hi.label};\n")
                is HI.Brf -> sb.append("    if (!r[${hi.reg}].i) goto hy_L${hi.label};\n")
                is HI.Str -> sb.append("    r[${hi.dst}].o = hy_d2c_const_string(${hi.stringId}, &hy_ok); if (!hy_ok) return ${defaultRet(ir.returnType)};\n")
                is HI.New -> sb.append("    r[${hi.dst}].o = hy_d2c_new_instance(${hi.typeIdx}, &hy_ok); if (!hy_ok) return ${defaultRet(ir.returnType)};\n")
                is HI.IGet -> sb.append("    r[${hi.dst}] = hy_d2c_iget(${hi.fieldIdx}, r[${hi.obj}].o, &hy_ok); if (!hy_ok) return ${defaultRet(ir.returnType)};\n")
                is HI.IPut -> sb.append("    hy_d2c_iput(${hi.fieldIdx}, r[${hi.obj}].o, r[${hi.src}], &hy_ok); if (!hy_ok) return ${defaultRet(ir.returnType)};\n")
                is HI.SGet -> sb.append("    r[${hi.dst}] = hy_d2c_sget(${hi.fieldIdx}, &hy_ok); if (!hy_ok) return ${defaultRet(ir.returnType)};\n")
                is HI.SPut -> sb.append("    hy_d2c_sput(${hi.fieldIdx}, r[${hi.src}], &hy_ok); if (!hy_ok) return ${defaultRet(ir.returnType)};\n")
                is HI.ALen -> sb.append("    r[${hi.dst}].i = hy_d2c_array_length(r[${hi.arr}].o, &hy_ok); if (!hy_ok) return ${defaultRet(ir.returnType)};\n")
                is HI.ANew -> sb.append("    r[${hi.dst}].o = hy_d2c_new_array(${hi.elemTag}, r[${hi.lenReg}].i, &hy_ok); if (!hy_ok) return ${defaultRet(ir.returnType)};\n")
                is HI.AGet -> sb.append("    r[${hi.dst}] = hy_d2c_array_get(r[${hi.arr}].o, r[${hi.idx}].i, &hy_ok); if (!hy_ok) return ${defaultRet(ir.returnType)};\n")
                is HI.APut -> sb.append("    hy_d2c_array_put(r[${hi.arr}].o, r[${hi.idx}].i, r[${hi.src}], &hy_ok); if (!hy_ok) return ${defaultRet(ir.returnType)};\n")
                is HI.Invk -> {
                    val selfExpr = if (hi.kind == InvokeKind.STATIC) "NULL" else "r[${hi.args.first()}].o"
                    val argsExpr = hi.args.drop(if (hi.kind == InvokeKind.STATIC) 0 else 1)
                    sb.append("    {\n")
                    sb.append("        hyvm_val hy_a[${maxOf(argsExpr.size, 1)}];\n")
                    argsExpr.forEachIndexed { i, a -> sb.append("        hy_a[$i] = r[$a];\n") }
                    sb.append("        hyvm_val hy_rv = hy_d2c_invoke(${hi.methodIdx}, ${hi.kind}, $selfExpr, hy_a, ${argsExpr.size}, &hy_ok);\n")
                    sb.append("        if (!hy_ok) return ${defaultRet(ir.returnType)};\n")
                    if (hi.dst != null) sb.append("        r[${hi.dst}] = hy_rv;\n")
                    sb.append("    }\n")
                }
                is HI.Ret -> {
                    val t = ir.returnType
                    if (t == "V") sb.append("    return;\n")
                    else sb.append("    return ${convertOut(t, "r[${hi.reg!!}]." + if (t == "F" || t == "D") "d" else if (t.startsWith("L") || t.startsWith("[")) "o" else "i")};\n")
                }
                is HI.RetV -> sb.append("    return;\n")
                is HI.ChkCast -> sb.append("    r[${hi.reg}].o = hy_d2c_check_cast(r[${hi.reg}].o, ${hi.typeIdx}, &hy_ok); if (!hy_ok) return ${defaultRet(ir.returnType)};\n")
                is HI.InstOf -> sb.append("    r[${hi.dst}].i = hy_d2c_instance_of(r[${hi.reg}].o, ${hi.typeIdx}, &hy_ok); if (!hy_ok) return ${defaultRet(ir.returnType)};\n")
                is HI.Throw -> sb.append("    hy_d2c_throw_obj(r[${hi.reg}].o, &hy_ok); return ${defaultRet(ir.returnType)};\n")
                is HI.Monitor -> sb.append("    hy_d2c_monitor(r[${hi.reg}].o, ${hi.mode}, &hy_ok); if (!hy_ok) return ${defaultRet(ir.returnType)};\n")
                is HI.Label -> sb.append("hy_L${hi.id}:\n")
            }
        }
        sb.append("    return ${defaultRet(ir.returnType)};\n")
        sb.append("}\n")
        return sb.toString()
    }

    private fun defaultRet(t: String): String = when {
        t == "V" -> ""
        t == "J" -> "0LL"
        t == "F" -> "0.0f"
        t == "D" -> "0.0"
        t.startsWith("L") || t.startsWith("[") -> "NULL"
        else -> "0"
    }
}

```

`hyapk-cli/src/main/kotlin/com/blcyber/hyapk/ir/Hyir.kt`:

```kt
package com.blcyber.hyapk.ir

import org.jf.dexlib2.Opcode
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.Method
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.instruction.Instruction
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.instruction.ReferenceInstruction
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.instruction.formats.*
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.reference.FieldReference
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.reference.MethodReference
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.reference.StringReference
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.reference.TypeReference

/**
 * VM opcode numbers. These MUST match hyapk-native/src/vm/hyvm_opcodes.h;
 * a unit test parses the C header and enforces consistency.
 */
enum class VmOp(val id: Int) {
    NOP(0), LDI(1), LDD(2), MOVE(3),
    IADD(4), ISUB(5), IMUL(6), IDIV(7), IREM(8), INEG(9),
    IAND(10), IOR(11), IXOR(12), ISHL(13), ISHR(14), IUSHR(15),
    FADD(16), FSUB(17), FMUL(18), FDIV(19), FNEG(20),
    I2F(21), F2I(22), D2I(23),
    ICMP(24), FCMP(25),
    BR(26), BRT(27), BRF(28),
    STR(29), NEW(30),
    IGET(31), IPUT(32), SGET(33), SPUT(34),
    ALEN(35), ANEW(36), AGET(37), APUT(38),
    INVK(39), RET(40), RETV(41),
    CHKCAST(42), INSTOF(43), THROW(44), MONITOR(45), MOVEF(46)
}

object InvokeKind {
    const val STATIC = 0
    const val VIRTUAL = 1
    const val SPECIAL = 2
    const val INTERFACE = 3
}

object Cond {
    const val EQ = 0
    const val NE = 1
    const val LT = 2
    const val GE = 3
    const val GT = 4
    const val LE = 5
}

/** Translated method IR. */
class Hyir(
    val registerCount: Int,
    val paramCount: Int,
    val isStatic: Boolean,
    val returnType: String,
    val instructions: List<HI>,
    val scratchCount: Int = 0
)

sealed class HI {
    object Nop : HI()
    data class Ldi(val dst: Int, val imm: Long) : HI()
    data class Ldd(val dst: Int, val bits: Long) : HI()
    data class Move(val dst: Int, val src: Int) : HI()
    data class Bin(val op: VmOp, val dst: Int, val a: Int, val b: Int) : HI()
    data class Neg(val op: VmOp, val dst: Int, val a: Int) : HI()
    data class Conv(val op: VmOp, val dst: Int, val a: Int) : HI()
    data class Cmp(val dst: Int, val a: Int, val b: Int, val cond: Int) : HI()
    data class Br(val label: Int) : HI()
    data class Brt(val reg: Int, val label: Int) : HI()
    data class Brf(val reg: Int, val label: Int) : HI()
    data class Str(val dst: Int, val stringId: Int) : HI()
    data class New(val dst: Int, val typeIdx: Int) : HI()
    data class IGet(val dst: Int, val obj: Int, val fieldIdx: Int) : HI()
    data class IPut(val obj: Int, val src: Int, val fieldIdx: Int) : HI()
    data class SGet(val dst: Int, val fieldIdx: Int) : HI()
    data class SPut(val src: Int, val fieldIdx: Int) : HI()
    data class ALen(val dst: Int, val arr: Int) : HI()
    data class ANew(val dst: Int, val elemTag: Int, val lenReg: Int) : HI()
    data class AGet(val dst: Int, val arr: Int, val idx: Int) : HI()
    data class APut(val arr: Int, val idx: Int, val src: Int) : HI()
    data class Invk(val kind: Int, val methodIdx: Int, val args: List<Int>, val dst: Int?) : HI()
    data class Ret(val reg: Int?) : HI()
    object RetV : HI()
    data class ChkCast(val reg: Int, val typeIdx: Int) : HI()
    data class InstOf(val dst: Int, val reg: Int, val typeIdx: Int) : HI()
    data class Throw(val reg: Int) : HI()
    data class Monitor(val reg: Int, val mode: Int) : HI()
    data class Label(val id: Int) : HI()
}

/**
 * Translates a dexlib2 method into Hyir. Returns a null IR with a reason when
 * the method uses instructions outside the supported subset (documented in
 * the docs architecture reference).
 */
class Translator(
    private val internString: (String) -> Int,
    private val internType: (String) -> Int,
    private val internMethod: (String, String, String, Int) -> Int,
    private val internField: (String, String, Char) -> Int
) {

    data class Result(val ir: Hyir?, val reason: String?)

    private val supported = setOf(
        Opcode.NOP,
        Opcode.CONST_4, Opcode.CONST_16, Opcode.CONST, Opcode.CONST_HIGH16,
        Opcode.CONST_WIDE_16, Opcode.CONST_WIDE_32, Opcode.CONST_WIDE, Opcode.CONST_WIDE_HIGH16,
        Opcode.CONST_STRING, Opcode.CONST_STRING_JUMBO,
        Opcode.MOVE, Opcode.MOVE_FROM16, Opcode.MOVE_16,
        Opcode.MOVE_OBJECT, Opcode.MOVE_OBJECT_FROM16, Opcode.MOVE_OBJECT_16,
        Opcode.MOVE_WIDE, Opcode.MOVE_WIDE_FROM16, Opcode.MOVE_WIDE_16,
        Opcode.NEW_INSTANCE, Opcode.ARRAY_LENGTH, Opcode.NEW_ARRAY,
        Opcode.ADD_INT, Opcode.ADD_INT_2ADDR, Opcode.SUB_INT, Opcode.SUB_INT_2ADDR,
        Opcode.MUL_INT, Opcode.MUL_INT_2ADDR, Opcode.DIV_INT, Opcode.DIV_INT_2ADDR,
        Opcode.REM_INT, Opcode.REM_INT_2ADDR, Opcode.AND_INT, Opcode.AND_INT_2ADDR,
        Opcode.OR_INT, Opcode.OR_INT_2ADDR, Opcode.XOR_INT, Opcode.XOR_INT_2ADDR,
        Opcode.SHL_INT, Opcode.SHL_INT_2ADDR, Opcode.SHR_INT, Opcode.SHR_INT_2ADDR,
        Opcode.USHR_INT, Opcode.USHR_INT_2ADDR, Opcode.NEG_INT,
        Opcode.ADD_LONG, Opcode.ADD_LONG_2ADDR, Opcode.SUB_LONG, Opcode.SUB_LONG_2ADDR,
        Opcode.MUL_LONG, Opcode.MUL_LONG_2ADDR, Opcode.DIV_LONG, Opcode.DIV_LONG_2ADDR,
        Opcode.REM_LONG, Opcode.REM_LONG_2ADDR, Opcode.AND_LONG, Opcode.AND_LONG_2ADDR,
        Opcode.OR_LONG, Opcode.OR_LONG_2ADDR, Opcode.XOR_LONG, Opcode.XOR_LONG_2ADDR,
        Opcode.SHL_LONG, Opcode.SHL_LONG_2ADDR, Opcode.SHR_LONG, Opcode.SHR_LONG_2ADDR,
        Opcode.USHR_LONG, Opcode.USHR_LONG_2ADDR, Opcode.NEG_LONG,
        Opcode.ADD_DOUBLE, Opcode.ADD_DOUBLE_2ADDR, Opcode.SUB_DOUBLE, Opcode.SUB_DOUBLE_2ADDR,
        Opcode.MUL_DOUBLE, Opcode.MUL_DOUBLE_2ADDR, Opcode.DIV_DOUBLE, Opcode.DIV_DOUBLE_2ADDR,
        Opcode.NEG_DOUBLE, Opcode.DOUBLE_TO_LONG, Opcode.LONG_TO_DOUBLE, Opcode.INT_TO_DOUBLE, Opcode.DOUBLE_TO_INT,
        Opcode.IF_EQ, Opcode.IF_NE, Opcode.IF_LT, Opcode.IF_GE, Opcode.IF_GT, Opcode.IF_LE,
        Opcode.IF_EQZ, Opcode.IF_NEZ, Opcode.IF_LTZ, Opcode.IF_GEZ, Opcode.IF_GTZ, Opcode.IF_LEZ,
        Opcode.GOTO, Opcode.GOTO_16, Opcode.GOTO_32,
        Opcode.INVOKE_STATIC, Opcode.INVOKE_STATIC_RANGE,
        Opcode.INVOKE_VIRTUAL, Opcode.INVOKE_VIRTUAL_RANGE,
        Opcode.INVOKE_DIRECT, Opcode.INVOKE_DIRECT_RANGE,
        Opcode.INVOKE_SUPER, Opcode.INVOKE_SUPER_RANGE,
        Opcode.INVOKE_INTERFACE, Opcode.INVOKE_INTERFACE_RANGE,
        Opcode.MOVE_RESULT, Opcode.MOVE_RESULT_WIDE, Opcode.MOVE_RESULT_OBJECT,
        Opcode.RETURN, Opcode.RETURN_WIDE, Opcode.RETURN_OBJECT, Opcode.RETURN_VOID,
        Opcode.CHECK_CAST, Opcode.INSTANCE_OF,
        Opcode.MONITOR_ENTER, Opcode.MONITOR_EXIT,
        Opcode.THROW,
        Opcode.IGET, Opcode.IGET_WIDE, Opcode.IGET_BOOLEAN, Opcode.IGET_BYTE, Opcode.IGET_CHAR, Opcode.IGET_SHORT,
        Opcode.IPUT, Opcode.IPUT_WIDE, Opcode.IPUT_BOOLEAN, Opcode.IPUT_BYTE, Opcode.IPUT_CHAR, Opcode.IPUT_SHORT,
        Opcode.SGET, Opcode.SGET_WIDE, Opcode.SGET_BOOLEAN, Opcode.SGET_BYTE, Opcode.SGET_CHAR, Opcode.SGET_SHORT,
        Opcode.SPUT, Opcode.SPUT_WIDE, Opcode.SPUT_BOOLEAN, Opcode.SPUT_BYTE, Opcode.SPUT_CHAR, Opcode.SPUT_SHORT
    )

    fun translate(m: Method): Result {
        val impl = m.implementation ?: return Result(null, "abstract or native")
        if (impl.tryBlocks.isNotEmpty()) return Result(null, "has try/catch")

        val instrs = impl.instructions.toList()
        for (ins in instrs) {
            if (ins.opcode !in supported) return Result(null, "unsupported opcode ${ins.opcode.name}")
        }

        val isStatic = (m.accessFlags and 0x0008) != 0
        val paramTypes = m.parameterTypes.toList()
        val paramRegs = ArrayList<Int>(paramTypes.size)
        var reg = impl.registerCount - (if (isStatic) 0 else 1) -
            paramTypes.fold(0) { acc, t -> acc + (if (t == "J" || t == "D") 2 else 1) }
        if (!isStatic) {
            paramRegs.add(reg); reg += 1
        }
        for (t in paramTypes) {
            paramRegs.add(reg)
            reg += if (t == "J" || t == "D") 2 else 1
        }

        val out = ArrayList<HI>()
        var scratch = impl.registerCount

        // dexlib2 branch offsets are INSTRUCTION-RELATIVE and in CODE UNITS;
        // key the map by the absolute code-unit offset of each instruction.
        val codeOffsetToIndex = HashMap<Int, Int>()
        val instrCuOffsets = IntArray(instrs.size)
        var cu = 0
        for ((idx, ins) in instrs.withIndex()) {
            codeOffsetToIndex[cu] = idx
            instrCuOffsets[idx] = cu
            cu += ins.codeUnits
        }
        val labelAt = HashMap<Int, Int>()
        var nextLabel = 0
        fun labelFor(index: Int): Int = labelAt.getOrPut(index) { nextLabel++ }

        for (ins in instrs) {
            when (ins.opcode) {
                Opcode.GOTO, Opcode.GOTO_16, Opcode.GOTO_32 -> {}
                Opcode.IF_EQ, Opcode.IF_NE, Opcode.IF_LT, Opcode.IF_GE, Opcode.IF_GT, Opcode.IF_LE -> {
                    val b = ins as Instruction22t
                    labelFor(codeOffsetToIndex[instrCuOffsets[idxOf(ins, instrs)] + b.codeOffset] ?: return Result(null, "bad branch"))
                }
                Opcode.IF_EQZ, Opcode.IF_NEZ, Opcode.IF_LTZ, Opcode.IF_GEZ, Opcode.IF_GTZ, Opcode.IF_LEZ -> {
                    val b = ins as Instruction21t
                    labelFor(codeOffsetToIndex[instrCuOffsets[idxOf(ins, instrs)] + b.codeOffset] ?: return Result(null, "bad branch"))
                }
                else -> {}
            }
        }

        fun intBin(ins23x: Instruction23x, op: VmOp) =
            out.add(HI.Bin(op, ins23x.registerA, ins23x.registerB, ins23x.registerC))

        fun intBin2addr(ins12x: Instruction12x, op: VmOp) =
            out.add(HI.Bin(op, ins12x.registerA, ins12x.registerA, ins12x.registerB))

        fun intNeg(ins12x: Instruction12x) =
            out.add(HI.Neg(VmOp.INEG, ins12x.registerA, ins12x.registerB))

        fun primFieldType(t: String): Char? = when (t) {
            "I" -> 'I'; "J" -> 'J'; "Z" -> 'Z'; "B" -> 'B'; "C" -> 'C'; "S" -> 'S'; "F" -> 'F'; "D" -> 'D'
            else -> null
        }

        var i = 0
        var curCu = 0
        while (i < instrs.size) {
            val ins = instrs[i]
            labelAt[i]?.let { out.add(HI.Label(it)) }
            curCu = instrCuOffsets[i]

            when (ins.opcode) {
                Opcode.NOP -> out.add(HI.Nop)

                Opcode.CONST_4 -> out.add(HI.Ldi((ins as Instruction11n).registerA, ins.narrowLiteral.toLong()))
                Opcode.CONST_16 -> out.add(HI.Ldi((ins as Instruction21s).registerA, ins.narrowLiteral.toLong()))
                Opcode.CONST_HIGH16 -> out.add(HI.Ldi((ins as Instruction21ih).registerA, (ins as Instruction21ih).narrowLiteral.toLong()))
                Opcode.CONST -> out.add(HI.Ldi((ins as Instruction31i).registerA, ins.narrowLiteral.toLong()))
                Opcode.CONST_WIDE_16 -> out.add(HI.Ldi((ins as Instruction21s).registerA, ins.narrowLiteral.toLong()))
                Opcode.CONST_WIDE_32 -> out.add(HI.Ldi((ins as Instruction31i).registerA, ins.narrowLiteral.toLong()))
                Opcode.CONST_WIDE_HIGH16 -> out.add(HI.Ldi((ins as Instruction21lh).registerA, (ins as Instruction21lh).wideLiteral))
                Opcode.CONST_WIDE -> {
                    val w = ins as Instruction51l
                    val d = Double.fromBits(w.wideLiteral)
                    out.add(HI.Ldd(w.registerA, d.toRawBits().toLong()))
                }

                Opcode.CONST_STRING, Opcode.CONST_STRING_JUMBO -> {
                    val c = ins as Instruction21c
                    out.add(HI.Str(c.registerA, internString((c.reference as StringReference).string)))
                }

                Opcode.MOVE, Opcode.MOVE_OBJECT, Opcode.MOVE_WIDE -> {
                    val mv = ins as Instruction12x
                    out.add(HI.Move(mv.registerA, mv.registerB))
                }
                Opcode.MOVE_FROM16, Opcode.MOVE_OBJECT_FROM16, Opcode.MOVE_WIDE_FROM16 -> {
                    val mv = ins as Instruction22x
                    out.add(HI.Move(mv.registerA, mv.registerB))
                }
                Opcode.MOVE_16, Opcode.MOVE_OBJECT_16, Opcode.MOVE_WIDE_16 -> {
                    val mv = ins as Instruction32x
                    out.add(HI.Move(mv.registerA, mv.registerB))
                }

                Opcode.NEW_INSTANCE -> {
                    val c = ins as Instruction21c
                    out.add(HI.New(c.registerA, internType((c.reference as TypeReference).type)))
                }

                Opcode.ARRAY_LENGTH -> {
                    val a = ins as Instruction12x
                    out.add(HI.ALen(a.registerA, a.registerB))
                }

                Opcode.NEW_ARRAY -> {
                    val c = ins as Instruction22c
                    val t = (c.reference as TypeReference).type
                    val elemTag = when (t) {
                        "[J" -> 0
                        "[I" -> 1
                        "[D" -> 2
                        else -> 3
                    }
                    out.add(HI.ANew(c.registerA, elemTag, c.registerB))
                }

                Opcode.ADD_INT -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.IADD)
                Opcode.SUB_INT -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.ISUB)
                Opcode.MUL_INT -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.IMUL)
                Opcode.DIV_INT -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.IDIV)
                Opcode.REM_INT -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.IREM)
                Opcode.AND_INT -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.IAND)
                Opcode.OR_INT -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.IOR)
                Opcode.XOR_INT -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.IXOR)
                Opcode.SHL_INT -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.ISHL)
                Opcode.SHR_INT -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.ISHR)
                Opcode.USHR_INT -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.IUSHR)
                Opcode.ADD_INT_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.IADD)
                Opcode.SUB_INT_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.ISUB)
                Opcode.MUL_INT_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.IMUL)
                Opcode.DIV_INT_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.IDIV)
                Opcode.REM_INT_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.IREM)
                Opcode.AND_INT_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.IAND)
                Opcode.OR_INT_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.IOR)
                Opcode.XOR_INT_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.IXOR)
                Opcode.SHL_INT_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.ISHL)
                Opcode.SHR_INT_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.ISHR)
                Opcode.USHR_INT_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.IUSHR)
                Opcode.NEG_INT -> out.add(HI.Neg(VmOp.INEG, (ins as Instruction12x).registerA, ins.registerB))

                Opcode.ADD_LONG -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.IADD)
                Opcode.SUB_LONG -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.ISUB)
                Opcode.MUL_LONG -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.IMUL)
                Opcode.DIV_LONG -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.IDIV)
                Opcode.REM_LONG -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.IREM)
                Opcode.AND_LONG -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.IAND)
                Opcode.OR_LONG -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.IOR)
                Opcode.XOR_LONG -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.IXOR)
                Opcode.SHL_LONG -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.ISHL)
                Opcode.SHR_LONG -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.ISHR)
                Opcode.USHR_LONG -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.IUSHR)
                Opcode.ADD_LONG_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.IADD)
                Opcode.SUB_LONG_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.ISUB)
                Opcode.MUL_LONG_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.IMUL)
                Opcode.DIV_LONG_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.IDIV)
                Opcode.REM_LONG_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.IREM)
                Opcode.AND_LONG_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.IAND)
                Opcode.OR_LONG_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.IOR)
                Opcode.XOR_LONG_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.IXOR)
                Opcode.SHL_LONG_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.ISHL)
                Opcode.SHR_LONG_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.ISHR)
                Opcode.USHR_LONG_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.IUSHR)
                Opcode.NEG_LONG -> out.add(HI.Neg(VmOp.INEG, (ins as Instruction12x).registerA, ins.registerB))

                Opcode.ADD_DOUBLE -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.FADD)
                Opcode.SUB_DOUBLE -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.FSUB)
                Opcode.MUL_DOUBLE -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.FMUL)
                Opcode.DIV_DOUBLE -> intBin(ins as Instruction23x, VmOp.FDIV)
                Opcode.ADD_DOUBLE_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.FADD)
                Opcode.SUB_DOUBLE_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.FSUB)
                Opcode.MUL_DOUBLE_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.FMUL)
                Opcode.DIV_DOUBLE_2ADDR -> intBin2addr(ins as Instruction12x, VmOp.FDIV)
                Opcode.NEG_DOUBLE -> out.add(HI.Neg(VmOp.FNEG, (ins as Instruction12x).registerA, ins.registerB))
                Opcode.DOUBLE_TO_LONG, Opcode.DOUBLE_TO_INT ->
                    out.add(HI.Conv(VmOp.F2I, (ins as Instruction12x).registerA, ins.registerB))
                Opcode.LONG_TO_DOUBLE, Opcode.INT_TO_DOUBLE ->
                    out.add(HI.Conv(VmOp.I2F, (ins as Instruction12x).registerA, ins.registerB))

                Opcode.IF_EQ, Opcode.IF_NE, Opcode.IF_LT, Opcode.IF_GE, Opcode.IF_GT, Opcode.IF_LE -> {
                    val t = ins as Instruction22t
                    val c = when (ins.opcode) {
                        Opcode.IF_EQ -> Cond.EQ
                        Opcode.IF_NE -> Cond.NE
                        Opcode.IF_LT -> Cond.LT
                        Opcode.IF_GE -> Cond.GE
                        Opcode.IF_GT -> Cond.GT
                        else -> Cond.LE
                    }
                    val target = labelFor(codeOffsetToIndex[curCu + t.codeOffset] ?: return Result(null, "bad branch"))
                    out.add(HI.Cmp(scratch, t.registerA, t.registerB, c))
                    out.add(HI.Brt(scratch, target))
                    scratch++
                }
                Opcode.IF_EQZ, Opcode.IF_NEZ, Opcode.IF_LTZ, Opcode.IF_GEZ, Opcode.IF_GTZ, Opcode.IF_LEZ -> {
                    val t = ins as Instruction21t
                    val c = when (ins.opcode) {
                        Opcode.IF_EQZ -> Cond.EQ
                        Opcode.IF_NEZ -> Cond.NE
                        Opcode.IF_LTZ -> Cond.LT
                        Opcode.IF_GEZ -> Cond.GE
                        Opcode.IF_GTZ -> Cond.GT
                        else -> Cond.LE
                    }
                    val target = labelFor(codeOffsetToIndex[curCu + t.codeOffset] ?: return Result(null, "bad branch"))
                    val zero = scratch++
                    out.add(HI.Ldi(zero, 0))
                    out.add(HI.Cmp(zero, t.registerA, zero, c))
                    out.add(HI.Brt(zero, target))
                }

                Opcode.GOTO -> out.add(HI.Br(labelFor(codeOffsetToIndex[curCu + (ins as Instruction10t).codeOffset] ?: return Result(null, "bad goto"))))
                Opcode.GOTO_16 -> out.add(HI.Br(labelFor(codeOffsetToIndex[curCu + (ins as Instruction20t).codeOffset] ?: return Result(null, "bad goto"))))
                Opcode.GOTO_32 -> out.add(HI.Br(labelFor(codeOffsetToIndex[curCu + (ins as Instruction30t).codeOffset] ?: return Result(null, "bad goto"))))

                Opcode.INVOKE_STATIC, Opcode.INVOKE_STATIC_RANGE,
                Opcode.INVOKE_VIRTUAL, Opcode.INVOKE_VIRTUAL_RANGE,
                Opcode.INVOKE_DIRECT, Opcode.INVOKE_DIRECT_RANGE,
                Opcode.INVOKE_SUPER, Opcode.INVOKE_SUPER_RANGE,
                Opcode.INVOKE_INTERFACE, Opcode.INVOKE_INTERFACE_RANGE -> {
                    val isRange = ins.opcode.name.endsWith("RANGE")
                    val ref = (ins as ReferenceInstruction).reference as MethodReference
                    val kind = when (ins.opcode) {
                        Opcode.INVOKE_STATIC, Opcode.INVOKE_STATIC_RANGE -> InvokeKind.STATIC
                        Opcode.INVOKE_VIRTUAL, Opcode.INVOKE_VIRTUAL_RANGE -> InvokeKind.VIRTUAL
                        Opcode.INVOKE_INTERFACE, Opcode.INVOKE_INTERFACE_RANGE -> InvokeKind.INTERFACE
                        else -> InvokeKind.SPECIAL
                    }
                    val argRegs: IntArray = if (isRange) {
                        val r = ins as Instruction3rc
                        IntArray(r.registerCount) { r.startRegister + it }
                    } else {
                        val r = ins as Instruction35c
                        when (r.registerCount) {
                            0 -> IntArray(0)
                            1 -> intArrayOf(r.registerC)
                            2 -> intArrayOf(r.registerC, r.registerD)
                            3 -> intArrayOf(r.registerC, r.registerD, r.registerE)
                            4 -> intArrayOf(r.registerC, r.registerD, r.registerE, r.registerF)
                            else -> intArrayOf(r.registerC, r.registerD, r.registerE, r.registerF, r.registerG)
                        }
                    }
                    val sig = "(" + ref.parameterTypes.joinToString("") + ")" + ref.returnType
                    val mid = internMethod(ref.definingClass, ref.name, sig, kind)

                    var dst: Int? = null
                    val next = instrs.getOrNull(i + 1)
                    if (next != null && (next.opcode == Opcode.MOVE_RESULT ||
                            next.opcode == Opcode.MOVE_RESULT_WIDE || next.opcode == Opcode.MOVE_RESULT_OBJECT)
                    ) {
                        dst = (next as Instruction11x).registerA
                        i++
                    }
                    out.add(HI.Invk(kind, mid, argRegs.toList(), dst))
                }

                Opcode.MOVE_RESULT, Opcode.MOVE_RESULT_WIDE, Opcode.MOVE_RESULT_OBJECT ->
                    return Result(null, "orphan move-result")

                Opcode.RETURN, Opcode.RETURN_WIDE, Opcode.RETURN_OBJECT ->
                    out.add(HI.Ret((ins as Instruction11x).registerA))
                Opcode.RETURN_VOID -> out.add(HI.RetV)

                Opcode.CHECK_CAST -> {
                    val c = ins as Instruction21c
                    out.add(HI.ChkCast(c.registerA, internType((c.reference as TypeReference).type)))
                }
                Opcode.INSTANCE_OF -> {
                    val c = ins as Instruction22c
                    out.add(HI.InstOf(c.registerA, c.registerB, internType((c.reference as TypeReference).type)))
                }
                Opcode.MONITOR_ENTER -> out.add(HI.Monitor((ins as Instruction11x).registerA, 0))
                Opcode.MONITOR_EXIT -> out.add(HI.Monitor((ins as Instruction11x).registerA, 1))
                Opcode.THROW -> out.add(HI.Throw((ins as Instruction11x).registerA))

                Opcode.IGET, Opcode.IGET_WIDE, Opcode.IGET_BOOLEAN, Opcode.IGET_BYTE, Opcode.IGET_CHAR, Opcode.IGET_SHORT -> {
                    val c = ins as Instruction22c
                    val fr = c.reference as FieldReference
                    val fc = primFieldType(fr.type) ?: return Result(null, "object field ${fr.name}")
                    out.add(HI.IGet(c.registerA, c.registerB, internField(fr.definingClass, fr.name, fc)))
                }
                Opcode.IPUT, Opcode.IPUT_WIDE, Opcode.IPUT_BOOLEAN, Opcode.IPUT_BYTE, Opcode.IPUT_CHAR, Opcode.IPUT_SHORT -> {
                    val c = ins as Instruction22c
                    val fr = c.reference as FieldReference
                    val fc = primFieldType(fr.type) ?: return Result(null, "object field ${fr.name}")
                    out.add(HI.IPut(c.registerB, c.registerA, internField(fr.definingClass, fr.name, fc)))
                }
                Opcode.SGET, Opcode.SGET_WIDE, Opcode.SGET_BOOLEAN, Opcode.SGET_BYTE, Opcode.SGET_CHAR, Opcode.SGET_SHORT -> {
                    val c = ins as Instruction21c
                    val fr = c.reference as FieldReference
                    val fc = primFieldType(fr.type) ?: return Result(null, "object field ${fr.name}")
                    out.add(HI.SGet(c.registerA, internField(fr.definingClass, fr.name, fc)))
                }
                Opcode.SPUT, Opcode.SPUT_WIDE, Opcode.SPUT_BOOLEAN, Opcode.SPUT_BYTE, Opcode.SPUT_CHAR, Opcode.SPUT_SHORT -> {
                    val c = ins as Instruction21c
                    val fr = c.reference as FieldReference
                    val fc = primFieldType(fr.type) ?: return Result(null, "object field ${fr.name}")
                    out.add(HI.SPut(c.registerA, internField(fr.definingClass, fr.name, fc)))
                }

                else -> return Result(null, "unhandled ${ins.opcode.name}")
            }
            i++
        }

        return Result(
            Hyir(impl.registerCount, paramRegs.size, isStatic, m.returnType, out, scratch),
            null
        )
    }

    private fun idxOf(ins: Instruction, instrs: List<Instruction>): Int =
        instrs.indexOf(ins)
}

```

`hyapk-cli/src/main/kotlin/com/blcyber/hyapk/passes/Conversion.kt`:

```kt
package com.blcyber.hyapk.passes

import com.blcyber.hyapk.Log
import com.blcyber.hyapk.apk.ApkInfo
import com.blcyber.hyapk.dex.DexStore
import com.blcyber.hyapk.ir.Emitters
import com.blcyber.hyapk.ir.InvokeKind
import com.blcyber.hyapk.ir.Translator
import org.jf.dexlib2.AccessFlags
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.ClassDef
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.Method
import org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.ImmutableClassDef
import org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.ImmutableMethod
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream
import java.io.File

/**
 * Method conversion pass: selects methods for dex2c and VMP by pattern,
 * translates them and converts them into native stubs. Generated C sources
 * and VM programs are collected in the payload for the native build pass.
 */
object Conversion {

    class Converted(
        val className: String,
        val methodName: String,
        val signature: String,
        val fnName: String,
        val kind: String
    )

    val converted = mutableListOf<Converted>()
    val skipped = mutableListOf<Pair<String, String>>()

    fun run(ctx: PipelineContext) {
        val translator = Translator(
            internString = { ctx.payload.internString(it) },
            internType = { ctx.payload.internType(it) },
            internMethod = { c, n, s, k -> ctx.payload.internMethod(c, n, s, k) },
            internField = { c, n, t -> ctx.payload.internField(c, n, t) }
        )

        data class Target(val slot: DexStore.Slot, val cls: ClassDef, val m: Method, val kind: String)

        val targets = mutableListOf<Target>()
        for ((slot, cls) in ctx.dexStore.allClasses().toList()) {
            if (isExcluded(cls, ctx)) continue
            val dotted = cls.type.removePrefix("L").removeSuffix(";").replace('/', '.')
            for (m in cls.directMethods) {
                val member = m.name
                val d2c = ctx.opts.dex2cPatterns.any { matchPattern(it, dotted, member) }
                val vmp = ctx.opts.vmpPatterns.any { matchPattern(it, dotted, member) }
                when {
                    d2c -> targets.add(Target(slot, cls, m, "d2c"))
                    vmp -> targets.add(Target(slot, cls, m, "vmp"))
                }
            }
            for (m in cls.virtualMethods) {
                val member = m.name
                val d2c = ctx.opts.dex2cPatterns.any { matchPattern(it, dotted, member) }
                val vmp = ctx.opts.vmpPatterns.any { matchPattern(it, dotted, member) }
                when {
                    d2c -> targets.add(Target(slot, cls, m, "d2c"))
                    vmp -> targets.add(Target(slot, cls, m, "vmp"))
                }
            }
        }

        val cParts = StringBuilder()
        var fnIndex = 0

        val updates = mutableListOf<Triple<DexStore.Slot, ClassDef, ClassDef>>()
        val stubs = linkedMapOf<String, MutableList<Method>>()
        val classSlots = linkedMapOf<String, Pair<DexStore.Slot, ClassDef>>()

        for (t in targets) {
            if (t.m.name == "<init>" || t.m.name == "<clinit>") {
                skipped.add(t.m.definingClass + "#" + t.m.name to "constructor")
                continue
            }
            if ((t.m.accessFlags and AccessFlags.NATIVE.value) != 0) {
                skipped.add(t.m.definingClass + "#" + t.m.name to "already native")
                continue
            }
            if ((t.m.accessFlags and AccessFlags.ABSTRACT.value) != 0) {
                skipped.add(t.m.definingClass + "#" + t.m.name to "abstract")
                continue
            }
            // synchronized/strictfp flags survive; native conversion keeps the rest
            val result = translator.translate(t.m)
            if (result.ir == null) {
                skipped.add(t.m.definingClass + "#" + t.m.name to (result.reason ?: "unknown"))
                continue
            }
            val ir = result.ir
            val paramTypes = ArrayList<String>()
            if (!ir.isStatic) paramTypes.add(t.cls.type)
            for (pt in t.m.parameterTypes) paramTypes.add(pt.toString())

            // param first registers
            val paramRegs = ArrayList<Int>()
            var reg = ir.registerCount - paramTypes.fold(0) { acc, t2 -> acc + (if (t2 == "J" || t2 == "D") 2 else 1) }
            for (pt in paramTypes) {
                paramRegs.add(reg)
                reg += if (pt == "J" || pt == "D") 2 else 1
            }

            val fnName: String
            // registration signature excludes the receiver; the thunk's C
            // signature includes it as the first parameter
            val regSig = "(" + t.m.parameterTypes.joinToString("") + ")" + t.m.returnType

            if (t.kind == "vmp") {
                val program = Emitters.emitVmProgram(ir, paramRegs, ctx.random, ctx.keys.libSecret)
                val mid = ctx.payload.nativeRegs.size
                ctx.payload.vmPrograms[mid] = program.program
                fnName = "hy_vmp_thunk_$fnIndex"
                cParts.append(vmThunk(fnName, mid, ir, paramTypes))
            } else {
                fnName = "hy_d2c_fn_$fnIndex"
                cParts.append(Emitters.emitCFunction(ir, paramRegs, paramTypes, fnName))
            }
            fnIndex++

            val dottedClass = t.cls.type.removePrefix("L").removeSuffix(";").replace('/', '.')
            ctx.payload.nativeRegs.add(
                PayloadBuilder.NativeReg(dottedClass, t.m.name, regSig, fnName)
            )
            converted.add(Converted(dottedClass, t.m.name, regSig, fnName, t.kind))
            ctx.report.log(t.kind, "${dottedClass}.${t.m.name}${regSig} -> ${if (t.kind == "vmp") "VM program" else "C function"}")

            // remember the stub for the per-class rebuild below
            stubs.getOrPut(t.cls.type) { mutableListOf() }.add(t.m)
            classSlots[t.cls.type] = t.slot to t.cls
        }

        // apply ALL native stubs of each class in a single rebuild
        for ((type, methods) in stubs) {
            val (slot, cls) = classSlots[type]!!
            fun toStub(m: Method): Method? {
                val match = methods.any { sel -> sel === m || (sel.name == m.name && sel.parameterTypes.toList() == m.parameterTypes.toList()) }
                if (!match) return null
                return ImmutableMethod(
                    cls.type, m.name, m.parameters, m.returnType,
                    m.accessFlags or AccessFlags.NATIVE.value,
                    m.annotations, m.hiddenApiRestrictions, null
                )
            }
            val newDirect = cls.directMethods.map { toStub(it) ?: it }
            val newVirtual = cls.virtualMethods.map { toStub(it) ?: it }
            updates.add(
                Triple(
                    slot, cls,
                    ImmutableClassDef(
                        cls.type, cls.accessFlags, cls.superclass, cls.interfaces,
                        cls.sourceFile, cls.annotations, cls.staticFields, cls.instanceFields,
                        newDirect, newVirtual
                    )
                )
            )
        }

        for ((slot, old, new) in updates) ctx.dexStore.replaceClass(slot, old, new)

        if (converted.isNotEmpty()) {
            ctx.generatedCBody = cParts.toString()
            ctx.report.count("methods converted to native", converted.size)
            Log.info("converted ${converted.size} methods (${skipped.size} skipped)")
            for ((who, why) in skipped.take(20)) {
                Log.debug("skipped $who: $why")
            }
        }
    }

    private fun isExcluded(cls: ClassDef, ctx: PipelineContext): Boolean {
        if (cls.type == ctx.apkInfo.applicationClass) return true
        if (cls.type.startsWith("Lcom/hyapk/runtime/")) return true
        if (cls.type.startsWith("Landroid/") || cls.type.startsWith("Ljava/") ||
            cls.type.startsWith("Landroidx/") || cls.type.startsWith("Lkotlin/")
        ) return true
        return false
    }

    private fun vmThunk(fnName: String, methodIdx: Int, ir: com.blcyber.hyapk.ir.Hyir, paramTypes: List<String>): String {
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        val ret = Emitters.jniType(ir.returnType)
        sb.append("static $ret $fnName(JNIEnv *env")
        sb.append(if (ir.isStatic) ", jclass cls" else ", jobject thiz")
        paramTypes.forEachIndexed { i, t -> sb.append(", ${Emitters.jniType(t)} p$i") }
        sb.append(") {\n")
        sb.append("    (void)env; (void)${if (ir.isStatic) "cls" else "thiz"};\n")
        sb.append("    hy_d2c_set_env(env);\n")
        if (paramTypes.isNotEmpty()) {
            sb.append("    hyvm_val hy_args[${paramTypes.size}];\n")
            paramTypes.forEachIndexed { i, t ->
                val cv = Emitters.convertIn(t, "p$i")
                val field = if (t == "F" || t == "D") "d" else if (t.startsWith("L") || t.startsWith("[")) "o" else "i"
                // wide/objects go through the raw bits; floats via double promotion
                if (field == "d") {
                    sb.append("    hy_args[$i].d = $cv;\n")
                } else if (field == "o") {
                    sb.append("    hy_args[$i].o = (void *)p$i;\n")
                } else {
                    sb.append("    hy_args[$i].i = $cv;\n")
                }
            }
            sb.append("    hyvm_val hy_ret;\n")
            sb.append("    if (hy_d2c_run_vm(env, $methodIdx, hy_args, ${paramTypes.size}, &hy_ret) != 0) {\n")
            sb.append("        return ${vmDefaultRet(ir.returnType)};\n")
            sb.append("    }\n")
        } else {
            sb.append("    hyvm_val hy_ret;\n")
            sb.append("    if (hy_d2c_run_vm(env, $methodIdx, NULL, 0, &hy_ret) != 0) {\n")
            sb.append("        return ${vmDefaultRet(ir.returnType)};\n")
            sb.append("    }\n")
        }
        if (ir.returnType == "V") {
            sb.append("    return;\n")
        } else {
            val t = ir.returnType
            val field = if (t == "F" || t == "D") "d" else if (t.startsWith("L") || t.startsWith("[")) "o" else "i"
            sb.append("    return ${Emitters.convertOut(t, "hy_ret.$field")};\n")
        }
        sb.append("}\n")
        return sb.toString()
    }

    private fun vmDefaultRet(t: String): String = when {
        t == "V" -> ""
        t == "J" -> "0LL"
        t == "F" -> "0.0f"
        t == "D" -> "0.0"
        t.startsWith("L") || t.startsWith("[") -> "NULL"
        else -> "0"
    }

    private fun buildGenD2c(body: String, regs: List<PayloadBuilder.NativeReg>): String {
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        sb.append("/* Generated by Hyapk. Do not edit. */\n")
        sb.append("#include <jni.h>\n")
        sb.append("#include <string.h>\n")
        sb.append("#include <stdint.h>\n")
        sb.append("#include \"jni/hy_d2c_api.h\"\n")
        sb.append("#include \"jni/hy_bridge_jni.h\"\n\n")
        sb.append(body)
        sb.append("\nconst hy_native_reg hyapk_native_registrations[] = {\n")
        for (r in regs) {
            sb.append("    {\"${r.className}\", \"${r.methodName}\", \"${r.signature}\", ${r.fnName}},\n")
        }
        sb.append("};\n")
        sb.append("const unsigned int hyapk_native_reg_count = ${regs.size};\n")
        return sb.toString()
    }

    /** Assembles the final generated file; called by the native build pass. */
    fun assembleGenD2c(body: String, regs: List<PayloadBuilder.NativeReg>): String = buildGenD2c(body, regs)
}

```

`hyapk-cli/src/main/kotlin/com/blcyber/hyapk/passes/NativeBuild.kt`:

```kt
package com.blcyber.hyapk.passes

import com.blcyber.hyapk.Log
import com.blcyber.hyapk.crypto.Crypto
import com.blcyber.hyapk.findNdk
import com.blcyber.hyapk.findRuntimeSrc
import java.io.File
import java.util.Base64

/**
 * Native build pass: assembles the final payload, generates the C sources and
 * builds libhyapkrt.so for every configured ABI with the NDK.
 */
fun nativeBuildPass(ctx: PipelineContext) {
    // dex hashes (from the final dex bytes)
    for ((name, data) in ctx.dexOutput) {
        ctx.payload.dexHashes[name] = Crypto.sha256(data.first)
    }

    // signing material: the certificate digest is embedded in the payload
    val material = com.blcyber.hyapk.signing.Signer.prepare(ctx.opts)
    ctx.payload.certDigest = material.certSha256()
    ctx.signingMaterial = material

    ctx.payload.setLibSecret(ctx.keys.libSecret)
    val (blob, _) = ctx.payload.buildEncrypted(ctx.random)

    val ndk = findNdk(ctx.opts.ndkPath)
        ?: Log.die("NDK not found (pass --ndk or set ANDROID_NDK_HOME)")
    val runtimeSrc = findRuntimeSrc(ctx.opts.runtimeSrc)
        ?: Log.die("hyapk-native sources not found (pass --runtime-src or set HYAPK_RUNTIME_SRC)")
    Log.info("native runtime sources: $runtimeSrc")

    // generated files
    val genDir = File(ctx.genDir())
    genDir.mkdirs()
    writeGenPayload(genDir, blob, ctx.keys.libSecret)
    val d2cBody = ctx.generatedCBody
    if (d2cBody != null) {
        val genD2c = Conversion.assembleGenD2c(d2cBody, ctx.payload.nativeRegs)
        File(genDir, "hyapk_gen_d2c.c").writeText(genD2c)
    }

    val toolchain = File(ndk, "build/cmake/android.toolchain.cmake")
    if (!toolchain.isFile) Log.die("NDK toolchain not found under $ndk")

    for (abi in ctx.opts.abis) {
        val buildDir = File(ctx.genDir(), "build-$abi")
        buildDir.mkdirs()
        val cmakeCmd = listOf(
            "cmake",
            "-G", "Ninja",
            "-S", runtimeSrc.absolutePath,
            "-B", buildDir.absolutePath,
            "-DCMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE=${toolchain.absolutePath}",
            "-DANDROID_ABI=$abi",
            "-DANDROID_PLATFORM=android-${ctx.opts.minApi}",
            "-DHYAPK_GEN_DIR=${genDir.absolutePath}"
        )
        runCommand(cmakeCmd, "cmake configure ($abi)")
        runCommand(listOf("cmake", "--build", buildDir.absolutePath, "--target", "hyapkrt"), "cmake build ($abi)")

        val so = File(buildDir, "libhyapkrt.so")
        if (!so.isFile) Log.die("build produced no libhyapkrt.so for $abi")
        ctx.source.put("lib/$abi/libhyapkrt.so", so.readBytes(), java.util.zip.ZipEntry.STORED, 16384)
        ctx.report.log("native", "built libhyapkrt.so for $abi (${so.length()} bytes)")
    }
}

private fun writeGenPayload(genDir: File, blob: ByteArray, libSecret: ByteArray) {
    val sb = StringBuilder()
    sb.append("/* Generated by Hyapk. Do not edit. */\n")
    sb.append("#include <stdint.h>\n")
    sb.append("#include \"jni/hy_bridge_jni.h\"\n\n")
    sb.append("const unsigned char hyapk_payload_blob[] = {\n")
    blob.forEachIndexed { i, b ->
        if (i % 20 == 0) sb.append("    ")
        sb.append(b.toInt() and 0xff).append(',')
        if (i % 20 == 19) sb.append('\n')
    }
    sb.append("\n};\n")
    sb.append("const unsigned int hyapk_payload_blob_size = ${blob.size};\n")
    // lib_secret stored XOR-masked; the mask is (0xA5 + i)
    // Weak stubs: overridden by hyapk_gen_d2c.c when methods were converted.
    sb.append("const hy_native_reg hyapk_native_registrations[] __attribute__((weak)) = {{0, 0, 0, 0}};\n")
    sb.append("const unsigned int hyapk_native_reg_count __attribute__((weak)) = 0;\n")
    sb.append("\n")
    sb.append("const unsigned char hyapk_lib_secret[32] = {\n    ")
    for (i in 0 until 32) {
        sb.append(((libSecret[i].toInt() and 0xff) xor ((0xA5 + i) and 0xff))).append(',')
    }
    sb.append("\n};\n")
    File(genDir, "hyapk_gen.c").writeText(sb.toString())
}

private fun runCommand(cmd: List<String>, what: String) {
    Log.debug("running: ${cmd.joinToString(" ")}")
    try {
        val pb = ProcessBuilder(cmd).redirectErrorStream(true)
        val proc = pb.start()
        val output = proc.inputStream.readBytes().decodeToString()
        val code = proc.waitFor()
        if (code != 0) {
            Log.error("$what failed:\n$output")
            Log.die("$what failed with exit code $code")
        }
        if (Log.verbose) {
            println(output)
        }
    } catch (e: java.io.IOException) {
        Log.die("$what could not start (${e.message}); is cmake/ninja installed and in PATH?")
    }
}

```

`hyapk-cli/src/main/kotlin/com/blcyber/hyapk/passes/Passes.kt`:

```kt
package com.blcyber.hyapk.passes

import com.blcyber.hyapk.Log
import com.blcyber.hyapk.ProtectOptions
import com.blcyber.hyapk.apk.ApkInfo
import com.blcyber.hyapk.apk.Axml
import com.blcyber.hyapk.apk.ZipSource
import com.blcyber.hyapk.crypto.Crypto
import com.blcyber.hyapk.crypto.DerivedKeys
import com.blcyber.hyapk.dex.DexStore
import com.blcyber.hyapk.dex.secStringRef
import org.jf.dexlib2.AccessFlags
import org.jf.dexlib2.Opcode
import org.jf.dexlib2.Opcodes
import org.jf.dexlib2.builder.MutableMethodImplementation
import org.jf.dexlib2.builder.instruction.BuilderInstruction11x
import org.jf.dexlib2.builder.instruction.BuilderInstruction21c
import org.jf.dexlib2.builder.instruction.BuilderInstruction31i
import org.jf.dexlib2.builder.instruction.BuilderInstruction35c
import org.jf.dexlib2.builder.instruction.BuilderInstruction3rc
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.ClassDef
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.Method
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.instruction.Instruction
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.instruction.ReferenceInstruction
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.reference.FieldReference
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.reference.MethodReference
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.reference.StringReference
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.reference.TypeReference
import org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.ImmutableClassDef
import org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.ImmutableMethod
import org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.reference.ImmutableFieldReference
import org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.reference.ImmutableMethodReference
import org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.ImmutableMethodParameter
import org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.reference.ImmutableStringReference
import org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.reference.ImmutableTypeReference
import java.io.File
import java.security.SecureRandom

/** Shared state across passes. */
class PipelineContext(
    val opts: ProtectOptions,
    val source: ZipSource,
    val apkInfo: ApkInfo,
    val dexStore: DexStore,
    val random: SecureRandom,
    val keys: DerivedKeys,
    val payload: PayloadBuilder = PayloadBuilder(),
    val report: Report = Report()
) {
    /** dex entry name -> (bytes, compress) written during finalize. */
    val dexOutput = linkedMapOf<String, Pair<ByteArray, Boolean>>()
    var generatedCBody: String? = null
    var signingMaterial: com.blcyber.hyapk.signing.KeyMaterial? = null
    private var workdirCount = 0

    fun putDex(name: String, data: ByteArray, compress: Boolean) {
        dexOutput[name] = Pair(data, compress)
        source.put(name, data, if (compress) java.util.zip.ZipEntry.DEFLATED else java.util.zip.ZipEntry.STORED, 4)
    }

    fun genDir(): String {
        val base = opts.workdir ?: File(System.getProperty("java.io.tmpdir"), "hyapk-work")
        return File(base, "native-gen").absolutePath
    }
}

fun runPipeline(ctx: PipelineContext) {
    val o = ctx.opts

    if (o.hasPass("hardening")) hardeningPass(ctx)
    if (o.hasPass("bootstrap")) bootstrapPass(ctx)
    if (o.hasPass("assets")) assetsPass(ctx)
    if (o.hasPass("strings")) stringsPass(ctx)
    // conversion must run before rename so user patterns match original names
    if (o.hasPass("dex2c") || o.hasPass("vmp") ||
        o.dex2cPatterns.isNotEmpty() || o.vmpPatterns.isNotEmpty()
    ) conversionPass(ctx)
    if (o.hasPass("rename")) renamePass(ctx)

    if (o.hasPass("protect")) protectPass(ctx)

    // Final dex write.
    ctx.dexStore.writeAll({ n, d, c -> ctx.putDex(n, d, c) }, o.compressDex)

    // Native build (payload + .so) when any native-dependent pass ran.
    if (nativeNeeded(o)) {
        nativeBuildPass(ctx)
    }
}

private fun nativeNeeded(o: ProtectOptions): Boolean =
    o.hasPass("protect") || o.hasPass("strings") || o.hasPass("assets") ||
        o.hasPass("dex2c") || o.hasPass("vmp") ||
        o.dex2cPatterns.isNotEmpty() || o.vmpPatterns.isNotEmpty()

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* hardening                                                           */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

fun hardeningPass(ctx: PipelineContext) {
    val manifest = ctx.source["AndroidManifest.xml"] ?: return
    var changed = false
    if (Axml.flipBoolean(manifest.data, "application", "debuggable", false)) {
        changed = true
        ctx.report.log("hardening", "android:debuggable set to false")
    }
    if (Axml.flipBoolean(manifest.data, "application", "allowBackup", false)) {
        changed = true
        ctx.report.log("hardening", "android:allowBackup set to false")
    }
    if (ctx.apkInfo.hasDebuggable) {
        ctx.report.log("hardening", "manifest had debuggable=true; flipped")
    }
    if (changed) {
        ctx.source.put("AndroidManifest.xml", manifest.data, manifest.method, manifest.align)
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* bootstrap                                                           */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

fun bootstrapPass(ctx: PipelineContext) {
    val slot = ctx.dexStore.slots.first()
    for (cls in RuntimeInjector.buildRuntimeClasses()) {
        ctx.dexStore.addClass(slot, cls)
    }
    ctx.report.log("bootstrap", "injected Sec, HyapkBridge and Assets runtime classes")

    val targets = mutableSetOf<String>()
    ctx.apkInfo.applicationClass?.let { targets.add(it) }
    targets.addAll(ctx.apkInfo.launcherActivities)

    for (type in targets) {
        val found = ctx.dexStore.findClass(type) ?: continue
        val (s, cls) = found
        var updated = RuntimeInjector.injectClinit(cls)
        if (type == ctx.apkInfo.applicationClass) {
            updated = RuntimeInjector.injectAttachBaseContext(updated)
        }
        ctx.dexStore.replaceClass(s, cls, updated)
        ctx.report.log("bootstrap", "bootstrap injected into $type")
    }
    if (targets.isEmpty()) {
```
