```kt
        Log.warn("no application or launcher activity found; runtime not bootstrapped")
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* strings                                                             */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

fun stringsPass(ctx: PipelineContext) {
    var encrypted = 0
    val slotUpdates = mutableListOf<Triple<DexStore.Slot, ClassDef, ClassDef>>()

    for ((slot, cls) in ctx.dexStore.allClasses().toList()) {
        if (cls.type.startsWith(RuntimeInjector.SEC_CLASS) ||
            cls.type.startsWith(RuntimeInjector.BRIDGE_CLASS) ||
            cls.type.startsWith(RuntimeInjector.ASSETS_CLASS)
        ) continue

        var classModified = false
        val newDirect = cls.directMethods.map { m ->
            val r = rewriteStringsInMethod(ctx, m)
            if (r != null) { classModified = true; encrypted += r.second; r.first } else m
        }
        val newVirtual = cls.virtualMethods.map { m ->
            val r = rewriteStringsInMethod(ctx, m)
            if (r != null) { classModified = true; encrypted += r.second; r.first } else m
        }
        if (classModified) {
            val updated = ImmutableClassDef(
                cls.type, cls.accessFlags, cls.superclass, cls.interfaces, cls.sourceFile, cls.annotations,
                cls.staticFields, cls.instanceFields, newDirect, newVirtual
            )
            slotUpdates.add(Triple(slot, cls, updated))
        }
    }
    for ((slot, old, new) in slotUpdates) {
        ctx.dexStore.replaceClass(slot, old, new)
    }
    ctx.report.count("strings encrypted", encrypted)
    Log.info("string encryption: $encrypted constants moved to the native payload")
}

/** Returns the new method plus the number of strings encrypted. */
private fun rewriteStringsInMethod(ctx: PipelineContext, m: Method): Pair<Method, Int>? {
    val impl = m.implementation ?: return null
    if (m.name == "<clinit>" && m.definingClass == ctx.apkInfo.applicationClass) {
        // the bootstrap loader runs before Sec is usable; still safe to encrypt
        // because loadLibrary precedes any Sec.s call we inserted ourselves.
    }
    val mutable = MutableMethodImplementation(impl)
    var count = 0
    var i = 0
    while (i < mutable.instructions.size) {
        val ins = mutable.instructions[i]
        val op = ins.opcode
        if (op == Opcode.CONST_STRING || op == Opcode.CONST_STRING_JUMBO) {
            val reg = (ins as org.jf.dexlib2.iface.instruction.formats.Instruction21c).registerA
            val str = (ins.reference as StringReference).string
            if (str == RuntimeInjector.LIB_NAME) { i++; continue } // bootstrap loads before Sec exists
            val id = ctx.payload.internString(str)
            // const reg, id  (31i)
            mutable.replaceInstruction(i, BuilderInstruction31i(Opcode.CONST, reg, id))
            // invoke-static Sec.s(I)  using the same register
            mutable.addInstruction(i + 1, RuntimeInjector.invokeStatic1(reg, secStringRef()))
            // move-result-object reg
            mutable.addInstruction(i + 2, BuilderInstruction11x(Opcode.MOVE_RESULT_OBJECT, reg))
            i += 3
            count++
        } else {
            i++
        }
    }
    if (count == 0) return null
    val newImpl = org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.ImmutableMethodImplementation(
        mutable.registerCount, mutable.instructions, mutable.tryBlocks, mutable.debugItems
    )
    return ImmutableMethod(
        m.definingClass, m.name, m.parameters, m.returnType, m.accessFlags,
        m.annotations, m.hiddenApiRestrictions, newImpl
    ) to count
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* assets                                                              */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

fun assetsPass(ctx: PipelineContext) {
    val assetNames = ctx.source.names().filter { it.startsWith("assets/") && !it.endsWith("/") }
    if (assetNames.isEmpty()) {
        ctx.report.log("assets", "no assets found")
        return
    }
    var encryptedCount = 0
    for (name in assetNames) {
        val entry = ctx.source[name] ?: continue
        if (entry.data.isEmpty()) continue
        val asset = ctx.payload.addAsset(ctx, name.removePrefix("assets/"), entry.data, ctx.random)
        ctx.source.put("assets/${asset.stored}", asset.encrypted, java.util.zip.ZipEntry.STORED, 4)
        ctx.source.remove(name)
        encryptedCount++
    }
    ctx.report.count("assets encrypted", encryptedCount)

    // rewrite AssetManager.open call sites
    var rewritten = 0
    val updates = mutableListOf<Triple<DexStore.Slot, ClassDef, ClassDef>>()
    for ((slot, cls) in ctx.dexStore.allClasses().toList()) {
        var modified = false
        val newDirect = cls.directMethods.map { m ->
            val r = rewriteAssetCalls(ctx, m)
            if (r != null) { modified = true; rewritten += r.second; r.first } else m
        }
        val newVirtual = cls.virtualMethods.map { m ->
            val r = rewriteAssetCalls(ctx, m)
            if (r != null) { modified = true; rewritten += r.second; r.first } else m
        }
        if (modified) {
            updates.add(Triple(slot, cls, ImmutableClassDef(
                cls.type, cls.accessFlags, cls.superclass, cls.interfaces, cls.sourceFile, null,
                cls.staticFields, cls.instanceFields, newDirect, newVirtual
            )))
        }
    }
    for ((slot, old, new) in updates) ctx.dexStore.replaceClass(slot, old, new)
    ctx.report.count("asset call sites rewritten", rewritten)
}

private fun rewriteAssetCalls(ctx: PipelineContext, m: Method): Pair<Method, Int>? {
    val impl = m.implementation ?: return null
    val mutable = MutableMethodImplementation(impl)
    var count = 0
    var i = 0
    while (i < mutable.instructions.size) {
        val ins = mutable.instructions[i]
        if (ins.opcode == Opcode.INVOKE_VIRTUAL || ins.opcode == Opcode.INVOKE_VIRTUAL_RANGE ||
            ins.opcode == Opcode.INVOKE_INTERFACE || ins.opcode == Opcode.INVOKE_INTERFACE_RANGE
        ) {
            val ref = (ins as ReferenceInstruction).reference as? MethodReference
            val assetOpen = ref != null && ref.definingClass == "Landroid/content/res/AssetManager;" &&
                ref.name == "open" &&
                ref.parameterTypes.toList() == listOf("Ljava/lang/String;") &&
                ref.returnType == "Ljava/io/InputStream;"
            if (assetOpen) {
                val isRange = ins.opcode.name.endsWith("RANGE")
                val stringReg: Int
                if (isRange) {
                    val r = ins as org.jf.dexlib2.iface.instruction.formats.Instruction3rc
                    // range layout: startRegister = receiver, string is startRegister + 1
                    stringReg = r.startRegister + 1
                } else {
                    val r = ins as org.jf.dexlib2.iface.instruction.formats.Instruction35c
                    stringReg = r.registerD
                }
                mutable.replaceInstruction(
                    i,
                    RuntimeInjector.invokeStatic1(
                        stringReg,
                        ImmutableMethodReference(
                            RuntimeInjector.ASSETS_CLASS, "open",
                            listOf("Ljava/lang/String;"), "Ljava/io/InputStream;"
                        )
                    )
                )
                count++
            }
        }
        i++
    }
    if (count == 0) return null
    val newImpl = org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.ImmutableMethodImplementation(
        mutable.registerCount, mutable.instructions, mutable.tryBlocks, mutable.debugItems
    )
    return ImmutableMethod(
        m.definingClass, m.name, m.parameters, m.returnType, m.accessFlags,
        m.annotations, m.hiddenApiRestrictions, newImpl
    ) to count
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* rename (smali class obfuscation)                                    */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

private val DEFAULT_KEEP_PREFIXES = listOf(
    "Landroid/", "Ljava/", "Landroidx/", "Lkotlin/", "Lkotlinx/",
    "Lcom/google/android/", "Lorg/apache/", "Lokhttp3/", "Lokio/",
    "Lretrofit2/", "Lcom/squareup/", "Lorg/json/", "Lio/reactivex/",
    "Lhyapk/", "Lcom/hyapk/"
)

private fun defaultKeep(ctx: PipelineContext): MutableSet<String> {
    val keep = mutableSetOf<String>()
    ctx.apkInfo.applicationClass?.let { keep.add(it) }
    keep.addAll(ctx.apkInfo.launcherActivities)
    keep.add(RuntimeInjector.SEC_CLASS)
    keep.add(RuntimeInjector.BRIDGE_CLASS)
    keep.add(RuntimeInjector.ASSETS_CLASS)
    return keep
}

fun renamePass(ctx: PipelineContext) {
    val keep = defaultKeep(ctx)
    val userKeeps = ctx.opts.keepPatterns

    // Collect manifest component names to keep them intact.
    val manifestKeeps = collectManifestComponents(ctx)
    keep.addAll(manifestKeeps)

    // All classes with native methods stay (JNI name binding).
    for ((_, cls) in ctx.dexStore.allClasses()) {
        val hasNative = (cls.directMethods.asSequence() + cls.virtualMethods.asSequence())
            .any { (it.accessFlags and AccessFlags.NATIVE.value) != 0 }
        if (hasNative) keep.add(cls.type)
    }

    fun typeKept(t: String): Boolean {
        if (keep.contains(t)) return true
        if (DEFAULT_KEEP_PREFIXES.any { t.startsWith(it) }) return true
        val dotted = t.removePrefix("L").removeSuffix(";").replace('/', '.')
        return userKeeps.any { p -> matchPattern(p, dotted) }
    }

    // assign new names
    val map = HashMap<String, String>()
    var counter = 0
    for ((_, cls) in ctx.dexStore.allClasses()) {
        if (!typeKept(cls.type)) {
            map[cls.type] = "Lhyapk/n${counter++};"
        }
    }
    if (map.isEmpty()) {
        ctx.report.log("rename", "no classes selected for renaming")
        return
    }

    fun mapType(t: String): String {
        // handles "Lx/y;" and "[Lx/y;" and "[[..."
        var arrays = 0
        var s = t
        while (s.startsWith("[")) {
            arrays++
            s = s.substring(1)
        }
        val mapped = map[s] ?: s
        return "[".repeat(arrays) + mapped
    }

    fun mapProto(p: String): String {
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        var i = 0
        val n = p.length
        while (i < n) {
            val c = p[i]
            if (c == 'L') {
                val end = p.indexOf(';', i)
                sb.append(mapType(p.substring(i, end + 1)))
                i = end + 1
            } else {
                sb.append(c)
                i++
            }
        }
        return sb.toString()
    }

    fun mapRef(r: org.jf.dexlib2.iface.reference.Reference): org.jf.dexlib2.iface.reference.Reference {
        return when (r) {
            is TypeReference -> ImmutableTypeReference(mapType(r.type))
            is StringReference -> r
            is MethodReference -> {
                val mc = mapType(r.definingClass)
                ImmutableMethodReference(mc, r.name, r.parameterTypes.map { mapType(it.toString()) }, mapType(r.returnType))
            }
            is FieldReference -> ImmutableFieldReference(mapType(r.definingClass), r.name, mapType(r.type))
            else -> r
        }
    }

    // apply to every class
    for ((slot, cls) in ctx.dexStore.allClasses().toList()) {
        val newType = map[cls.type] ?: cls.type
        val newSuper = cls.superclass?.let { mapType(it) }
        val newInterfaces = cls.interfaces.map { mapType(it) }
        val newStatic = cls.staticFields.map { f ->
            org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.ImmutableField(mapType(f.definingClass), f.name, mapType(f.type), f.accessFlags,
                f.initialValue?.let { org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.value.ImmutableEncodedValueFactory.of(it) },
                f.annotations, f.hiddenApiRestrictions)
        }
        val newInstance = cls.instanceFields.map { f ->
            org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.ImmutableField(mapType(f.definingClass), f.name, mapType(f.type), f.accessFlags,
                f.initialValue?.let { org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.value.ImmutableEncodedValueFactory.of(it) },
                f.annotations, f.hiddenApiRestrictions)
        }

        fun convert(m: Method): Method {
            val impl = m.implementation ?: return ImmutableMethod(
                newType, m.name,
                m.parameters.map { ImmutableMethodParameter(mapType(it.type), it.annotations, it.name) },
                mapType(m.returnType),
                m.accessFlags, m.annotations, m.hiddenApiRestrictions, null
            )
            val newInstrs = impl.instructions.map { ins ->
                if (ins is ReferenceInstruction) {
                    mapInstruction(ins, ::mapRef)
                } else {
                    ins
                }
            }
            val newImpl = org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.ImmutableMethodImplementation(
                impl.registerCount, newInstrs, impl.tryBlocks, impl.debugItems
            )
            return ImmutableMethod(
                newType, m.name,
                m.parameters.map { ImmutableMethodParameter(mapType(it.type), it.annotations, it.name) },
                mapType(m.returnType),
                m.accessFlags, m.annotations, m.hiddenApiRestrictions, newImpl
            )
        }

        val newDirect = cls.directMethods.map { convert(it) }
        val newVirtual = cls.virtualMethods.map { convert(it) }
        val updated = ImmutableClassDef(
            newType, cls.accessFlags, newSuper, newInterfaces, cls.sourceFile, cls.annotations,
            newStatic, newInstance, newDirect, newVirtual
        )
        ctx.dexStore.replaceClass(slot, cls, updated)
        ctx.report.mapping(cls.type, newType)
    }
    ctx.report.count("classes renamed", map.size)
    Log.info("renamed ${map.size} classes")
}

/** Rebuilds an instruction with a mapped reference, preserving its format. */
private fun mapInstruction(
    ins: Instruction,
    mapper: (org.jf.dexlib2.iface.reference.Reference) -> org.jf.dexlib2.iface.reference.Reference
): Instruction {
    val ref = (ins as ReferenceInstruction).reference
    val newRef = mapper(ref)
    if (newRef === ref) return ins
    return when (ins) {
        is org.jf.dexlib2.iface.instruction.formats.Instruction21c ->
            org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.instruction.ImmutableInstruction21c(ins.opcode, ins.registerA, newRef)
        is org.jf.dexlib2.iface.instruction.formats.Instruction22c ->
            org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.instruction.ImmutableInstruction22c(ins.opcode, ins.registerA, ins.registerB, newRef)
        is org.jf.dexlib2.iface.instruction.formats.Instruction31c ->
            org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.instruction.ImmutableInstruction31c(ins.opcode, ins.registerA, newRef as StringReference)
        is org.jf.dexlib2.iface.instruction.formats.Instruction35c ->
            org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.instruction.ImmutableInstruction35c(
                ins.opcode, ins.registerCount,
                ins.registerC, ins.registerD, ins.registerE, ins.registerF, ins.registerG, newRef
            )
        is org.jf.dexlib2.iface.instruction.formats.Instruction3rc ->
            org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.instruction.ImmutableInstruction3rc(ins.opcode, ins.registerCount, ins.startRegister, newRef)
        else -> ins /* 45cc/4rcc and other rare formats are left untouched */
    }
}

private fun collectManifestComponents(ctx: PipelineContext): Set<String> {
    val manifest = ctx.source["AndroidManifest.xml"]?.data ?: return emptySet()
    val doc = Axml.parse(manifest)
    val out = mutableSetOf<String>()
    for (ev in doc.events) {
        if (ev.isEnd) continue
        val node = ev.node ?: continue
        val nameAttr = node.attrs.firstOrNull {
            it.name == "name" && it.ns?.contains("res/android") == true
        }?.rawValue ?: continue
        when (node.name) {
            "activity", "service", "receiver", "provider", "application", "activity-alias" -> {
                out.add(ApkInfo.toDescriptor(nameAttr))
            }
        }
    }
    return out
}

/** Supports: com.example.Calc, com.example.Calc#member, com.example.** */
fun matchPattern(pattern: String, dottedClass: String, member: String? = null): Boolean {
    val parts = pattern.split("#", limit = 2)
    val classPat = parts[0]
    val memberPat = parts.getOrNull(1)
    val clsOk = when {
        classPat.endsWith(".**") -> dottedClass.startsWith(classPat.removeSuffix(".**") + ".") ||
            dottedClass == classPat.removeSuffix(".**")
        classPat.endsWith(".*") -> dottedClass.startsWith(classPat.removeSuffix(".*") + ".")
        else -> dottedClass == classPat
    }
    if (!clsOk) return false
    if (memberPat == null) return true
    if (memberPat == "*") return true
    return member == memberPat
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* protect (runtime checks toggle via payload config)                  */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

fun protectPass(ctx: PipelineContext) {
    ctx.payload.setConfig(
        skipRoot = ctx.opts.skipRootCheck,
        skipEmulator = ctx.opts.skipEmulatorCheck
    )
    ctx.report.log("protect", "anti-debug, anti-frida, anti-root, signature and tamper checks enabled")
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* conversion entry (dex2c + vmp) lives in Conversion.kt               */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

fun conversionPass(ctx: PipelineContext) {
    Conversion.run(ctx)
}

```

`hyapk-cli/src/main/kotlin/com/blcyber/hyapk/passes/PayloadBuilder.kt`:

```kt
package com.blcyber.hyapk.passes

import com.blcyber.hyapk.Log
import com.blcyber.hyapk.apk.ZipSource
import com.blcyber.hyapk.crypto.Crypto
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream
import java.io.File
import java.security.SecureRandom

/**
 * Assembles the encrypted payload blob and the generated C sources consumed
 * by the native build. The format matches hyapk-native/src/jni/hy_payload.h.
 */
class PayloadBuilder {
    /** interned strings: content -> id */
    private val strings = ArrayList<String>(4096)
    private val stringIds = HashMap<String, Int>(4096)

    /** asset entries: original name -> (stored name, seed, nonce, tag, size) */
    class AssetEntry(
        val original: String, val stored: String, val seed: ByteArray,
        val nonce: ByteArray, val tag: ByteArray, val encrypted: ByteArray
    )

    val assets = ArrayList<AssetEntry>()

    /** dex hashes: entry name -> sha256 */
    val dexHashes = LinkedHashMap<String, ByteArray>()

    var certDigest: ByteArray? = null

    /** vm programs: method idx (in nativeRegistrations) -> program bytes */
    val vmPrograms = LinkedHashMap<Int, ByteArray>()

    /** native registrations: class (dotted), name, signature, function */
    class NativeReg(val className: String, val methodName: String, val signature: String, val fnName: String)

    val nativeRegs = ArrayList<NativeReg>()

    /** shared method/type/field tables for the VM bridge and dex2c */
    private val methodTable = ArrayList<MethodTableEntry>()
    private val methodIds = HashMap<String, Int>()
    private val typeTable = ArrayList<String>()
    private val typeIds = HashMap<String, Int>()
    private val fieldTable = ArrayList<FieldTableEntry>()
    private val fieldIds = HashMap<String, Int>()

    class MethodTableEntry(val classDesc: String, val name: String, val sig: String, val kind: Int, val argc: Int, val ret: Char)
    class FieldTableEntry(val classDesc: String, val name: String, val typeChar: Char)

    var skipRoot = false
    var skipEmulator = false
    var guardInterval = 12

    fun setConfig(skipRoot: Boolean, skipEmulator: Boolean) {
        this.skipRoot = skipRoot
        this.skipEmulator = skipEmulator
    }

    /* ---------------- string interning ---------------- */

    fun internString(s: String): Int {
        val existing = stringIds[s]
        if (existing != null) return existing
        val id = strings.size
        strings.add(s)
        stringIds[s] = id
        return id
    }

    fun stringCount(): Int = strings.size

    /* ---------------- type/method tables ---------------- */

    fun internType(desc: String): Int {
        val existing = typeIds[desc]
        if (existing != null) return existing
        val id = typeTable.size
        typeTable.add(desc)
        typeIds[desc] = id
        return id
    }

    /** Registers a method invocation target for the VM/dex2c bridge. */
    fun internMethod(classDesc: String, name: String, sig: String, kind: Int): Int {
        val key = "$classDesc|$name|$sig|$kind"
        val existing = methodIds[key]
        if (existing != null) return existing
        val ret = sig.substringAfter(')').firstOrNull() ?: 'V'
        val entry = MethodTableEntry(classDesc, name, sig, kind, countParams(sig), ret)
        val id = methodTable.size
        methodTable.add(entry)
        methodIds[key] = id
        return id
    }

    private fun countParams(sig: String): Int {
        var n = 0
        var i = 0
        while (i < sig.length) {
            when (sig[i]) {
                '(' -> {}
                ')' -> return n
                'L' -> { n++; i = sig.indexOf(';', i); if (i < 0) return n }
                '[' -> { /* array prefix stays with the next element */ }
                else -> n++
            }
            i++
        }
        return n
    }

    fun internField(classDesc: String, name: String, typeChar: Char): Int {
        val key = "$classDesc|$name|$typeChar"
        val existing = fieldIds[key]
        if (existing != null) return existing
        val id = fieldTable.size
        fieldTable.add(FieldTableEntry(classDesc, name, typeChar))
        fieldIds[key] = id
        return id
    }

    /* ---------------- assets ---------------- */

    fun addAsset(ctx: PipelineContext, original: String, data: ByteArray, random: SecureRandom): AssetEntry {
        val stored = Crypto.sha256(original.toByteArray()).take(8)
            .joinToString("") { String.format("%02x", it) }
        val seed = ByteArray(32)
        random.nextBytes(seed)
        val nonce = ByteArray(12)
        random.nextBytes(nonce)
        val key = Crypto.derivePayloadKey(ctx.keys.libSecret, seed)
        val aead = Crypto.aeadEncrypt(key, nonce, ByteArray(0), data)
        val entry = AssetEntry(original, stored, seed, nonce, aead.tag, aead.ciphertext + aead.tag)
        assets.add(entry)
        internString(original)
        return entry
    }

    /* ---------------- serialization ---------------- */

    private fun le16(v: Int) = byteArrayOf(v.toByte(), (v shr 8).toByte())
    private fun le32(v: Int) = byteArrayOf(
        v.toByte(), (v shr 8).toByte(), (v shr 16).toByte(), (v shr 24).toByte()
    )

    private fun section(id: Int, body: ByteArray): ByteArray {
        val out = ByteArrayOutputStream()
        out.write(le16(id))
        out.write(le32(0)) // placeholder offset
        out.write(le32(body.size))
        out.write(body)
        return out.toByteArray()
    }

    fun buildPlaintext(): ByteArray {
        val sections = mutableListOf<Pair<Int, ByteArray>>()

        // STRINGS
        run {
            val b = ByteArrayOutputStream()
            b.write(le32(strings.size))
            for (s in strings) {
                val bytes = s.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)
                b.write(le32(bytes.size))
                b.write(bytes)
            }
            sections.add(HY_SEC_STRINGS to b.toByteArray())
        }

        // ASSETMAP: orig_idx u32 | stored[16] | seed[32] | nonce[12] | tag[16] | size u32
        if (assets.isNotEmpty()) {
            val b = ByteArrayOutputStream()
            b.write(le32(assets.size))
            for (a in assets) {
                b.write(le32(internString(a.original)))
                val storedBytes = a.stored.toByteArray()
                require(storedBytes.size == 16) { "stored name must be 16 bytes" }
                b.write(storedBytes)
                b.write(a.seed)
                b.write(a.nonce)
                b.write(a.tag)
                b.write(le32(a.encrypted.size))
            }
            sections.add(HY_SEC_ASSETMAP to b.toByteArray())
        }

        // DEXHASH: name_idx u32 | sha256[32]
        if (dexHashes.isNotEmpty()) {
            val b = ByteArrayOutputStream()
            b.write(le32(dexHashes.size))
            for ((name, hash) in dexHashes) {
                b.write(le32(internString(name)))
                b.write(hash)
            }
            sections.add(HY_SEC_DEXHASH to b.toByteArray())
        }

        // CERTDIGEST
        certDigest?.let {
            sections.add(HY_SEC_CERTDIGEST to it)
        }

        // VMPROG: method_idx u32 | len u32 | bytes
        if (vmPrograms.isNotEmpty()) {
            val b = ByteArrayOutputStream()
            b.write(le32(vmPrograms.size))
            for ((mid, prog) in vmPrograms) {
                b.write(le32(mid))
                b.write(le32(prog.size))
                b.write(prog)
            }
            sections.add(HY_SEC_VMPROG to b.toByteArray())
        }

        // METHODS: class_idx u32 | name_idx u32 | sig_idx u32 | kind u8 | argc u8 | ret u8
        if (methodTable.isNotEmpty()) {
            val b = ByteArrayOutputStream()
            b.write(le32(methodTable.size))
            for (m in methodTable) {
                b.write(le32(internType(m.classDesc)))
                b.write(le32(internString(m.name)))
                b.write(le32(internString(m.sig)))
                b.write(m.kind)
                b.write(m.argc)
                b.write(m.ret.code)
            }
            sections.add(HY_SEC_METHODS to b.toByteArray())
        }

        // TYPES: len u32 + descriptor
        if (typeTable.isNotEmpty()) {
            val b = ByteArrayOutputStream()
            b.write(le32(typeTable.size))
            for (t in typeTable) {
                val bytes = t.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)
                b.write(le32(bytes.size))
                b.write(bytes)
            }
            sections.add(HY_SEC_TYPES to b.toByteArray())
        }

        // FIELDS: class_idx u32 | name_idx u32 | type_char u8
        if (fieldTable.isNotEmpty()) {
            val b = ByteArrayOutputStream()
            b.write(le32(fieldTable.size))
            for (f in fieldTable) {
                b.write(le32(internType(f.classDesc)))
                b.write(le32(internString(f.name)))
                b.write(f.typeChar.code)
            }
            sections.add(HY_SEC_FIELDS to b.toByteArray())
        }

        // CONFIG: skip_root u8 | skip_emu u8 | pad u16 | interval u32
        run {
            val b = ByteArrayOutputStream()
            b.write(if (skipRoot) 1 else 0)
            b.write(if (skipEmulator) 1 else 0)
            b.write(le16(0))
            b.write(le32(guardInterval))
            sections.add(HY_SEC_CONFIG to b.toByteArray())
        }

        // assemble
        val dirSize = 2 + sections.size * 10
        val b = ByteArrayOutputStream()
        b.write(le16(sections.size))
        val dir = ByteArray(dirSize - 2)
        val bodies = ArrayList<ByteArray>(sections.size)
        var offset = dirSize
        sections.forEachIndexed { i, (id, body) ->
            val d = i * 10
            dir[d] = (id and 0xff).toByte()
            dir[d + 1] = ((id shr 8) and 0xff).toByte()
            val offB = le32(offset)
            System.arraycopy(offB, 0, dir, d + 2, 4)
            val lenB = le32(body.size)
            System.arraycopy(lenB, 0, dir, d + 6, 4)
            offset += body.size
            bodies.add(body)
        }
        b.write(dir)
        for (body in bodies) b.write(body)
        return b.toByteArray()
    }

    fun buildEncrypted(random: SecureRandom): Pair<ByteArray, ByteArray> {
        val plain = buildPlaintext()
        val fileSalt = ByteArray(32)
        random.nextBytes(fileSalt)
        val nonce = ByteArray(12)
        random.nextBytes(nonce)
        val key = Crypto.derivePayloadKey(libSecretForBuild, fileSalt)
        // AAD = first 12 header bytes (magic+version+flags+size), matching the runtime.
        val headerPrefix = byteArrayOf(0x48, 0x50, 0x41, 0x59) + // 'HPAY' LE
            le16(1) + le16(0x0003) + le32(plain.size)
        val aead = Crypto.aeadEncrypt(key, nonce, headerPrefix, plain)
        // header: magic(4) version(2) flags(2) size(4) nonce(12) salt(32) tag(16)
        val header = ByteArrayOutputStream()
        header.write(headerPrefix)
        header.write(nonce)
        header.write(fileSalt)
        header.write(aead.tag)
        val blob = header.toByteArray() + aead.ciphertext
        return blob to fileSalt
    }

    /** set later by the native pass (decoupled for testability) */
    var libSecretForBuild: ByteArray = ByteArray(32)

    fun setLibSecret(secret: ByteArray) {
        require(secret.size == 32)
        libSecretForBuild = secret
    }

    companion object {
        const val HY_SEC_STRINGS = 1
        const val HY_SEC_ASSETMAP = 2
        const val HY_SEC_DEXHASH = 3
        const val HY_SEC_CERTDIGEST = 4
        const val HY_SEC_VMPROG = 5
        const val HY_SEC_METHODS = 6
        const val HY_SEC_TYPES = 7
        const val HY_SEC_FIELDS = 8
        const val HY_SEC_CONFIG = 9
    }
}

/** Human and machine readable pipeline report. */
class Report {
    private val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
    private val counts = linkedMapOf<String, Int>()
    val mapping = linkedMapOf<String, String>()

    fun log(pass: String, message: String) {
        lines.add("[$pass] $message")
    }

    fun count(what: String, n: Int) {
        counts[what] = (counts[what] ?: 0) + n
    }

    fun mapping(from: String, to: String) {
        mapping[from] = to
    }

    fun writeTo(dir: File) {
        dir.mkdirs()
        File(dir, "protection-report.txt").writeText(buildString {
            appendLine("Hyapk protection report")
            appendLine()
            for (line in lines) appendLine(line)
            appendLine()
            for ((k, v) in counts) appendLine("$k: $v")
        })
        if (mapping.isNotEmpty()) {
            File(dir, "mapping.txt").writeText(buildString {
                for ((from, to) in mapping) appendLine("$from -> $to")
            })
        }
        Log.info("report written to ${dir.path}")
    }
}
```

`hyapk-cli/src/main/kotlin/com/blcyber/hyapk/passes/RuntimeInjector.kt`:

```kt
package com.blcyber.hyapk.passes

import org.jf.dexlib2.AccessFlags
import org.jf.dexlib2.Opcode
import org.jf.dexlib2.builder.BuilderInstruction
import org.jf.dexlib2.builder.MutableMethodImplementation
import org.jf.dexlib2.builder.instruction.BuilderInstruction10x
import org.jf.dexlib2.builder.instruction.BuilderInstruction11x
import org.jf.dexlib2.builder.instruction.BuilderInstruction21c
import org.jf.dexlib2.builder.instruction.BuilderInstruction35c
import org.jf.dexlib2.builder.instruction.BuilderInstruction3rc
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.ClassDef
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.Method
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.instruction.Instruction
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.instruction.ReferenceInstruction
import org.jf.dexlib2.iface.reference.MethodReference
import org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.ImmutableClassDef
import org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.ImmutableMethod
import org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.ImmutableMethodImplementation
import org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.ImmutableMethodParameter
import org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.reference.ImmutableMethodReference
import org.jf.dexlib2.immutable.reference.ImmutableStringReference

/**
 * Builds the injected runtime classes and the bootstrap code that loads
 * libhyapkrt.so before any app code runs.
 */
object RuntimeInjector {

    const val BRIDGE_CLASS = "Lcom/hyapk/runtime/HyapkBridge;"
    const val SEC_CLASS = "Lcom/hyapk/runtime/Sec;"
    const val ASSETS_CLASS = "Lcom/hyapk/runtime/Assets;"
    const val LIB_NAME = "hyapkrt"

    fun objectCtor(clsType: String): ImmutableMethod =
        ImmutableMethod(
            clsType,
            "<init>",
            emptyList(),
            "V",
            AccessFlags.PUBLIC.value or AccessFlags.CONSTRUCTOR.value,
            null, null,
            ImmutableMethodImplementation(
                1,
                listOf(
                    BuilderInstruction35c(
                        Opcode.INVOKE_DIRECT, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0,
                        ImmutableMethodReference("Ljava/lang/Object;", "<init>", emptyList(), "V")
                    ),
                    BuilderInstruction10x(Opcode.RETURN_VOID)
                ),
                emptyList(), emptyList()
            )
        )

    /** Sec.s(I)String : native, plus constructor. */
    fun buildSec(): ImmutableClassDef = ImmutableClassDef(
        SEC_CLASS,
        AccessFlags.PUBLIC.value or AccessFlags.FINAL.value,
        "Ljava/lang/Object;",
        emptyList(), "Sec.java", null,
        emptyList(), emptyList(),
        listOf(
            objectCtor(SEC_CLASS),
            ImmutableMethod(
                SEC_CLASS, "s", emptyList(), "Ljava/lang/String;",
                AccessFlags.PUBLIC.value or AccessFlags.STATIC.value or AccessFlags.NATIVE.value,
                null, null, null
            )
        ),
        emptyList()
    )

    fun buildBridge(): ImmutableClassDef = ImmutableClassDef(
        BRIDGE_CLASS,
        AccessFlags.PUBLIC.value or AccessFlags.FINAL.value,
        "Ljava/lang/Object;",
        emptyList(), "HyapkBridge.java", null,
        emptyList(), emptyList(),
        listOf(
            objectCtor(BRIDGE_CLASS),
            ImmutableMethod(
                BRIDGE_CLASS, "initNative",
                listOf(ImmutableMethodParameter("Landroid/content/Context;", null as Set<org.jf.dexlib2.iface.Annotation>?, null)),
                "V",
                AccessFlags.PUBLIC.value or AccessFlags.STATIC.value or AccessFlags.NATIVE.value,
                null, null, null
            ),
            ImmutableMethod(
                BRIDGE_CLASS, "asset",
                listOf(ImmutableMethodParameter("Ljava/lang/String;", null as Set<org.jf.dexlib2.iface.Annotation>?, null)),
                "Ljava/io/InputStream;",
                AccessFlags.PUBLIC.value or AccessFlags.STATIC.value or AccessFlags.NATIVE.value,
                null, null, null
            )
        ),
        emptyList()
    )

    fun buildAssets(): ImmutableClassDef {
        val impl = ImmutableMethodImplementation(
            2,
            listOf(
                BuilderInstruction35c(
                    Opcode.INVOKE_STATIC, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0,
                    ImmutableMethodReference(
                        BRIDGE_CLASS, "asset",
                        listOf("Ljava/lang/String;"), "Ljava/io/InputStream;"
                    )
                ),
                BuilderInstruction11x(Opcode.MOVE_RESULT_OBJECT, 1),
                BuilderInstruction11x(Opcode.RETURN_OBJECT, 1)
            ),
            emptyList(), emptyList()
        )
        return ImmutableClassDef(
            ASSETS_CLASS,
            AccessFlags.PUBLIC.value or AccessFlags.FINAL.value,
            "Ljava/lang/Object;",
            emptyList(), "Assets.java", null,
            emptyList(), emptyList(),
            listOf(
                objectCtor(SEC_CLASS),
                ImmutableMethod(
                    ASSETS_CLASS, "open",
                    listOf(ImmutableMethodParameter("Ljava/lang/String;", null as Set<org.jf.dexlib2.iface.Annotation>?, null)),
                    "Ljava/io/InputStream;",
                    AccessFlags.PUBLIC.value or AccessFlags.STATIC.value,
                    null, null, impl
                )
            ),
            emptyList()
        )
    }

    fun buildRuntimeClasses(): List<ClassDef> = listOf(buildSec(), buildBridge(), buildAssets())

    private fun loadLibraryCall(reg: Int): List<BuilderInstruction> = listOf(
        BuilderInstruction21c(Opcode.CONST_STRING, reg, ImmutableStringReference(LIB_NAME)),
        invokeStatic1(reg, ImmutableMethodReference("Ljava/lang/System;", "loadLibrary", listOf("Ljava/lang/String;"), "V"))
    )

    fun invokeStatic1(reg: Int, ref: MethodReference): BuilderInstruction =
        if (reg < 16) {
            BuilderInstruction35c(Opcode.INVOKE_STATIC, 1, reg, 0, 0, 0, 0, ref)
        } else {
            BuilderInstruction3rc(Opcode.INVOKE_STATIC_RANGE, 1, reg, ref)
        }

    /**
     * Adds a static initializer loading libhyapkrt, or prepends the load to an
     * existing one. <clinit> has no parameter registers, so growing the
     * register count is safe.
     */
    fun injectClinit(cls: ClassDef): ClassDef {
        val clinit = cls.directMethods.firstOrNull { it.name == "<clinit>" }
        val direct = cls.directMethods.toMutableList()

        if (clinit == null) {
            val impl = MutableMethodImplementation(1)
            impl.addInstruction(0, loadLibraryCall(0)[0])
            impl.addInstruction(1, loadLibraryCall(0)[1])
            impl.addInstruction(2, BuilderInstruction10x(Opcode.RETURN_VOID))
            direct.add(
                ImmutableMethod(
                    cls.type, "<clinit>", emptyList(), "V",
                    AccessFlags.CONSTRUCTOR.value or AccessFlags.STATIC.value,
                    null, null,
                    ImmutableMethodImplementation(impl.registerCount, impl.instructions, impl.tryBlocks, impl.debugItems)
                )
            )
        } else {
            val impl = MutableMethodImplementation(clinit.implementation!!)
            impl.addInstruction(0, loadLibraryCall(0)[0])
            impl.addInstruction(1, loadLibraryCall(0)[1])
            val idx = direct.indexOfFirst { it.name == "<clinit>" }
            val regCount = if (impl.registerCount == 0) 1 else impl.registerCount
            direct[idx] = ImmutableMethod(
                cls.type, clinit.name, clinit.parameters, clinit.returnType,
                clinit.accessFlags, null, null,
                ImmutableMethodImplementation(regCount, impl.instructions, impl.tryBlocks, impl.debugItems)
            )
        }
        return ImmutableClassDef(
            cls.type, cls.accessFlags, cls.superclass, cls.interfaces, cls.sourceFile, null,
            cls.staticFields, cls.instanceFields, direct, cls.virtualMethods
        )
    }

    /**
     * Ensures attachBaseContext(Context) exists and calls
     * HyapkBridge.initNative(Context) right after the super call.
     */
    fun injectAttachBaseContext(cls: ClassDef): ClassDef {
        val existing = cls.virtualMethods.firstOrNull {
            it.name == "attachBaseContext" && it.parameterTypes.toList() == listOf("Landroid/content/Context;")
        }
        val virtual = cls.virtualMethods.toMutableList()

        if (existing == null) {
            val superDescriptor = cls.superclass ?: "Landroid/app/Application;"
            // .registers 2: p0=this(v0), p1=ctx(v1)
            val impl = MutableMethodImplementation(2)
            impl.addInstruction(0, BuilderInstruction35c(
                Opcode.INVOKE_SUPER, 2, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0,
                ImmutableMethodReference(superDescriptor, "attachBaseContext", listOf("Landroid/content/Context;"), "V")
            ))
            impl.addInstruction(1, BuilderInstruction3rc(
                Opcode.INVOKE_STATIC_RANGE, 1, 1,
                ImmutableMethodReference(BRIDGE_CLASS, "initNative", listOf("Landroid/content/Context;"), "V")
            ))
            impl.addInstruction(2, BuilderInstruction10x(Opcode.RETURN_VOID))
            virtual.add(
                ImmutableMethod(
                    cls.type, "attachBaseContext",
                    listOf(ImmutableMethodParameter("Landroid/content/Context;", null as Set<org.jf.dexlib2.iface.Annotation>?, null)),
                    "V",
                    AccessFlags.PROTECTED.value,
                    null, null,
                    ImmutableMethodImplementation(impl.registerCount, impl.instructions, impl.tryBlocks, impl.debugItems)
                )
            )
        } else {
            val impl = MutableMethodImplementation(existing.implementation!!)
            // p1 (the Context) is always the highest register of this method
            val ctxReg = impl.registerCount - 1
            // find the first invoke-super to attachBaseContext and insert after it,
            // otherwise insert at the start (after any const-string setup is fine:
            // initNative tolerates being called before super in the worst case).
            var insertAt = 0
            outer@ for (i in impl.instructions.indices) {
                val ins: Instruction = impl.instructions[i]
                if (ins.opcode == Opcode.INVOKE_SUPER || ins.opcode == Opcode.INVOKE_SUPER_RANGE) {
                    val ref = (ins as ReferenceInstruction).reference as? MethodReference ?: continue@outer
                    if (ref.name == "attachBaseContext") {
                        insertAt = i + 1
                        break@outer
                    }
                }
            }
            impl.addInstruction(
                insertAt,
                BuilderInstruction3rc(
                    Opcode.INVOKE_STATIC_RANGE, 1, ctxReg,
                    ImmutableMethodReference(BRIDGE_CLASS, "initNative", listOf("Landroid/content/Context;"), "V")
                )
            )
            val idx = virtual.indexOfFirst {
                it.name == "attachBaseContext" && it.parameterTypes.toList() == listOf("Landroid/content/Context;")
            }
            virtual[idx] = ImmutableMethod(
                cls.type, existing.name, existing.parameters, existing.returnType,
                existing.accessFlags, null, null,
                ImmutableMethodImplementation(impl.registerCount, impl.instructions, impl.tryBlocks, impl.debugItems)
            )
        }
        return ImmutableClassDef(
            cls.type, cls.accessFlags, cls.superclass, cls.interfaces, cls.sourceFile, null,
            cls.staticFields, cls.instanceFields, cls.directMethods, virtual
        )
    }
}

```

`hyapk-cli/src/main/kotlin/com/blcyber/hyapk/signing/Signing.kt`:

```kt
package com.blcyber.hyapk.signing

import com.android.apksig.ApkSigner
import com.android.apksig.ApkVerifier
import com.blcyber.hyapk.Log
import com.blcyber.hyapk.ProtectOptions
import org.bouncycastle.cert.jcajce.JcaX509v3CertificateBuilder
import org.bouncycastle.jce.provider.BouncyCastleProvider
import org.bouncycastle.operator.jcajce.JcaContentSignerBuilder
import java.io.File
import java.math.BigInteger
import java.security.KeyPairGenerator
import java.security.KeyStore
import java.security.PrivateKey
import java.security.SecureRandom
import java.security.cert.X509Certificate
import java.security.spec.RSAKeyGenParameterSpec
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale
import javax.security.auth.x500.X500Principal

/** Signing material prepared before the payload build (cert digest needed). */
class KeyMaterial(
    val privateKey: PrivateKey,
    val certificate: X509Certificate,
    val alias: String,
    val password: CharArray
) {
    fun certSha256(): ByteArray =
        java.security.MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(certificate.encoded)
}

object Signer {

    fun prepare(opts: ProtectOptions): KeyMaterial {
        java.security.Security.addProvider(BouncyCastleProvider())
        val alias = opts.keyAlias ?: "hyapk"
        val pass = (opts.keystorePass ?: opts.keyPass ?: "hyapk").toCharArray()
        if (opts.keystorePass == null && opts.keyPass == null) {
            Log.warn("using default keystore password; pass --keystore-pass for real releases")
        }

        if (opts.keystore != null && opts.keystore!!.isFile) {
            val ks = KeyStore.getInstance(if (opts.keystore!!.name.endsWith(".jks")) "JKS" else "PKCS12")
            opts.keystore!!.inputStream().use { ks.load(it, pass) }
            val key = ks.getKey(alias, pass) as? PrivateKey
                ?: Log.die("alias $alias not found in keystore")
            val chain = ks.getCertificateChain(alias)
            if (chain == null || chain.isEmpty()) Log.die("no certificate for alias $alias")
            return KeyMaterial(key, chain[0] as X509Certificate, alias, pass)
        }

        if (opts.genKeystore != null && !opts.genKeystore!!.isFile) {
            generateKeystore(opts.genKeystore!!, alias, pass)
            opts.keystore = opts.genKeystore
            return prepare(opts)
        }

        // ephemeral key
        Log.warn("no keystore given; generating an ephemeral signing key (not reproducible)")
        return generate(alias, pass)
    }

    private fun generate(alias: String, pass: CharArray): KeyMaterial {
        val kpg = KeyPairGenerator.getInstance("RSA")
        kpg.initialize(RSAKeyGenParameterSpec(4096, RSAKeyGenParameterSpec.F4))
        val kp = kpg.generateKeyPair()
        val now = Date()
        val until = Date(now.time + 1000L * 60L * 60L * 24L * 365L * 30L)
        val issuer = X500Principal("CN=Hyapk, O=BL Cyber")
        val serial = BigInteger(159, SecureRandom())
        val builder = JcaX509v3CertificateBuilder(issuer, serial, now, until, issuer, kp.public)
        val signer = JcaContentSignerBuilder("SHA256withRSA").build(kp.private)
        val holder = builder.build(signer)
        val cert = org.bouncycastle.cert.jcajce.JcaX509CertificateConverter()
            .setProvider(BouncyCastleProvider())
            .getCertificate(holder)
        return KeyMaterial(kp.private, cert, alias, pass)
    }

    fun generateKeystore(out: File, alias: String, pass: CharArray) {
        val material = generate(alias, pass)
        val ks = KeyStore.getInstance("PKCS12")
        ks.load(null, null)
        ks.setKeyEntry(alias, material.privateKey, pass, arrayOf(material.certificate))
        out.parentFile?.mkdirs()
        out.outputStream().use { ks.store(it, pass) }
        Log.info("generated keystore: $out (keep it safe; re-signing updates require it)")
    }

    fun sign(material: KeyMaterial, input: File, output: File) {
        val config = ApkSigner.SignerConfig.Builder(
            material.alias, material.privateKey, listOf(material.certificate)
        ).build()
        val signer = ApkSigner.Builder(listOf(config))
            .setV1SigningEnabled(true)
            .setV2SigningEnabled(true)
            .setV3SigningEnabled(true)
            .setInputApk(input)
            .setOutputApk(output)
            .build()
        signer.sign()
        if (!verifySignatures(output)) {
            Log.die("post-sign verification failed")
        }
    }

    fun verifySignatures(file: File): Boolean {
        return try {
            val verifier = ApkVerifier.Builder(file).build()
            val result = verifier.verify()
            result.isVerified && result.isVerified
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.debug("verify error: ${e.message}")
            false
        }
    }
}
```

`hyapk-cli/src/test/kotlin/com/blcyber/hyapk/ContainerTest.kt`:

```kt
package com.blcyber.hyapk

import com.blcyber.hyapk.apk.AlignedZipWriter
import com.blcyber.hyapk.apk.readCentralDirectory
import com.blcyber.hyapk.apk.verifyStructure
import com.blcyber.hyapk.ir.VmOp
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import java.io.File
import java.util.zip.ZipEntry
import java.util.zip.ZipFile

class ContainerTest {

    /** The Kotlin opcode table must match hyapk-native/src/vm/hyvm_opcodes.h. */
    @Test
    fun opcodeTableMatchesCHeader() {
        val header = File("../hyapk-native/src/vm/hyvm_opcodes.h").readText()
        val regex = Regex("#define HYVM_OP_(\\w+) (\\d+)")
        val fromC = regex.findAll(header).associate { it.groupValues[1] to it.groupValues[2].toInt() }
        assertTrue("C header should define opcodes", fromC.size >= VmOp.values().size)
        for (op in VmOp.values()) {
            val cVal = fromC[op.name]
            assertEquals("opcode ${op.name} differs between Kotlin and C", cVal, op.id)
        }
    }

    /** Written zips must keep stored entries aligned (16K for libs). */
    @Test
    fun zipWriterAlignsStoredEntries() {
        val f = File.createTempFile("hyapk-zip-test", ".zip")
        AlignedZipWriter.writeStream(f.outputStream()) { w ->
            w.writeEntry("AndroidManifest.xml", ByteArray(64), ZipEntry.DEFLATED, 4)
            w.writeEntry("classes.dex", ByteArray(256) { 7 }, ZipEntry.STORED, 4)
            w.writeEntry("lib/arm64-v8a/libhyapkrt.so", ByteArray(1024) { 9 }, ZipEntry.STORED, 16384)
            w.writeEntry("lib/x86_64/libhyapkrt.so", ByteArray(512) { 9 }, ZipEntry.STORED, 16384)
        }
        // read back and validate
        ZipFile(f).use { zf ->
            assertEquals(4, zf.size())
            val e = zf.getEntry("classes.dex")
            assertEquals(ZipEntry.STORED, e.method)
        }
        val data = f.readBytes()
        val entries = readCentralDirectory(data)
        assertEquals(4, entries.size)
        val so = entries.getValue("lib/arm64-v8a/libhyapkrt.so")
        assertEquals(0L, so.dataOffset % 16384L)
        val dex = entries.getValue("classes.dex")
        assertEquals(0L, dex.dataOffset % 4L)
        assertTrue(verifyStructure(f).none { it.startsWith("ERROR") })
        f.delete()
    }
}
```

`hyapk-cli/src/test/kotlin/com/blcyber/hyapk/CryptoTest.kt`:

```kt
package com.blcyber.hyapk

import com.blcyber.hyapk.crypto.Crypto
import org.junit.Assert.assertArrayEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Test
import java.math.BigInteger

/**
 * Crypto known-answer tests shared with the C runtime (test_crypto.c) so both
 * implementations stay in sync byte-for-byte.
 */
class CryptoTest {

    @Test
    fun sha256Vectors() {
        assertArrayEquals(
            hex("e3b0c44298fc1c149afbf4c8996fb92427ae41e4649b934ca495991b7852b855"),
            Crypto.sha256(ByteArray(0))
        )
        assertArrayEquals(
            hex("ba7816bf8f01cfea414140de5dae2223b00361a396177a9cb410ff61f20015ad"),
            Crypto.sha256("abc".toByteArray())
        )
    }

    @Test
    fun hmacVector() {
        assertArrayEquals(
            hex("b0344c61d8db38535ca8afceaf0bf12b881dc200c9833da726e9376c2e32cff7"),
            Crypto.hmacSha256(ByteArray(20) { 0x0b }, "Hi There".toByteArray())
        )
    }

    @Test
    fun chacha20BlockVector() {
        val key = ByteArray(32) { it.toByte() }
        val nonce = hex("000000090000004a00000000")
        val block = Crypto.chacha20Block(key, 1, nonce)
        assertEquals(
            "10f1e7e4d13b5915500fdd1fa32071c4c7d1f4c733c068030422aa9ac3d46c4e" +
                "d2826446079faa0914c2d705d98b02a2b5129cd1de164eb9cbd083e8a2503c4e",
            block.toHex()
        )
    }

    @Test
    fun poly1305Vector() {
        val key = hex("85d6be7857556d337f4452fe42d506a8" + "0103808afb0db2fd4abff6af4149f51b")
        val tag = Crypto.poly1305(key, "Cryptographic Forum Research Group".toByteArray())
        assertEquals("a8061dc1305136c6c22b8baf0c0127a9", tag.toHex())
    }

    @Test
    fun aeadRoundTrip() {
        val key = ByteArray(32) { (0x80 + it).toByte() }
        val nonce = ByteArray(12) { 0 }
        nonce[0] = 0x07; nonce[4] = 0x4a
        val aad = byteArrayOf(0x50, 0x51, 0x52, 0x53, 0xC0.toByte(), 0xC1.toByte(), 0xC2.toByte(), 0xC3.toByte(), 0xC4.toByte(), 0xC5.toByte(), 0xC6.toByte(), 0xC7.toByte())
        val pt = "Hyapk round trip test payload".toByteArray()

        val aead = Crypto.aeadEncrypt(key, nonce, aad, pt)
        val back = Crypto.aeadDecrypt(key, nonce, aad, aead.ciphertext, aead.tag)
        assertArrayEquals(pt, back)

        aead.tag[0] = (aead.tag[0].toInt() xor 1).toByte()
        assertNull(Crypto.aeadDecrypt(key, nonce, aad, aead.ciphertext, aead.tag))
    }

    /** Same vector as the C kdf-aead tests. */
    @Test
    fun payloadKdfKat() {
        val libSecret = ByteArray(32) { it.toByte() }
        val fileSalt = ByteArray(32) { (0xA0 + it).toByte() }
        val key = Crypto.derivePayloadKey(libSecret, fileSalt)
        assertEquals(
            "7c7580dcb01f6b77ad31af79cb41afebe59a511864774ea608c7ca19d4450d13",
            key.toHex()
        )

        val nonce = ByteArray(12) { (0xC0 + it).toByte() }
        val pt = ByteArray(16) { (0xE0 + it).toByte() }
        val aead = Crypto.aeadEncrypt(key, nonce, "kat".toByteArray(), pt)
        assertEquals("2f9bfec90fc4dcfde440d5aae48d255f", aead.ciphertext.toHex())
        assertEquals("e7298ce3c36e32de22d29cdc43a1e053", aead.tag.toHex())
    }

    private fun hex(s: String): ByteArray {
        val out = ByteArray(s.length / 2)
        for (i in out.indices) {
            out[i] = s.substring(i * 2, i * 2 + 2).toInt(16).toByte()
        }
        return out
    }

    private fun ByteArray.toHex(): String = joinToString("") {
        String.format("%02x", it)
    }
}
```

`hyapk-native/CMakeLists.txt`:

```txt
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.18)
project(hyapk-native C)

set(CMAKE_C_STANDARD 99)
set(CMAKE_C_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

set(HYAPK_RUNTIME_SOURCES
    src/jni/hy_jni.c
)

if(ANDROID)
    # Android runtime library (libhyapkrt.so), built with the NDK toolchain:
    #   cmake -DCMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE=$ANDROID_NDK_HOME/build/cmake/android.toolchain.cmake \
    #         -DANDROID_ABI=arm64-v8a -DANDROID_PLATFORM=android-21 \
    #         -DHYAPK_GEN_DIR=<generated payload dir> ..
    add_library(hyapkrt SHARED ${HYAPK_RUNTIME_SOURCES})
    target_include_directories(hyapkrt PRIVATE src)
    target_link_libraries(hyapkrt android log)
    target_compile_options(hyapkrt PRIVATE
        -O2 -fvisibility=hidden -fstack-protector-strong -fno-strict-aliasing
        -ffunction-sections -fdata-sections -Wall -Wextra)
    target_link_options(hyapkrt PRIVATE
        -Wl,--gc-sections -Wl,-z,relro -Wl,-z,now -Wl,--exclude-libs,ALL
        -Wl,-z,max-page-size=16384)
    # Strip symbols in release builds.
    target_link_options(hyapkrt PRIVATE -Wl,-s)

    if(HYAPK_GEN_DIR)
        if(EXISTS "${HYAPK_GEN_DIR}/hyapk_gen.c")
            target_sources(hyapkrt PRIVATE "${HYAPK_GEN_DIR}/hyapk_gen.c")
        endif()
        if(EXISTS "${HYAPK_GEN_DIR}/hyapk_gen_d2c.c")
            target_sources(hyapkrt PRIVATE "${HYAPK_GEN_DIR}/hyapk_gen_d2c.c")
        endif()
        target_include_directories(hyapkrt PRIVATE "${HYAPK_GEN_DIR}")
    endif()
else()
    # Host build: unit tests only (no JNI runtime; it needs the NDK).
    enable_testing()
    foreach(t test_crypto test_vm test_payload)
        add_executable(${t} tests/${t}.c)
        target_include_directories(${t} PRIVATE src)
        target_compile_options(${t} PRIVATE -O2 -Wall -Wextra)
        add_test(NAME ${t} COMMAND ${t})
    endforeach()
endif()
```
