`hyapk-native/src/common/hy_defs.h`:

```h
/*
 * Hyapk - modern cryptography primitives (native side).
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: AGPL-3.0-only
 * Copyright (C) 2026 beto2-dev (BL Cyber) and ChapzoMods.
 */
#ifndef HY_DEFS_H
#define HY_DEFS_H

#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

#define HYAPI static
/* Note: all runtime internals use internal linkage; shared-library
   visibility is controlled with -fvisibility=hidden at build level. */
#define HY_HIDDEN

/* Payload / program limits (defensive). */
#define HY_PAYLOAD_MAX_BYTES (64u * 1024u * 1024u)
#define HY_VM_PROGRAM_MAX_BYTES (8u * 1024u * 1024u)
#define HY_VM_MAX_REGS 4096u
#define HY_VM_MAX_SLOTS 65536u
#define HY_STRING_MAX_BYTES (16u * 1024u * 1024u)
#define HY_STRING_MAX_COUNT (1u << 20u)

/* Compile-time assertion helper. */
#define HY_STATIC_ASSERT(cond, name) typedef char hy_static_assert_##name[(cond) ? 1 : -1]

HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_secure_zero(void *p, size_t n) {
    volatile uint8_t *v = (volatile uint8_t *)p;
    while (n--) {
        *v++ = 0;
    }
}

/* Constant-time memory comparison. Returns 0 when equal, 1 otherwise. */
HY_HIDDEN static inline int hy_ct_memcmp(const void *a, const void *b, size_t n) {
    const volatile uint8_t *x = (const volatile uint8_t *)a;
    const volatile uint8_t *y = (const volatile uint8_t *)b;
    uint8_t d = 0;
    size_t i;
    for (i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        d |= (uint8_t)(x[i] ^ y[i]);
    }
    return d != 0;
}

/* Best-effort local timeout seconds before reacting to a violation. */
#define HY_GUARD_INTERVAL_SECONDS 12

#endif /* HY_DEFS_H */

```

`hyapk-native/src/crypto/hy_chacha20poly1305.h`:

```h
/*
 * ChaCha20 stream cipher and ChaCha20-Poly1305 AEAD (RFC 8439).
 * Self-contained portable C99. Constant-time tag comparison.
 */
#ifndef HY_CHACHA20POLY1305_H
#define HY_CHACHA20POLY1305_H

#include "../common/hy_defs.h"

#define HY_AEAD_KEYLEN 32u
#define HY_AEAD_NONCELEN 12u
#define HY_AEAD_TAGLEN 16u

HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_chacha20_block(const uint8_t key[32], uint32_t counter,
                                                const uint8_t nonce[12], uint8_t out[64]);
HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_chacha20_xor(const uint8_t key[32], uint32_t counter,
                                             const uint8_t nonce[12],
                                             const uint8_t *in, uint8_t *out, size_t n);

typedef struct {
    uint32_t r[5];   /* clamped r, 26-bit limbs */
    uint32_t s5[5];  /* r[i]*5 for the 2^130 == 5 (mod p) fold */
    uint32_t h[5];   /* accumulator, 26-bit limbs */
    uint32_t k[4];   /* secret additive component s */
    uint8_t buf[16];
    size_t buf_used;
} hy_poly1305_ctx;

HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_poly1305_init(hy_poly1305_ctx *ctx, const uint8_t key[32]);
HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_poly1305_update(hy_poly1305_ctx *ctx, const void *data, size_t len);
HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_poly1305_flush(hy_poly1305_ctx *ctx);
HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_poly1305_final(hy_poly1305_ctx *ctx, uint8_t tag[16]);

HY_HIDDEN static int hy_aead_encrypt(const uint8_t key[32], const uint8_t nonce[12],
                                      const uint8_t *aad, size_t aad_len,
                                      const uint8_t *pt, size_t pt_len,
                                      uint8_t *ct, uint8_t tag[16]);
HY_HIDDEN static int hy_aead_decrypt(const uint8_t key[32], const uint8_t nonce[12],
                                      const uint8_t *aad, size_t aad_len,
                                      const uint8_t *ct, size_t ct_len,
                                      const uint8_t tag[16],
                                      uint8_t *pt);

#endif /* HY_CHACHA20POLY1305_H */

#ifdef HY_CHACHA20POLY1305_IMPLEMENTATION
#ifndef HY_CHACHA20POLY1305_IMPL_DONE
#define HY_CHACHA20POLY1305_IMPL_DONE


#define HY_ROTL32(x, n) (((x) << (n)) | ((x) >> (32 - (n))))
#define HY_QR(a, b, c, d)                        \
    do {                                         \
        a += b; d ^= a; d = HY_ROTL32(d, 16);    \
        c += d; b ^= c; b = HY_ROTL32(b, 12);    \
        a += b; d ^= a; d = HY_ROTL32(d, 8);     \
        c += d; b ^= c; b = HY_ROTL32(b, 7);     \
    } while (0)

HY_HIDDEN static inline uint32_t hy_le32(const uint8_t *p) {
    return (uint32_t)p[0] | ((uint32_t)p[1] << 8) | ((uint32_t)p[2] << 16) | ((uint32_t)p[3] << 24);
}

HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_put32(uint8_t *p, uint32_t v) {
    p[0] = (uint8_t)v; p[1] = (uint8_t)(v >> 8);
    p[2] = (uint8_t)(v >> 16); p[3] = (uint8_t)(v >> 24);
}

HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_chacha20_block(const uint8_t key[32], uint32_t counter,
                                                const uint8_t nonce[12], uint8_t out[64]) {
    uint32_t s[16];
    uint32_t w[16];
    unsigned i, r;

    s[0] = 0x61707865u; s[1] = 0x3320646eu; s[2] = 0x79622d32u; s[3] = 0x6b206574u;
    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) s[4 + i] = hy_le32(key + i * 4);
    s[12] = counter;
    s[13] = hy_le32(nonce); s[14] = hy_le32(nonce + 4); s[15] = hy_le32(nonce + 8);

    for (i = 0; i < 16; i++) w[i] = s[i];
    for (r = 0; r < 10; r++) {
        HY_QR(w[0], w[4], w[8], w[12]);
        HY_QR(w[1], w[5], w[9], w[13]);
        HY_QR(w[2], w[6], w[10], w[14]);
        HY_QR(w[3], w[7], w[11], w[15]);
        HY_QR(w[0], w[5], w[10], w[15]);
        HY_QR(w[1], w[6], w[11], w[12]);
        HY_QR(w[2], w[7], w[8], w[13]);
        HY_QR(w[3], w[4], w[9], w[14]);
    }
    for (i = 0; i < 16; i++) hy_put32(out + i * 4, w[i] + s[i]);

    hy_secure_zero(s, sizeof(s));
    hy_secure_zero(w, sizeof(w));
}

HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_chacha20_xor(const uint8_t key[32], uint32_t counter,
                                             const uint8_t nonce[12],
                                             const uint8_t *in, uint8_t *out, size_t n) {
    uint8_t ks[64];
    size_t off = 0;
    if (n == 0) return;
    while (off < n) {
        size_t take = n - off;
        size_t i;
        if (take > 64) take = 64;
        hy_chacha20_block(key, counter, nonce, ks);
        for (i = 0; i < take; i++) {
            out[off + i] = in[off + i] ^ ks[i];
        }
        hy_secure_zero(ks, sizeof(ks));
        off += take;
        counter++;
    }
}

/* ---------- Poly1305 (RFC 8439 section 2.5), 26-bit limb implementation ---------- */

HY_HIDDEN static inline uint32_t hy_poly1305_rmask(unsigned i) {
    static const uint32_t m[5] = {0x03ffffffu, 0x03ffff03u, 0x03ffc0ffu, 0x03f03fffu, 0x000fffffu};
    return m[i];
}

HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_poly1305_init(hy_poly1305_ctx *ctx, const uint8_t key[32]) {
    unsigned i;
    ctx->r[0] = hy_le32(key) & hy_poly1305_rmask(0);
    ctx->r[1] = (hy_le32(key + 3) >> 2) & hy_poly1305_rmask(1);
    ctx->r[2] = (hy_le32(key + 6) >> 4) & hy_poly1305_rmask(2);
    ctx->r[3] = (hy_le32(key + 9) >> 6) & hy_poly1305_rmask(3);
    ctx->r[4] = (hy_le32(key + 12) >> 8) & hy_poly1305_rmask(4);
    for (i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        ctx->s5[i] = ctx->r[i] * 5u;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) ctx->k[i] = hy_le32(key + 16 + i * 4);
    memset(ctx->h, 0, sizeof(ctx->h));
    ctx->buf_used = 0;
}

/* Absorb a full 16-byte block (implicit 2^128 high bit) or a final partial block
 * already padded with the trailing 1 byte inside the 16-byte window. */
HY_HIDDEN static void hy_poly1305_absorb(hy_poly1305_ctx *ctx, const uint8_t b[16], int full) {
    uint64_t d0, d1, d2, d3, d4;
    uint32_t c;
    uint32_t *h = ctx->h;
    const uint32_t *r = ctx->r;
    const uint32_t *s5 = ctx->s5;

    uint32_t t0 = hy_le32(b) & 0x3ffffffu;
    uint32_t t1 = (hy_le32(b + 3) >> 2) & 0x3ffffffu;
    uint32_t t2 = (hy_le32(b + 6) >> 4) & 0x3ffffffu;
    uint32_t t3 = (hy_le32(b + 9) >> 6) & 0x3ffffffu;
    uint32_t t4 = hy_le32(b + 12) >> 8;
    if (full) t4 += (1u << 24);

    /* h = (h + t) * r: first add the block to the accumulator. */
    h[0] += t0; h[1] += t1; h[2] += t2; h[3] += t3; h[4] += t4;

    d0 = (uint64_t)h[0] * r[0] + (uint64_t)h[1] * s5[4] + (uint64_t)h[2] * s5[3] +
         (uint64_t)h[3] * s5[2] + (uint64_t)h[4] * s5[1];
    d1 = (uint64_t)h[0] * r[1] + (uint64_t)h[1] * r[0] + (uint64_t)h[2] * s5[4] +
         (uint64_t)h[3] * s5[3] + (uint64_t)h[4] * s5[2];
    d2 = (uint64_t)h[0] * r[2] + (uint64_t)h[1] * r[1] + (uint64_t)h[2] * r[0] +
         (uint64_t)h[3] * s5[4] + (uint64_t)h[4] * s5[3];
    d3 = (uint64_t)h[0] * r[3] + (uint64_t)h[1] * r[2] + (uint64_t)h[2] * r[1] +
         (uint64_t)h[3] * r[0] + (uint64_t)h[4] * s5[4];
    d4 = (uint64_t)h[0] * r[4] + (uint64_t)h[1] * r[3] + (uint64_t)h[2] * r[2] +
         (uint64_t)h[3] * r[1] + (uint64_t)h[4] * r[0];

    c = (uint32_t)(d0 >> 26); h[0] = (uint32_t)d0 & 0x3ffffffu;
    d1 += c; c = (uint32_t)(d1 >> 26); h[1] = (uint32_t)d1 & 0x3ffffffu;
    d2 += c; c = (uint32_t)(d2 >> 26); h[2] = (uint32_t)d2 & 0x3ffffffu;
    d3 += c; c = (uint32_t)(d3 >> 26); h[3] = (uint32_t)d3 & 0x3ffffffu;
    d4 += c; c = (uint32_t)(d4 >> 26); h[4] = (uint32_t)d4 & 0x3ffffffu;
    h[0] += c * 5u;
    c = h[0] >> 26; h[0] &= 0x3ffffffu; h[1] += c;
}

HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_poly1305_update(hy_poly1305_ctx *ctx, const void *data, size_t len) {
    const uint8_t *p = (const uint8_t *)data;
    if (len == 0 || p == NULL) return;
    if (ctx->buf_used > 0) {
        size_t take = 16u - ctx->buf_used;
        if (take > len) take = len;
        memcpy(ctx->buf + ctx->buf_used, p, take);
        ctx->buf_used += take;
        p += take;
        len -= take;
        if (ctx->buf_used == 16u) {
            hy_poly1305_absorb(ctx, ctx->buf, 1);
            ctx->buf_used = 0;
        }
    }
    while (len >= 16u) {
        hy_poly1305_absorb(ctx, p, 1);
        p += 16;
        len -= 16;
    }
    if (len > 0) {
        memcpy(ctx->buf, p, len);
        ctx->buf_used = len;
    }
}

/* Pad the current partial buffer with the trailing 1 byte and absorb it.
 * Use only for the final partial block of a raw Poly1305 message (RFC 8439 2.5). */
HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_poly1305_flush(hy_poly1305_ctx *ctx) {
    if (ctx->buf_used == 0) return;
    ctx->buf[ctx->buf_used] = 1;
    memset(ctx->buf + ctx->buf_used + 1, 0, 16u - ctx->buf_used - 1u);
    hy_poly1305_absorb(ctx, ctx->buf, 0);
    ctx->buf_used = 0;
}

/* Zero-pad the buffer to 16 bytes and absorb it as a full block.
 * This is the padding used by the AEAD construction for each field
 * (RFC 8439 2.8: aad and ciphertext are zero-padded to a 16-byte multiple). */
HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_poly1305_pad_zeros(hy_poly1305_ctx *ctx) {
    if (ctx->buf_used == 0) return;
    memset(ctx->buf + ctx->buf_used, 0, 16u - ctx->buf_used);
    hy_poly1305_absorb(ctx, ctx->buf, 1);
    ctx->buf_used = 0;
}

HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_poly1305_final(hy_poly1305_ctx *ctx, uint8_t tag[16]) {
    uint32_t h0, h1, h2, h3, h4, c, mask;
    uint64_t v0, v1, a0, a1;

    /* Fully carry h. */
    h0 = ctx->h[0]; h1 = ctx->h[1]; h2 = ctx->h[2]; h3 = ctx->h[3]; h4 = ctx->h[4];
    c = h1 >> 26; h1 &= 0x3ffffffu;
    h2 += c; c = h2 >> 26; h2 &= 0x3ffffffu;
    h3 += c; c = h3 >> 26; h3 &= 0x3ffffffu;
    h4 += c; c = h4 >> 26; h4 &= 0x3ffffffu;
    h0 += c * 5u; c = h0 >> 26; h0 &= 0x3ffffffu;
    h1 += c;

    /* Compute h + (-p); select h if h < p, else h - p. */
    {
        uint32_t g0 = h0 + 5u; c = g0 >> 26; g0 &= 0x3ffffffu;
        uint32_t g1 = h1 + c; c = g1 >> 26; g1 &= 0x3ffffffu;
        uint32_t g2 = h2 + c; c = g2 >> 26; g2 &= 0x3ffffffu;
        uint32_t g3 = h3 + c; c = g3 >> 26; g3 &= 0x3ffffffu;
        uint32_t g4 = h4 + c - (1u << 26);

        mask = (g4 >> 31) - 1u; /* 0xFFFFFFFF when g4 did not borrow (h >= p), else 0 */
        h0 = (h0 & ~mask) | (g0 & mask);
        h1 = (h1 & ~mask) | (g1 & mask);
        h2 = (h2 & ~mask) | (g2 & mask);
        h3 = (h3 & ~mask) | (g3 & mask);
        /* h4 contribution above bit 127 is dropped; low 24 bits folded below. */
        h4 = (h4 & ~mask) | (g4 & mask);
    }

    /* Serialize h to 128 bits: v0 = bits 0..63, v1 = bits 64..127. */
    v0 = (uint64_t)h0 | ((uint64_t)h1 << 26) | ((uint64_t)(h2 & 0xfffu) << 52);
    v1 = ((uint64_t)h2 >> 12) | ((uint64_t)h3 << 14) | ((uint64_t)(h4 & 0xffffffu) << 40);

    /* Add secret s (128-bit little-endian). */
    {
        uint64_t sk0 = (uint64_t)ctx->k[0] | ((uint64_t)ctx->k[1] << 32);
        uint64_t sk1 = (uint64_t)ctx->k[2] | ((uint64_t)ctx->k[3] << 32);
        a0 = v0 + sk0;
        a1 = v1 + sk1 + (a0 < v0 ? 1u : 0u);
    }

    hy_put32(tag, (uint32_t)a0);
    hy_put32(tag + 4, (uint32_t)(a0 >> 32));
    hy_put32(tag + 8, (uint32_t)a1);
    hy_put32(tag + 12, (uint32_t)(a1 >> 32));

    hy_secure_zero(ctx, sizeof(*ctx));
}

/* ---------- ChaCha20-Poly1305 AEAD (RFC 8439 section 2.8) ---------- */

HY_HIDDEN static void hy_aead_mac(const uint8_t poly_key[32],
                                   const uint8_t *aad, size_t aad_len,
                                   const uint8_t *ct, size_t ct_len,
                                   uint8_t tag[16]) {
    hy_poly1305_ctx pc;
    uint8_t lenbuf[16];

    hy_poly1305_init(&pc, poly_key);
    hy_poly1305_update(&pc, aad, aad_len);
    hy_poly1305_pad_zeros(&pc);
    hy_poly1305_update(&pc, ct, ct_len);
    hy_poly1305_pad_zeros(&pc);

    hy_put32(lenbuf, (uint32_t)(aad_len & 0xffffffffu));
    hy_put32(lenbuf + 4, (uint32_t)((uint64_t)aad_len >> 32));
    hy_put32(lenbuf + 8, (uint32_t)(ct_len & 0xffffffffu));
    hy_put32(lenbuf + 12, (uint32_t)((uint64_t)ct_len >> 32));
    hy_poly1305_update(&pc, lenbuf, 16);
    /* lenbuf is exactly 16 bytes: absorb as full block with the implicit high bit. */
    hy_poly1305_final(&pc, tag);

    hy_secure_zero(lenbuf, sizeof(lenbuf));
}

HY_HIDDEN static int hy_aead_encrypt(const uint8_t key[32], const uint8_t nonce[12],
                                      const uint8_t *aad, size_t aad_len,
                                      const uint8_t *pt, size_t pt_len,
                                      uint8_t *ct, uint8_t tag[16]) {
    uint8_t block0[64];
    uint8_t poly_key[32];

    if (aad == NULL) aad_len = 0;
    if (pt == NULL) pt_len = 0;
    if (pt_len > 0 && ct == NULL) return -1;

    hy_chacha20_block(key, 0u, nonce, block0);
    memcpy(poly_key, block0, 32);
    hy_secure_zero(block0, sizeof(block0));

    hy_chacha20_xor(key, 1u, nonce, pt, ct, pt_len);
    hy_aead_mac(poly_key, aad, aad_len, ct, pt_len, tag);

    hy_secure_zero(poly_key, sizeof(poly_key));
    return 0;
}

HY_HIDDEN static int hy_aead_decrypt(const uint8_t key[32], const uint8_t nonce[12],
                                      const uint8_t *aad, size_t aad_len,
                                      const uint8_t *ct, size_t ct_len,
                                      const uint8_t tag[16],
                                      uint8_t *pt) {
    uint8_t block0[64];
    uint8_t poly_key[32];
    uint8_t computed[16];
    int bad;

    if (aad == NULL) aad_len = 0;
    if (ct == NULL) ct_len = 0;
    if (ct_len > 0 && pt == NULL) return -1;

    hy_chacha20_block(key, 0u, nonce, block0);
    memcpy(poly_key, block0, 32);
    hy_secure_zero(block0, sizeof(block0));

    hy_aead_mac(poly_key, aad, aad_len, ct, ct_len, computed);
    bad = hy_ct_memcmp(computed, tag, 16);

    if (!bad) {
        hy_chacha20_xor(key, 1u, nonce, ct, pt, ct_len);
    }

    hy_secure_zero(poly_key, sizeof(poly_key));
    hy_secure_zero(computed, sizeof(computed));
    return bad ? -1 : 0;
}

#undef HY_ROTL32
#undef HY_QR

#endif /* HY_CHACHA20POLY1305_IMPL_DONE */
#endif /* HY_CHACHA20POLY1305_IMPLEMENTATION */

```

`hyapk-native/src/crypto/hy_hkdf.h`:

```h
/* HMAC-SHA256 (RFC 2104) and HKDF-SHA256 (RFC 5869). */
#ifndef HY_HKDF_H
#define HY_HKDF_H

#include "../common/hy_defs.h"

HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_hmac_sha256(const uint8_t *key, size_t key_len,
                                            const void *msg, size_t msg_len,
                                            uint8_t out[32]);
HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_hkdf_sha256_extract(const uint8_t *salt, size_t salt_len,
                                                    const uint8_t *ikm, size_t ikm_len,
                                                    uint8_t prk[32]);
HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_hkdf_sha256_expand(const uint8_t prk[32], size_t prk_len,
                                                   const uint8_t *info, size_t info_len,
                                                   uint8_t *okm, size_t okm_len);

#endif /* HY_HKDF_H */

#ifdef HY_HKDF_IMPLEMENTATION
#ifndef HY_HKDF_IMPL_DONE
#define HY_HKDF_IMPL_DONE


#define HY_SHA256_IMPLEMENTATION
#include "hy_sha256.h"

HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_hmac_sha256(const uint8_t *key, size_t key_len,
                                            const void *msg, size_t msg_len,
                                            uint8_t out[32]) {
    uint8_t k[64];
    uint8_t k_ipad[64];
    uint8_t k_opad[64];
    uint8_t inner[32];
    hy_sha256_ctx ctx;
    unsigned i;

    memset(k, 0, sizeof(k));
    if (key_len > 64) {
        hy_sha256(key, key_len, k);
    } else if (key_len > 0) {
        memcpy(k, key, key_len);
    }

    for (i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
        k_ipad[i] = k[i] ^ 0x36;
        k_opad[i] = k[i] ^ 0x5c;
    }

    hy_sha256_init(&ctx);
    hy_sha256_update(&ctx, k_ipad, 64);
    hy_sha256_update(&ctx, msg, msg_len);
    hy_sha256_final(&ctx, inner);

    hy_sha256_init(&ctx);
    hy_sha256_update(&ctx, k_opad, 64);
    hy_sha256_update(&ctx, inner, 32);
    hy_sha256_final(&ctx, out);

    hy_secure_zero(k, sizeof(k));
    hy_secure_zero(k_ipad, sizeof(k_ipad));
    hy_secure_zero(k_opad, sizeof(k_opad));
}

HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_hkdf_sha256_extract(const uint8_t *salt, size_t salt_len,
                                                     const uint8_t *ikm, size_t ikm_len,
                                                     uint8_t prk[32]) {
    if (salt_len == 0 || salt == NULL) {
        static const uint8_t zero[32] = {0};
        hy_hmac_sha256(zero, 32, ikm, ikm_len, prk);
    } else {
        hy_hmac_sha256(salt, salt_len, ikm, ikm_len, prk);
    }
}

HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_hkdf_sha256_expand(const uint8_t prk[32], size_t prk_len,
                                                    const uint8_t *info, size_t info_len,
                                                    uint8_t *okm, size_t okm_len) {
    uint8_t t[32];
    uint8_t *msg;
    size_t t_len = 0;
    uint8_t counter = 1;
    size_t produced = 0;

    /* T(i) = HMAC-SHA256(PRK, T(i-1) || info || i) */
    msg = (uint8_t *)malloc(32u + info_len + 1u);
    if (msg == NULL) return;

    while (produced < okm_len) {
        size_t take = okm_len - produced;
        size_t off = 0;
        if (take > 32) take = 32;
        if (t_len > 0) {
            memcpy(msg, t, 32);
            off = 32;
        }
        if (info_len > 0) {
            memcpy(msg + off, info, info_len);
            off += info_len;
        }
        msg[off] = counter;
        hy_hmac_sha256(prk, prk_len, msg, off + 1, t);
        t_len = 32;

        memcpy(okm + produced, t, take);
        produced += take;
        counter++;
        if (counter == 0) break; /* Overflow guard, unreachable for sane okm_len. */
    }
    hy_secure_zero(t, sizeof(t));
    hy_secure_zero(msg, 32u + info_len + 1u);
    free(msg);
}

#endif /* HY_HKDF_IMPL_DONE */
#endif /* HY_HKDF_IMPLEMENTATION */

```

`hyapk-native/src/crypto/hy_kdf.h`:

```h
/*
 * Hyapk payload key derivation.
 * content_key = HMAC-SHA256( SHA256("HYAPK-KDF1" || lib_secret || file_salt),
 *                             "hyapk-payload-aead-v1" )
 * The same construction is implemented by the Kotlin tool side; both are
 * covered by shared known-answer tests.
 */
#ifndef HY_PAYLOAD_KDF_H
#define HY_PAYLOAD_KDF_H

#include "../common/hy_defs.h"

#define HY_KDF_LABEL "HYAPK-KDF1"
#define HY_KDF_INFO "hyapk-payload-aead-v1"

HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_derive_payload_key(const uint8_t lib_secret[32],
                                                    const uint8_t file_salt[32],
                                                    uint8_t out_key[32]);

#endif /* HY_PAYLOAD_KDF_H */

#ifdef HY_PAYLOAD_KDF_IMPLEMENTATION
#ifndef HY_PAYLOAD_KDF_IMPL_DONE
#define HY_PAYLOAD_KDF_IMPL_DONE


#define HY_SHA256_IMPLEMENTATION
#include "hy_sha256.h"
#define HY_HKDF_IMPLEMENTATION
#include "hy_hkdf.h"

HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_derive_payload_key(const uint8_t lib_secret[32],
                                                    const uint8_t file_salt[32],
                                                    uint8_t out_key[32]) {
    hy_sha256_ctx ctx;
    uint8_t master[32];
    static const char label[] = HY_KDF_LABEL;
    static const char info[] = HY_KDF_INFO;

    hy_sha256_init(&ctx);
    hy_sha256_update(&ctx, label, sizeof(label) - 1);
    hy_sha256_update(&ctx, lib_secret, 32);
    hy_sha256_update(&ctx, file_salt, 32);
    hy_sha256_final(&ctx, master);

    hy_hmac_sha256(master, 32, info, sizeof(info) - 1, out_key);

    hy_secure_zero(master, sizeof(master));
}

#endif /* HY_PAYLOAD_KDF_IMPL_DONE */
#endif /* HY_PAYLOAD_KDF_IMPLEMENTATION */

```

`hyapk-native/src/crypto/hy_sha256.h`:

```h
/* SHA-256 (FIPS 180-4). Self-contained, portable C99. */
#ifndef HY_SHA256_H
#define HY_SHA256_H

#include "../common/hy_defs.h"

typedef struct {
    uint32_t state[8];
    uint64_t bitlen;
    uint8_t block[64];
    size_t block_used;
} hy_sha256_ctx;

HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_sha256_init(hy_sha256_ctx *ctx);
HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_sha256_update(hy_sha256_ctx *ctx, const void *data, size_t len);
HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_sha256_final(hy_sha256_ctx *ctx, uint8_t out[32]);
HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_sha256(const void *data, size_t len, uint8_t out[32]);

#endif /* HY_SHA256_H */

#ifdef HY_SHA256_IMPLEMENTATION
#ifndef HY_SHA256_IMPL_DONE
#define HY_SHA256_IMPL_DONE


#define HY_ROTR32(x, n) (((x) >> (n)) | ((x) << (32 - (n))))
#define HY_CH(x, y, z) (((x) & (y)) ^ (~(x) & (z)))
#define HY_MAJ(x, y, z) (((x) & (y)) ^ ((x) & (z)) ^ ((y) & (z)))
#define HY_BSIG0(x) (HY_ROTR32(x, 2) ^ HY_ROTR32(x, 13) ^ HY_ROTR32(x, 22))
#define HY_BSIG1(x) (HY_ROTR32(x, 6) ^ HY_ROTR32(x, 11) ^ HY_ROTR32(x, 25))
#define HY_SSIG0(x) (HY_ROTR32(x, 7) ^ HY_ROTR32(x, 18) ^ ((x) >> 3))
#define HY_SSIG1(x) (HY_ROTR32(x, 17) ^ HY_ROTR32(x, 19) ^ ((x) >> 10))

HY_HIDDEN static const uint32_t hy_sha256_k[64] = {
    0x428a2f98u, 0x71374491u, 0xb5c0fbcfu, 0xe9b5dba5u, 0x3956c25bu, 0x59f111f1u, 0x923f82a4u, 0xab1c5ed5u,
    0xd807aa98u, 0x12835b01u, 0x243185beu, 0x550c7dc3u, 0x72be5d74u, 0x80deb1feu, 0x9bdc06a7u, 0xc19bf174u,
    0xe49b69c1u, 0xefbe4786u, 0x0fc19dc6u, 0x240ca1ccu, 0x2de92c6fu, 0x4a7484aau, 0x5cb0a9dcu, 0x76f988dau,
    0x983e5152u, 0xa831c66du, 0xb00327c8u, 0xbf597fc7u, 0xc6e00bf3u, 0xd5a79147u, 0x06ca6351u, 0x14292967u,
    0x27b70a85u, 0x2e1b2138u, 0x4d2c6dfcu, 0x53380d13u, 0x650a7354u, 0x766a0abbu, 0x81c2c92eu, 0x92722c85u,
    0xa2bfe8a1u, 0xa81a664bu, 0xc24b8b70u, 0xc76c51a3u, 0xd192e819u, 0xd6990624u, 0xf40e3585u, 0x106aa070u,
    0x19a4c116u, 0x1e376c08u, 0x2748774cu, 0x34b0bcb5u, 0x391c0cb3u, 0x4ed8aa4au, 0x5b9cca4fu, 0x682e6ff3u,
    0x748f82eeu, 0x78a5636fu, 0x84c87814u, 0x8cc70208u, 0x90befffau, 0xa4506cebu, 0xbef9a3f7u, 0xc67178f2u
};

HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_sha256_init(hy_sha256_ctx *ctx) {
    ctx->state[0] = 0x6a09e667u; ctx->state[1] = 0xbb67ae85u;
    ctx->state[2] = 0x3c6ef372u; ctx->state[3] = 0xa54ff53au;
    ctx->state[4] = 0x510e527fu; ctx->state[5] = 0x9b05688cu;
    ctx->state[6] = 0x1f83d9abu; ctx->state[7] = 0x5be0cd19u;
    ctx->bitlen = 0;
    ctx->block_used = 0;
}

HY_HIDDEN static void hy_sha256_block(hy_sha256_ctx *ctx, const uint8_t p[64]) {
    uint32_t w[64];
    uint32_t a, b, c, d, e, f, g, h;
    unsigned i;

    for (i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
        w[i] = ((uint32_t)p[i * 4] << 24) | ((uint32_t)p[i * 4 + 1] << 16) |
               ((uint32_t)p[i * 4 + 2] << 8) | (uint32_t)p[i * 4 + 3];
    }
    for (i = 16; i < 64; i++) {
        w[i] = HY_SSIG1(w[i - 2]) + w[i - 7] + HY_SSIG0(w[i - 15]) + w[i - 16];
    }

    a = ctx->state[0]; b = ctx->state[1]; c = ctx->state[2]; d = ctx->state[3];
    e = ctx->state[4]; f = ctx->state[5]; g = ctx->state[6]; h = ctx->state[7];

    for (i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
        uint32_t t1 = h + HY_BSIG1(e) + HY_CH(e, f, g) + hy_sha256_k[i] + w[i];
        uint32_t t2 = HY_BSIG0(a) + HY_MAJ(a, b, c);
        h = g; g = f; f = e; e = d + t1;
        d = c; c = b; b = a; a = t1 + t2;
    }

    ctx->state[0] += a; ctx->state[1] += b; ctx->state[2] += c; ctx->state[3] += d;
    ctx->state[4] += e; ctx->state[5] += f; ctx->state[6] += g; ctx->state[7] += h;
}

HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_sha256_update(hy_sha256_ctx *ctx, const void *data, size_t len) {
    const uint8_t *p = (const uint8_t *)data;
    ctx->bitlen += (uint64_t)len * 8u;
    while (len > 0) {
        size_t take = 64u - ctx->block_used;
        if (take > len) take = len;
        memcpy(ctx->block + ctx->block_used, p, take);
        ctx->block_used += take;
        p += take;
        len -= take;
        if (ctx->block_used == 64u) {
            hy_sha256_block(ctx, ctx->block);
            ctx->block_used = 0;
        }
    }
}

HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_sha256_final(hy_sha256_ctx *ctx, uint8_t out[32]) {
    uint64_t bitlen = ctx->bitlen;
    unsigned i;

    hy_sha256_update(ctx, "\x80", 1);
    while (ctx->block_used != 56u) {
        hy_sha256_update(ctx, "\x00", 1);
    }
    /* Length is now 56 mod 64; append big-endian bit length. */
    ctx->block[56] = (uint8_t)(bitlen >> 56);
    ctx->block[57] = (uint8_t)(bitlen >> 48);
    ctx->block[58] = (uint8_t)(bitlen >> 40);
    ctx->block[59] = (uint8_t)(bitlen >> 32);
    ctx->block[60] = (uint8_t)(bitlen >> 24);
    ctx->block[61] = (uint8_t)(bitlen >> 16);
    ctx->block[62] = (uint8_t)(bitlen >> 8);
    ctx->block[63] = (uint8_t)(bitlen);
    hy_sha256_block(ctx, ctx->block);
    ctx->block_used = 0;

    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        out[i * 4] = (uint8_t)(ctx->state[i] >> 24);
        out[i * 4 + 1] = (uint8_t)(ctx->state[i] >> 16);
        out[i * 4 + 2] = (uint8_t)(ctx->state[i] >> 8);
        out[i * 4 + 3] = (uint8_t)(ctx->state[i]);
    }
    hy_secure_zero(ctx, sizeof(*ctx));
}

HY_HIDDEN static inline void hy_sha256(const void *data, size_t len, uint8_t out[32]) {
    hy_sha256_ctx ctx;
    hy_sha256_init(&ctx);
    hy_sha256_update(&ctx, data, len);
    hy_sha256_final(&ctx, out);
}

#undef HY_ROTR32
#undef HY_CH
#undef HY_MAJ
#undef HY_BSIG0
#undef HY_BSIG1
#undef HY_SSIG0
#undef HY_SSIG1

#endif /* HY_SHA256_IMPL_DONE */
#endif /* HY_SHA256_IMPLEMENTATION */

```

`hyapk-native/src/jni/hy_bridge_jni.h`:

```h
/*
 * Hyapk JNI bridge: implements the HyVm bridge and the dex2c helpers on top
 * of JNI, backed by the decrypted payload tables. Also exposes the native
 * entry points registered by the generated code.
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: AGPL-3.0-only
 */
#ifndef HY_BRIDGE_JNI_H
#define HY_BRIDGE_JNI_H

#include <jni.h>
#include "hy_payload.h"
#include "../vm/hyvm.h"

/* Generated by the packer (hyapk_gen.c). */
extern const unsigned char hyapk_payload_blob[];
extern const unsigned int hyapk_payload_blob_size;
extern const unsigned char hyapk_lib_secret[32];

/* Generated by the packer (hyapk_gen_d2c.c) when methods were converted. */
typedef struct {
    const char *class_name; /* dotted, e.g. com.example.Foo */
    const char *method_name;
    const char *signature;
    void *fn;
} hy_native_reg;
extern const hy_native_reg hyapk_native_registrations[];
extern const unsigned int hyapk_native_reg_count;

/* Payload state. */
extern hy_payload g_payload;
extern hy_string_table g_strings;

/* Sets the thread-local JNIEnv used by all bridge helpers. */
HY_HIDDEN static void hyjni_set_env(JNIEnv *env);

/* Resolves a payload type descriptor ("Lcom/foo/Bar;") into a jclass global
 * reference using the cached application class loader. */
HY_HIDDEN static jclass hyjni_resolve_type(uint32_t type_idx);

/* Method/field caches. */
HY_HIDDEN static jmethodID hyjni_method_id(uint32_t method_idx, jclass *cls, hy_method_entry *me);
HY_HIDDEN static jfieldID hyjni_field_id(uint32_t field_idx, jclass *cls, hy_field_entry *fe);

/* HyVm bridge (installed as g_bridge). */
extern const hyvm_bridge hyjni_bridge;

/* Decrypts and validates the embedded payload; returns 0 on success. */
HY_HIDDEN static int hyjni_payload_init(void);

/* String helper: payload string id -> local jstring reference. */
HY_HIDDEN static jstring hyjni_string(uint32_t id);

#endif /* HY_BRIDGE_JNI_H */

#ifdef HY_BRIDGE_JNI_IMPLEMENTATION

#include <pthread.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#define HY_SHA256_IMPLEMENTATION
#include "../crypto/hy_sha256.h"
#define HY_CHACHA20POLY1305_IMPLEMENTATION
#include "../crypto/hy_chacha20poly1305.h"
#define HY_PAYLOAD_KDF_IMPLEMENTATION
#include "../crypto/hy_kdf.h"
#define HY_PAYLOAD_IMPLEMENTATION
#include "hy_payload.h"

hy_payload g_payload;
hy_string_table g_strings;

static pthread_mutex_t g_bridge_lock = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER;
static __thread JNIEnv *t_env = NULL;
static JavaVM *g_vm = NULL;
static jobject g_class_loader = NULL;
static jclass g_class_class = NULL;
static jmethodID g_load_class = NULL;

/* caches */
static jclass *g_type_cache = NULL;
static uint32_t g_type_cache_count = 0;
static struct { jclass cls; jmethodID mid; hy_method_entry me; uint8_t ok; } *g_mcache = NULL;
static uint32_t g_mcache_count = 0;
static struct { jclass cls; jfieldID fid; hy_field_entry fe; uint8_t ok; } *g_fcache = NULL;
static uint32_t g_fcache_count = 0;
static jstring *g_strcache = NULL;
static uint32_t g_strcache_count = 0;

#define HYJNI_LOCK() pthread_mutex_lock(&g_bridge_lock)
#define HYJNI_UNLOCK() pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_bridge_lock)

HY_HIDDEN static void hyjni_set_env(JNIEnv *env) {
    t_env = env;
}

HY_HIDDEN static int hyjni_raise_pending(JNIEnv *env) {
    return (*env)->ExceptionCheck(env) ? 1 : 0;
}

HY_HIDDEN static int hyjni_class_of(const uint8_t *desc, uint32_t desc_len,
                                     char *out, size_t out_cap) {
    /* "Lcom/foo/Bar;" -> "com.foo.Bar" */
    size_t i;
    size_t n = desc_len;
    if (n < 3 || n - 2u + 1u > out_cap) return -1;
    if (desc[0] != 'L' || desc[n - 1] != ';') return -1;
    n -= 2u;
    for (i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        char c = (char)desc[1 + i];
        if (c == '/') c = '.';
        if (c == '\0') return -1;
        out[i] = c;
    }
    out[n] = '\0';
    return 0;
}

HY_HIDDEN static jclass hyjni_load_class(const char *dotted) {
    JNIEnv *env = t_env;
    jstring name;
    jclass cls;

    if (env == NULL || g_class_loader == NULL || g_load_class == NULL) return NULL;
    name = (*env)->NewStringUTF(env, dotted);
    if (name == NULL) return NULL;
    cls = (jclass)(*env)->CallObjectMethod(env, g_class_loader, g_load_class, name);
    (*env)->DeleteLocalRef(env, name);
    return cls; /* local reference */
}

HY_HIDDEN static jclass hyjni_resolve_type(uint32_t type_idx) {
    jclass local, global;
    char dotted[512];
    const uint8_t *desc;
    uint32_t desc_len;
    uint32_t len;
    const uint8_t *types = hy_payload_find(&g_payload, HY_SEC_TYPES, &len);

    if (types == NULL) return NULL;
    if (type_idx >= hy_pay_le32(types)) return NULL;
    {
        uint32_t off = 4u;
        uint32_t i;
        const uint8_t *found = NULL;
        for (i = 0; i <= type_idx; i++) {
            uint32_t slen;
            if (off + 4u > len) return NULL;
            slen = hy_pay_le32(types + off);
            off += 4u;
            if ((uint64_t)off + slen > (uint64_t)len) return NULL;
            if (i == type_idx) { found = types + off; desc_len = slen; }
            off += slen;
        }
        if (found == NULL) return NULL;
        desc = found;
    }

    if (type_idx < g_type_cache_count && g_type_cache[type_idx] != NULL) {
        return g_type_cache[type_idx];
    }
    if (hyjni_class_of(desc, desc_len, dotted, sizeof(dotted)) != 0) return NULL;
    local = hyjni_load_class(dotted);
    if (local == NULL) return NULL;
    global = (jclass)(*t_env)->NewGlobalRef(t_env, local);
    (*t_env)->DeleteLocalRef(t_env, local);
    if (type_idx < g_type_cache_count) {
        g_type_cache[type_idx] = global;
    }
    return global;
}

HY_HIDDEN static jmethodID hyjni_method_id(uint32_t method_idx, jclass *cls, hy_method_entry *me) {
    hy_method_entry e;
    jclass resolved;
    const uint8_t *name;
    const uint8_t *sig;
    uint32_t nlen, slen;
    char name_buf[256];
    char sig_buf[512];
    jmethodID mid;

    if (method_idx >= g_mcache_count) return NULL;
    if (g_mcache == NULL) return NULL;
    if (g_mcache[method_idx].ok) {
        *cls = g_mcache[method_idx].cls;
        *me = g_mcache[method_idx].me;
        return g_mcache[method_idx].mid;
    }
    if (hy_method_get(&g_payload, method_idx, &e) != 0) return NULL;
    HYJNI_LOCK();
    if (g_mcache[method_idx].ok) {
        /* resolved by another thread while we waited */
        HYJNI_UNLOCK();
        *cls = g_mcache[method_idx].cls;
        *me = g_mcache[method_idx].me;
        return g_mcache[method_idx].mid;
    }
    resolved = hyjni_resolve_type(e.class_idx);
    if (resolved == NULL) { HYJNI_UNLOCK(); return NULL; }
    name = hy_string_get(&g_strings, e.name_idx, &nlen);
    sig = hy_string_get(&g_strings, e.sig_idx, &slen);
    if (name == NULL || sig == NULL || nlen >= sizeof(name_buf) || slen >= sizeof(sig_buf)) {
        HYJNI_UNLOCK();
        return NULL;
    }
    memcpy(name_buf, name, nlen); name_buf[nlen] = 0;
    memcpy(sig_buf, sig, slen); sig_buf[slen] = 0;

    if (e.kind == HYVM_INVOKE_STATIC) {
        mid = (*t_env)->GetStaticMethodID(t_env, resolved, name_buf, sig_buf);
    } else {
        mid = (*t_env)->GetMethodID(t_env, resolved, name_buf, sig_buf);
    }
    if (mid == NULL) {
        (*t_env)->ExceptionClear(t_env);
        HYJNI_UNLOCK();
        return NULL;
    }
    g_mcache[method_idx].cls = resolved;
    g_mcache[method_idx].mid = mid;
    g_mcache[method_idx].me = e;
    g_mcache[method_idx].ok = 1;
    HYJNI_UNLOCK();
    *cls = resolved;
    *me = e;
    return mid;
}

HY_HIDDEN static jfieldID hyjni_field_id(uint32_t field_idx, jclass *cls, hy_field_entry *fe) {
    hy_field_entry e;
    jclass resolved;
    const uint8_t *name;
    const uint8_t *sig;
    uint32_t nlen, slen;
    char name_buf[256];
    char type_buf[8];
    jfieldID fid;
    const char *sig_for_char;

    if (hy_field_get(&g_payload, field_idx, &e) != 0) return NULL;
    if (field_idx < g_fcache_count && g_fcache[field_idx].ok) {
        *cls = g_fcache[field_idx].cls;
        *fe = g_fcache[field_idx].fe;
        return g_fcache[field_idx].fid;
    }
    resolved = hyjni_resolve_type(e.class_idx);
    if (resolved == NULL) return NULL;
    name = hy_string_get(&g_strings, e.name_idx, &nlen);
    if (name == NULL || nlen >= sizeof(name_buf)) return NULL;
    memcpy(name_buf, name, nlen); name_buf[nlen] = 0;

    switch (e.type_char) {
        case 'Z': sig_for_char = "Z"; break;
        case 'B': sig_for_char = "B"; break;
        case 'C': sig_for_char = "C"; break;
        case 'S': sig_for_char = "S"; break;
        case 'I': sig_for_char = "I"; break;
        case 'J': sig_for_char = "J"; break;
        case 'F': sig_for_char = "F"; break;
        case 'D': sig_for_char = "D"; break;
        default: return NULL; /* object fields handled separately */
    }
    (void)type_buf;
    (void)sig;
    (void)slen;

    /* Object fields need their declared descriptor; the tool always emits
     * object field ids with type_char 'L' plus a real descriptor section is
     * not carried, so object field access is only supported through methods
     * in v1. Primitive fields only. */
    fid = (*t_env)->GetFieldID(t_env, resolved, name_buf, sig_for_char);
    if (fid == NULL) {
        (*t_env)->ExceptionClear(t_env);
        return NULL;
    }
    if (field_idx < g_fcache_count) {
        g_fcache[field_idx].cls = resolved;
        g_fcache[field_idx].fid = fid;
        g_fcache[field_idx].fe = e;
        g_fcache[field_idx].ok = 1;
    }
    *cls = resolved;
    *fe = e;
    return fid;
}

HY_HIDDEN static jstring hyjni_string(uint32_t id) {
    const uint8_t *bytes;
    uint32_t len;
    JNIEnv *env = t_env;
    jstring local, global;

    if (env == NULL) return NULL;
    if (id < g_strcache_count && g_strcache[id] != NULL) return g_strcache[id];
    bytes = hy_string_get(&g_strings, id, &len);
    if (bytes == NULL || len >= HY_STRING_MAX_BYTES) return NULL;

    /* Dex strings are MUTF-8, which JNI NewStringUTF accepts. But the bytes
     * are not NUL-terminated; build a local byte array and use String(byte[])
     * via the platform default is lossy for non-ASCII, so instead copy to a
     * stack buffer when short enough (dex utf8 strings are bounded). */
    if (len < 4096) {
        char buf[4096];
        memcpy(buf, bytes, len);
        buf[len] = '\0';
        local = (*env)->NewStringUTF(env, buf);
    } else {
        return NULL;
    }
    if (local == NULL) return NULL;
    if (id < g_strcache_count) {
        global = (*env)->NewGlobalRef(env, local);
        (*env)->DeleteLocalRef(env, local);
        g_strcache[id] = global;
        return global;
    }
    return local;
}

/* ---------------- HyVm bridge over JNI ---------------- */

static int jni_const_string(hyvm_ctx *c, uint32_t id, void **out) {
    (void)c;
    {
        jstring s = hyjni_string(id);
        if (s == NULL) return -1;
        *out = (void *)s;
        return 0;
    }
}

static int jni_new_instance(hyvm_ctx *c, uint32_t type_id, void **out) {
    (void)c;
    {
        jclass cls = hyjni_resolve_type(type_id);
        jobject o;
        if (cls == NULL) return -1;
        o = (*t_env)->NewObject(t_env, cls,
                                (*t_env)->GetMethodID(t_env, cls, "<init>", "()V"));
        if (o == NULL) return -1;
        *out = o;
        return 0;
    }
}

static int jni_get_field(hyvm_ctx *c, uint32_t field_id, void *obj, hyvm_val *out, char is_wide) {
    (void)c; (void)is_wide;
    {
        jclass cls;
        hy_field_entry fe;
        jfieldID fid = hyjni_field_id(field_id, &cls, &fe);
        if (fid == NULL || obj == NULL) return -1;
        switch (fe.type_char) {
            case 'J': out->i = (*t_env)->GetLongField(t_env, obj, fid); return 0;
            case 'I': out->i = (*t_env)->GetIntField(t_env, obj, fid); return 0;
            case 'Z': case 'B': case 'C': case 'S':
                out->i = (*t_env)->GetIntField(t_env, obj, fid); return 0;
            case 'F': out->d = (*t_env)->GetFloatField(t_env, obj, fid); return 0;
            case 'D': out->d = (*t_env)->GetDoubleField(t_env, obj, fid); return 0;
            default: return -1;
        }
    }
}

static int jni_put_field(hyvm_ctx *c, uint32_t field_id, void *obj, hyvm_val v, char is_wide) {
    (void)c; (void)is_wide;
    {
        jclass cls;
        hy_field_entry fe;
        jfieldID fid = hyjni_field_id(field_id, &cls, &fe);
        if (fid == NULL || obj == NULL) return -1;
        switch (fe.type_char) {
            case 'J': (*t_env)->SetLongField(t_env, obj, fid, (jlong)v.i); return 0;
            case 'I': (*t_env)->SetIntField(t_env, obj, fid, (jint)(int32_t)v.i); return 0;
            case 'Z': case 'B': case 'C': case 'S':
                (*t_env)->SetIntField(t_env, obj, fid, (jint)(int32_t)v.i); return 0;
            case 'F': (*t_env)->SetFloatField(t_env, obj, fid, (jfloat)v.d); return 0;
            case 'D': (*t_env)->SetDoubleField(t_env, obj, fid, (jdouble)v.d); return 0;
            default: return -1;
        }
    }
}

static int jni_get_static(hyvm_ctx *c, uint32_t field_id, hyvm_val *out, char is_wide) {
    (void)c; (void)is_wide;
    {
        jclass cls;
        hy_field_entry fe;
        jfieldID fid = hyjni_field_id(field_id, &cls, &fe);
        if (fid == NULL) return -1;
        switch (fe.type_char) {
            case 'J': out->i = (*t_env)->GetStaticLongField(t_env, cls, fid); return 0;
            case 'I': out->i = (*t_env)->GetStaticIntField(t_env, cls, fid); return 0;
            case 'F': out->d = (*t_env)->GetStaticFloatField(t_env, cls, fid); return 0;
            case 'D': out->d = (*t_env)->GetStaticDoubleField(t_env, cls, fid); return 0;
            default: return -1;
        }
    }
}

static int jni_put_static(hyvm_ctx *c, uint32_t field_id, hyvm_val v, char is_wide) {
    (void)c; (void)is_wide;
    {
        jclass cls;
        hy_field_entry fe;
        jfieldID fid = hyjni_field_id(field_id, &cls, &fe);
        if (fid == NULL) return -1;
        switch (fe.type_char) {
            case 'J': (*t_env)->SetStaticLongField(t_env, cls, fid, (jlong)v.i); return 0;
            case 'I': (*t_env)->SetStaticIntField(t_env, cls, fid, (jint)(int32_t)v.i); return 0;
            case 'F': (*t_env)->SetStaticFloatField(t_env, cls, fid, (jfloat)v.d); return 0;
            case 'D': (*t_env)->SetStaticDoubleField(t_env, cls, fid, (jdouble)v.d); return 0;
            default: return -1;
        }
    }
}

static int jni_invoke(hyvm_ctx *c, uint32_t method_id, uint8_t kind,
                      void *self, const hyvm_val *args, uint8_t argc,
                      hyvm_val *ret, char *pending_exception) {
    (void)c;
    {
        jclass cls;
        hy_method_entry me;
        jmethodID mid = hyjni_method_id(method_id, &cls, &me);
        jvalue jargs[8];
        uint8_t i;
        char retc;

        if (mid == NULL) return -1;
        if (argc > 8) return -1; /* jargs is fixed at 8 */
        if (me.sig_idx == 0) { /* unreachable */ }
        retc = me.ret;
        if (retc != 'L' && retc != '[' && retc != 'V' &&
            retc != 'Z' && retc != 'B' && retc != 'C' && retc != 'S' &&
            retc != 'I' && retc != 'J' && retc != 'F' && retc != 'D') {
            return -1;
        }
        for (i = 0; i < argc && i < 8; i++) {
            jargs[i].l = NULL; /* zero the union first */
            switch (i == 0 && kind != HYVM_INVOKE_STATIC ? 0 : 1) {
                default: break;
            }
            jargs[i].j = args[i].i; /* register values carry raw bits */
        }
        /* NOTE: the emitter guarantees args are already marshalled to the
         * right jvalue union slot: primitives carry i/d, objects carry o.
         * Because hyvm_val is a union and jvalue is a union, copying the
         * 8-byte representation preserves the bits for every type. */

        if (kind == HYVM_INVOKE_STATIC) {
            switch (retc) {
                case 'V': (*t_env)->CallStaticVoidMethodA(t_env, cls, mid, jargs); break;
                case 'J': ret->i = (*t_env)->CallStaticLongMethodA(t_env, cls, mid, jargs); break;
                case 'I': ret->i = (*t_env)->CallStaticIntMethodA(t_env, cls, mid, jargs); break;
                case 'Z': case 'B': case 'C': case 'S':
                    ret->i = (*t_env)->CallStaticIntMethodA(t_env, cls, mid, jargs); break;
                case 'F': ret->d = (*t_env)->CallStaticFloatMethodA(t_env, cls, mid, jargs); break;
                case 'D': ret->d = (*t_env)->CallStaticDoubleMethodA(t_env, cls, mid, jargs); break;
                default: ret->o = (*t_env)->CallStaticObjectMethodA(t_env, cls, mid, jargs); break;
            }
        } else {
            if (self == NULL) return -1;
            switch (retc) {
                case 'V': (*t_env)->CallVoidMethodA(t_env, self, mid, jargs); break;
                case 'J': ret->i = (*t_env)->CallLongMethodA(t_env, self, mid, jargs); break;
                case 'I': ret->i = (*t_env)->CallIntMethodA(t_env, self, mid, jargs); break;
                case 'Z': case 'B': case 'C': case 'S':
                    ret->i = (*t_env)->CallIntMethodA(t_env, self, mid, jargs); break;
                case 'F': ret->d = (*t_env)->CallFloatMethodA(t_env, self, mid, jargs); break;
                case 'D': ret->d = (*t_env)->CallDoubleMethodA(t_env, self, mid, jargs); break;
                default: ret->o = (*t_env)->CallObjectMethodA(t_env, self, mid, jargs); break;
            }
        }
        if (hyjni_raise_pending(t_env)) {
            *pending_exception = 1;
        }
        return 0;
    }
}

static int jni_new_array(hyvm_ctx *c, uint8_t elem_tag, int64_t length, void **out) {
    (void)c;
    if (length < 0 || length > (1 << 26)) return -1;
    switch (elem_tag) {
        case 0: *out = (*t_env)->NewLongArray(t_env, (jsize)length); break;
        case 1: *out = (*t_env)->NewIntArray(t_env, (jsize)length); break;
        case 2: *out = (*t_env)->NewDoubleArray(t_env, (jsize)length); break;
        case 3: *out = (*t_env)->NewObjectArray(t_env, (jsize)length,
                        (*t_env)->FindClass(t_env, "java/lang/Object"), NULL); break;
        default: return -1;
    }
    return *out != NULL ? 0 : -1;
}

static int jni_array_get(hyvm_ctx *c, void *arr, int64_t index, hyvm_val *out) {
    (void)c;
    if (arr == NULL || index < 0) return -1;
    /* We do not track the array element type here; the emitter emits typed
     * sequences via helper calls instead of generic AGET for primitives. */
    {
        jlong v = 0;
        (*t_env)->GetLongArrayRegion(t_env, arr, (jsize)index, 1, &v);
        if ((*t_env)->ExceptionCheck(t_env)) { (*t_env)->ExceptionClear(t_env); return -1; }
        out->i = v;
        return 0;
    }
}

static int jni_array_put(hyvm_ctx *c, void *arr, int64_t index, hyvm_val v) {
    (void)c;
    if (arr == NULL || index < 0) return -1;
    {
        jlong lv = v.i;
        (*t_env)->SetLongArrayRegion(t_env, arr, (jsize)index, 1, &lv);
        if ((*t_env)->ExceptionCheck(t_env)) { (*t_env)->ExceptionClear(t_env); return -1; }
        return 0;
    }
}

static int jni_array_length(hyvm_ctx *c, void *arr, int64_t *out) {
    (void)c;
    if (arr == NULL) return -1;
    *out = (*t_env)->GetArrayLength(t_env, arr);
    return 0;
}

static int jni_check_cast(hyvm_ctx *c, void *obj, uint32_t type_id, void **out) {
    (void)c;
    {
        jclass cls = hyjni_resolve_type(type_id);
        if (cls == NULL || obj == NULL) return -1;
        if ((*t_env)->IsInstanceOf(t_env, obj, cls) == JNI_FALSE) {
            return -1; /* ClassCastException semantics: reported as failure */
        }
        *out = obj;
        return 0;
    }
}

static int jni_instance_of(hyvm_ctx *c, void *obj, uint32_t type_id, int64_t *out) {
    (void)c;
    {
        jclass cls = hyjni_resolve_type(type_id);
        if (cls == NULL) return -1;
        *out = (obj != NULL) && ((*t_env)->IsInstanceOf(t_env, obj, cls) == JNI_TRUE);
        return 0;
    }
}

static int jni_throw(hyvm_ctx *c, void *obj) {
    (void)c;
    if (obj == NULL) return -1;
    return (*t_env)->Throw(t_env, obj) == 0 ? 0 : -1;
}

static int jni_monitor(hyvm_ctx *c, void *obj, uint8_t mode) {
    (void)c;
    if (obj == NULL) return -1;
    if (mode == 0) {
        return (*t_env)->MonitorEnter(t_env, obj) == 0 ? 0 : -1;
    }
    return (*t_env)->MonitorExit(t_env, obj) == 0 ? 0 : -1;
}

const hyvm_bridge hyjni_bridge = {
    jni_const_string,
    jni_new_instance,
    jni_get_field,
    jni_put_field,
    jni_get_static,
    jni_put_static,
    jni_invoke,
    jni_new_array,
    jni_array_get,
    jni_array_put,
    jni_array_length,
    jni_check_cast,
    jni_instance_of,
    jni_throw,
    jni_monitor,
    NULL /* release_ref: JNI local refs are managed by the frame */
};

/* ---------------- payload init ---------------- */

/* lib_secret is stored XOR-masked in the generated blob to avoid a plaintext
 * key in .rodata; the mask is fixed by the tool. */
#define HY_SECRET_MASK 0xA5

HY_HIDDEN static int hyjni_payload_init(void) {
    const uint8_t *p = hyapk_payload_blob;
    uint32_t size = hyapk_payload_blob_size;
    uint8_t lib_secret[32];
    uint8_t key[32];
    uint8_t *plain;
    uint32_t plain_size;
    unsigned i;
    int rc = -1;
    uint32_t flags;

    if (size < HY_PAYLOAD_HEADER_SIZE) return -1;
    if (hy_pay_le32(p) != HY_PAYLOAD_MAGIC) return -1;
    if (hy_pay_le16(p + 4) != HY_PAYLOAD_VERSION) return -1;
    plain_size = hy_pay_le32(p + 8);
    if ((uint64_t)HY_PAYLOAD_HEADER_SIZE + (uint64_t)plain_size != (uint64_t)size) return -1;
    if (plain_size == 0 || plain_size > HY_PAYLOAD_MAX_BYTES) return -1;

    for (i = 0; i < 32; i++) {
        lib_secret[i] = (uint8_t)(hyapk_lib_secret[i] ^ (HY_SECRET_MASK + i));
    }

    plain = (uint8_t *)malloc(plain_size);
    if (plain == NULL) return -1;

    /* header layout: magic(4) version(2) flags(2) size(4) nonce(12) salt(32) tag(16) */
    hy_derive_payload_key(lib_secret, p + 24, key);
    if (hy_aead_decrypt(key, p + 12, p, 12u,
                        p + HY_PAYLOAD_HEADER_SIZE, plain_size,
                        p + 56, plain) == 0) {
        g_payload.data = plain;
        g_payload.size = plain_size;
        memset((void *)&g_payload.sections, 0, sizeof(g_payload.sections));
        rc = hy_strings_init(&g_strings, &g_payload);
        if (rc == 0) {
            uint32_t len;
            const uint8_t *sec;
            uint16_t count = hy_pay_le16(plain);
            uint32_t off = 2u;
            uint16_t si;
            for (si = 0; si < count; si++) {
                uint16_t sid;
                uint32_t soff, slen;
                if (off + 10u > plain_size) break;
                sid = hy_pay_le16(plain + off);
                soff = hy_pay_le32(plain + off + 2);
                slen = hy_pay_le32(plain + off + 6);
                off += 10u;
                if ((uint64_t)soff + slen <= (uint64_t)plain_size && sid < 16) {
                    g_payload.sections[sid] = plain + soff;
                    g_payload.section_sizes[sid] = slen;
                }
            }
            /* allocate caches */
            sec = hy_payload_find(&g_payload, HY_SEC_TYPES, &len);
            g_type_cache_count = sec ? hy_pay_le32(sec) : 0;
            sec = hy_payload_find(&g_payload, HY_SEC_METHODS, &len);
            g_mcache_count = sec ? hy_pay_le32(sec) : 0;
            sec = hy_payload_find(&g_payload, HY_SEC_FIELDS, &len);
            g_fcache_count = sec ? hy_pay_le32(sec) : 0;
            g_strcache_count = g_strings.count;

            if (g_type_cache_count) g_type_cache = calloc(g_type_cache_count, sizeof(jclass));
            if (g_mcache_count) g_mcache = calloc(g_mcache_count, sizeof(*g_mcache));
            if (g_fcache_count) g_fcache = calloc(g_fcache_count, sizeof(*g_fcache));
            if (g_strcache_count) g_strcache = calloc(g_strcache_count, sizeof(jstring));
            if ((g_type_cache_count && g_type_cache == NULL) ||
                (g_mcache_count && g_mcache == NULL) ||
                (g_fcache_count && g_fcache == NULL) ||
                (g_strcache_count && g_strcache == NULL)) {
                free(plain);
                g_payload.data = NULL;
                rc = -1;
            }
        } else {
            free(plain);
            g_payload.data = NULL;
        }
    } else {
        free(plain);
    }

    (void)flags;
    hy_secure_zero(key, sizeof(key));
    hy_secure_zero(lib_secret, sizeof(lib_secret));
    return rc;
}

/* Keeps a strong reference to the application class loader. */
HY_HIDDEN static void hyjni_cache_loader(JNIEnv *env, jobject context) {
    jobject loader_local = NULL;

    if (context == NULL) return;
    g_class_class = (jclass)(*env)->NewGlobalRef(env, (*env)->FindClass(env, "java/lang/Class"));
    if (g_class_class == NULL) {
        (*env)->ExceptionClear(env);
        return;
    }
    g_load_class = (*env)->GetMethodID(env, g_class_class, "loadClass",
                                       "(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/Class;");
    if (g_load_class == NULL) {
        (*env)->ExceptionClear(env);
        return;
    }
    {
        jclass ctx_cls = (*env)->GetObjectClass(env, context);
        jmethodID get_loader = (*env)->GetMethodID(env, ctx_cls, "getClassLoader",
                                                   "()Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;");
        if (get_loader != NULL) {
            loader_local = (*env)->CallObjectMethod(env, context, get_loader);
        }
    }
    if (loader_local != NULL) {
        g_class_loader = (*env)->NewGlobalRef(env, loader_local);
        (*env)->DeleteLocalRef(env, loader_local);
    }
}

#endif /* HY_BRIDGE_JNI_IMPLEMENTATION */

```

```h
/*
 * Hyapk generated-code API: the functions available to the C sources that

/* Bridge operations mirroring the HyVm bridge. All set *ok to 0 on failure
 * (a pending exception, if any, propagates at the JNI boundary). */

                  const hyvm_val *args, unsigned char argc, hyvm_val *ret);
```

`hyapk-native/src/jni/hy_jni.c`:

```c
/*
 * Hyapk runtime library entry point (unity build).
 * Implements JNI_OnLoad, the injected bridge methods, the guard thread,
 * signature verification and dex tamper checks, plus the shared entry used
 * by generated dex2c/VMP thunks.
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: AGPL-3.0-only
 */
#define HY_SHA256_IMPLEMENTATION
#include "crypto/hy_sha256.h"
#define HY_CHACHA20POLY1305_IMPLEMENTATION
#include "crypto/hy_chacha20poly1305.h"
#define HY_PAYLOAD_KDF_IMPLEMENTATION
#include "crypto/hy_kdf.h"
#define HY_PROTECT_IMPLEMENTATION
#include "protect/hy_protect.h"
#define HYVM_IMPLEMENTATION
#include "vm/hyvm.h"
#define HY_PAYLOAD_IMPLEMENTATION
#include "jni/hy_payload.h"
#define HY_BRIDGE_JNI_IMPLEMENTATION
#include "jni/hy_bridge_jni.h"

#include <jni.h>
#include <pthread.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unistd.h>

#ifdef __ANDROID__
#include <android/log.h>
#endif

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* state                                                               */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

static jobject g_context = NULL;
static volatile int g_initialized = 0;
static volatile int g_guard_stop = 0;
static pthread_t g_guard_thread;
static unsigned g_guard_interval = HY_GUARD_INTERVAL_SECONDS;
static int g_skip_root = 0;
static int g_skip_emu = 0;
static int g_enforce_signature = 1;
static int g_enforce_dexhash = 1;

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* reaction policy                                                     */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

static void hy_react(const char *reason) {
#ifdef __ANDROID__
    __android_log_print(ANDROID_LOG_ERROR, "hyapk", "protection violation: %s", reason);
#else
    (void)reason;
#endif
    /* Policy: hard exit. Custom callbacks can be added by hooking
     * hy_react via a build flag; the default is the safest posture. */
    _exit(1);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* zip reading (stored entries only; the packer stores dex uncompressed)  */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

static uint32_t hy_le32b(const uint8_t *p) {
    return (uint32_t)((p[0] << 24) | (p[1] << 16) | (p[2] << 8) | p[3]); /* big-endian zip */
}

/* Reads a stored (uncompressed) entry from a zip file image.
 * Returns malloc'd buffer + size, or NULL. */
static uint8_t *hy_zip_read_entry(const uint8_t *image, size_t image_size,
                                  const char *name, uint32_t *out_size) {
    size_t i;
    if (image_size < 22) return NULL;

    /* find EOCD */
    for (i = image_size - 22u + 1u; i-- > 0;) {
        if (image[i] == 'P' && image[i + 1] == 'K' && image[i + 2] == 5 && image[i + 3] == 6) {
            uint32_t cd_entries, cd_size, cd_offset;
            uint32_t e;
            (void)cd_size;
            if (i + 22u > image_size) return NULL;
            cd_entries = hy_le32b(image + i + 10);
            cd_offset = hy_le32b(image + i + 16);
            if ((uint64_t)cd_offset >= image_size) return NULL;
            e = cd_offset;
            while (cd_entries-- > 0) {
                uint32_t method, comp_size, uncomp_size, name_len, extra_len, desc_len, local_off;
                if (e + 46u > image_size) return NULL;
                if (image[e] != 'P' || image[e + 1] != 'K') return NULL;
                method = hy_le32b(image + e + 10);
                comp_size = hy_le32b(image + e + 20);
                uncomp_size = hy_le32b(image + e + 24);
                name_len = (uint32_t)image[e + 28] | ((uint32_t)image[e + 29] << 8);
                extra_len = (uint32_t)image[e + 30] | ((uint32_t)image[e + 31] << 8);
                desc_len = (uint32_t)image[e + 32] | ((uint32_t)image[e + 33] << 8);
                local_off = hy_le32b(image + e + 42);
                if (e + 46u + name_len > image_size) return NULL;
                if ((size_t)name_len == strlen(name) &&
                    memcmp(image + e + 46, name, name_len) == 0) {
                    if (method != 0 || comp_size != uncomp_size) return NULL; /* must be stored */
                    if ((uint64_t)local_off + 30u + name_len + uncomp_size > (uint64_t)image_size) return NULL;
                    /* verify local header and find data start */
                    {
                        const uint8_t *lh = image + local_off;
                        uint32_t lh_name, lh_extra;
                        if (lh[0] != 'P' || lh[1] != 'K') return NULL;
                        lh_name = (uint32_t)lh[26] | ((uint32_t)lh[27] << 8);
                        lh_extra = (uint32_t)lh[28] | ((uint32_t)lh[29] << 8);
                        {
                            const uint8_t *data = lh + 30u + lh_name + lh_extra;
                            uint8_t *copy;
                            if ((uint64_t)(data - image) + uncomp_size > (uint64_t)image_size) return NULL;
                            copy = (uint8_t *)malloc(uncomp_size ? uncomp_size : 1u);
                            if (copy == NULL) return NULL;
                            memcpy(copy, data, uncomp_size);
                            *out_size = uncomp_size;
                            return copy;
                        }
                    }
                }
                e += 46u + name_len + extra_len + desc_len;
            }
            return NULL;
        }
    }
    return NULL;
}

static uint8_t *hy_read_whole_file(const char *path, size_t *out_size) {
    FILE *f = fopen(path, "rb");
    uint8_t *buf;
    long size;
    if (f == NULL) return NULL;
    if (fseek(f, 0, SEEK_END) != 0) { fclose(f); return NULL; }
    size = ftell(f);
    if (size < 0 || fseek(f, 0, SEEK_SET) != 0) { fclose(f); return NULL; }
    buf = (uint8_t *)malloc((size_t)size + 1u);
    if (buf == NULL) { fclose(f); return NULL; }
    if (size > 0 && fread(buf, 1, (size_t)size, f) != (size_t)size) {
        free(buf);
        fclose(f);
        return NULL;
    }
    fclose(f);
    *out_size = (size_t)size;
    return buf;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* tamper: dex hash verification                                       */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

static int hy_verify_dex_hashes(const char *apk_path) {
    uint32_t sec_len = 0;
    const uint8_t *sec = hy_payload_find(&g_payload, HY_SEC_DEXHASH, &sec_len);
    size_t image_size = 0;
    uint8_t *image;
    int ok = 1;

    if (sec == NULL || sec_len < 4u) return 1; /* nothing enforced */
    {
        uint32_t count = hy_pay_le32(sec);
        uint32_t off = 4u;
        uint32_t i;
        if (count == 0) return 1;
        image = hy_read_whole_file(apk_path, &image_size);
        if (image == NULL) return 0;
        for (i = 0; i < count && ok; i++) {
            char name[64];
            uint8_t digest[32];
            uint32_t dex_size = 0;
            uint8_t *dex;
            uint8_t computed[32];
            uint32_t name_idx;
            const uint8_t *nbytes;
            uint32_t nlen;
            int truncated;

            if (off + 4u + 32u > sec_len) { ok = 0; break; }
            name_idx = hy_pay_le32(sec + off);
            memcpy(digest, sec + off + 4, 32);
            off += 36u;

            nbytes = hy_string_get(&g_strings, name_idx, &nlen);
            if (nbytes == NULL || nlen >= sizeof(name)) { ok = 0; break; }
            memcpy(name, nbytes, nlen);
            name[nlen] = 0;

            dex = hy_zip_read_entry(image, image_size, name, &dex_size);
            if (dex == NULL) { ok = 0; break; }
            hy_sha256(dex, dex_size, computed);
            truncated = hy_ct_memcmp(computed, digest, 32) != 0;
            free(dex);
            if (truncated) { ok = 0; }
        }
        hy_secure_zero(image, image_size);
        free(image);
    }
    return ok;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* signature verification                                              */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

static int hy_verify_signature(JNIEnv *env, jobject context) {
    uint8_t digest[32];
    uint32_t sec_len = 0;
    const uint8_t *sec = hy_payload_find(&g_payload, HY_SEC_CERTDIGEST, &sec_len);
    jclass ctx_cls, pm_cls, pkg_cls;
    jmethodID get_pm, get_pkg;
    jobject pm, info;
    jmethodID get_sig, to_bytes;
    jobject sig_obj;
    jbyteArray cert_bytes;
    jsize cert_len;
    uint8_t *copy;
    uint8_t computed[32];
    int ok = 0;

    if (sec == NULL || sec_len < 32u) return 1; /* nothing enforced */
    memcpy(digest, sec, 32);

    if (context == NULL) return 0;
    ctx_cls = (*env)->GetObjectClass(env, context);
    get_pm = (*env)->GetMethodID(env, ctx_cls, "getPackageManager",
                                 "()Landroid/content/pm/PackageManager;");
    if (get_pm == NULL) return 0;
    pm = (*env)->CallObjectMethod(env, context, get_pm);
    if (pm == NULL) return 0;

    pm_cls = (*env)->GetObjectClass(env, pm);
    get_pkg = (*env)->GetMethodID(env, pm_cls, "getPackageInfo",
                                  "(Ljava/lang/String;I)Landroid/content/pm/PackageInfo;");
    if (get_pkg == NULL) return 0;
    {
        jclass ctx2 = (*env)->GetObjectClass(env, context);
        jmethodID get_name = (*env)->GetMethodID(env, ctx2, "getPackageName",
                                                 "()Ljava/lang/String;");
        jstring pkg_name = (*env)->CallObjectMethod(env, context, get_name);
        /* GET_SIGNATURES (0x40): still honored on all supported API levels */
        info = (*env)->CallObjectMethod(env, pm, get_pkg, pkg_name, (jint)0x40);
        if ((*env)->ExceptionCheck(env)) {
            (*env)->ExceptionClear(env);
            return 0;
        }
    }
    if (info == NULL) return 0;

    pkg_cls = (*env)->GetObjectClass(env, info);
    {
        jfieldID fid = (*env)->GetFieldID(env, pkg_cls, "signatures",
                                          "[Landroid/content/pm/Signature;");
        jobjectArray sigs;
        if (fid == NULL) {
            (*env)->ExceptionClear(env);
            return 0;
        }
        sigs = (jobjectArray)(*env)->GetObjectField(env, info, fid);
        if (sigs == NULL || (*env)->GetArrayLength(env, sigs) == 0) return 0;
        sig_obj = (*env)->GetObjectArrayElement(env, sigs, 0);
    }
    if (sig_obj == NULL) return 0;
    {
        jclass sig_cls = (*env)->GetObjectClass(env, sig_obj);
        to_bytes = (*env)->GetMethodID(env, sig_cls, "toByteArray", "()[B");
        if (to_bytes == NULL) return 0;
    }
    cert_bytes = (jbyteArray)(*env)->CallObjectMethod(env, sig_obj, to_bytes);
    if (cert_bytes == NULL) return 0;

    cert_len = (*env)->GetArrayLength(env, cert_bytes);
    copy = (uint8_t *)malloc((size_t)cert_len);
    if (copy == NULL) return 0;
    (*env)->GetByteArrayRegion(env, cert_bytes, 0, cert_len, (jbyte *)copy);
    hy_sha256(copy, (size_t)cert_len, computed);
    hy_secure_zero(copy, (size_t)cert_len);
    free(copy);

    ok = hy_ct_memcmp(computed, digest, 32) == 0;
    hy_secure_zero(computed, sizeof(computed));
    return ok;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* guard thread                                                        */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

static void *hy_guard_main(void *arg) {
    (void)arg;
    while (!g_guard_stop) {
        hy_detect_result r = hy_detect_all(g_skip_root, g_skip_emu);
        if (r.anti_debug) hy_react("debugger");
        if (r.anti_frida) hy_react("frida");
        if (r.anti_root) hy_react("root");
        /* Emulator is informational; it does not trigger the hard policy. */
        {
            unsigned interval = g_guard_interval > 2 ? (unsigned)(g_guard_interval - (rand() % 3)) : 2u;
            sleep(interval);
        }
    }
    return NULL;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* assets decryption                                                   */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

#ifdef __ANDROID__
#include <android/asset_manager.h>
#include <android/asset_manager_jni.h>

static jobject hy_open_asset(JNIEnv *env, jstring name) {
    const char *orig = (*env)->GetStringUTFChars(env, name, NULL);
    uint32_t sec_len = 0;
    const uint8_t *sec = hy_payload_find(&g_payload, HY_SEC_ASSETMAP, &sec_len);
    jobject result = NULL;

    if (orig != NULL && sec != NULL && sec_len >= 4u && g_context != NULL) {
        uint32_t count = hy_pay_le32(sec);
        uint32_t off = 4u;
        uint32_t i;
        size_t orig_len = strlen(orig);
        for (i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            uint32_t orig_idx;
            char stored[17];
            uint8_t seed[32];
            uint8_t nonce[12];
            uint8_t tag[16];
            uint32_t size;
            const uint8_t *oname;
            uint32_t onlen;

            /* entry layout: orig_idx u32 | stored[16] | seed[32] | nonce[12] | tag[16] | size u32 */
            if (off + 84u > sec_len) break;
            orig_idx = hy_pay_le32(sec + off); off += 4;
            memcpy(stored, sec + off, 16); stored[16] = '\0'; off += 16;
            memcpy(seed, sec + off, 32); off += 32;
            memcpy(nonce, sec + off, 12); off += 12;
            memcpy(tag, sec + off, 16); off += 16;
            size = hy_pay_le32(sec + off); off += 4;

            if (size < 16u || size > HY_PAYLOAD_MAX_BYTES) continue;

            oname = hy_string_get(&g_strings, orig_idx, &onlen);
            if (oname != NULL && onlen == orig_len && memcmp(oname, orig, orig_len) == 0) {
                /* decrypt from AAssetManager */
                jobject am_obj;
                jclass ctx_cls;
                jmethodID get_assets;
                AAssetManager *am;
                AAsset *asset;
                uint8_t key[32];
                uint8_t lib_secret[32];
                uint8_t *ct;
                uint8_t *pt;
                unsigned k;

                ctx_cls = (*env)->GetObjectClass(env, g_context);
                get_assets = (*env)->GetMethodID(env, ctx_cls, "getAssets",
                                                 "()Landroid/content/res/AssetManager;");
                am_obj = (*env)->CallObjectMethod(env, g_context, get_assets);
                am = AAssetManager_fromJava(env, am_obj);
                asset = am ? AAssetManager_open(am, (const char *)stored, AASSET_MODE_BUFFER) : NULL;
                if (asset == NULL) {
                    (*env)->ReleaseStringUTFChars(env, name, orig);
                    return NULL;
                }
                ct = (uint8_t *)malloc(size);
                if (ct == NULL) {
                    AAsset_close(asset);
                    (*env)->ReleaseStringUTFChars(env, name, orig);
                    return NULL;
                }
                {
                    int rd = AAsset_read(asset, ct, size);
                    AAsset_close(asset);
                    if (rd != (int)size) {
                        free(ct);
                        (*env)->ReleaseStringUTFChars(env, name, orig);
                        return NULL;
                    }
                }
                pt = (uint8_t *)malloc(size - 16u);
                if (pt == NULL) {
                    free(ct);
                    (*env)->ReleaseStringUTFChars(env, name, orig);
                    return NULL;
                }
                for (k = 0; k < 32; k++) {
                    lib_secret[k] = (uint8_t)(hyapk_lib_secret[k] ^ (HY_SECRET_MASK + k));
                }
                hy_derive_payload_key(lib_secret, seed, key);
                if (hy_aead_decrypt(key, nonce, NULL, 0, ct, size - 16u, tag, pt) == 0) {
                    jbyteArray arr = (*env)->NewByteArray(env, (jsize)(size - 16u));
                    jclass bais_cls;
                    jmethodID ctor;
                    if (arr != NULL) {
                        (*env)->SetByteArrayRegion(env, arr, 0, (jsize)(size - 16u), (const jbyte *)pt);
                        bais_cls = (*env)->FindClass(env, "java/io/ByteArrayInputStream");
                        if (bais_cls != NULL) {
                            ctor = (*env)->GetMethodID(env, bais_cls, "<init>", "([B)V");
                            if (ctor != NULL) {
                                result = (*env)->NewObject(env, bais_cls, ctor, arr);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                hy_secure_zero(key, sizeof(key));
                hy_secure_zero(lib_secret, sizeof(lib_secret));
                free(ct);
                free(pt);
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    if (orig != NULL) (*env)->ReleaseStringUTFChars(env, name, orig);
    return result;
}
#endif /* __ANDROID__ */

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* injected native methods                                             */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

static jstring native_sec_string(JNIEnv *env, jclass cls, jint id) {
    (void)cls;
    hyjni_set_env(env);
    return hyjni_string((uint32_t)id);
}

static void native_bridge_init(JNIEnv *env, jclass cls, jobject context) {
    (void)cls;
    hyjni_set_env(env);
    if (g_initialized) return;
    g_initialized = 1;

    if (context != NULL) {
        g_context = (*env)->NewGlobalRef(env, context);
        hyjni_cache_loader(env, context);
    }

    /* config */
    {
        uint32_t sec_len = 0;
        const uint8_t *sec = hy_payload_find(&g_payload, HY_SEC_CONFIG, &sec_len);
        if (sec != NULL && sec_len >= 8u) {
            g_skip_root = sec[0];
            g_skip_emu = sec[1];
            g_guard_interval = hy_pay_le32(sec + 4);
            if (g_guard_interval < 4) g_guard_interval = 4;
            if (g_guard_interval > 600) g_guard_interval = 600;
        }
    }

    /* signature verification */
    if (g_enforce_signature) {
        if (!hy_verify_signature(env, context)) {
            hy_react("signature");
        }
    }

    /* dex hashes */
    if (g_enforce_dexhash && context != NULL) {
        /* sourceDir via ApplicationInfo */
        jclass ctx_cls = (*env)->GetObjectClass(env, context);
        jmethodID get_ai = (*env)->GetMethodID(env, ctx_cls, "getApplicationInfo",
                                               "()Landroid/content/pm/ApplicationInfo;");
        if (get_ai != NULL) {
            jobject ai = (*env)->CallObjectMethod(env, context, get_ai);
            jclass ai_cls = (*env)->GetObjectClass(env, ai);
            jfieldID src_fid = (*env)->GetFieldID(env, ai_cls, "sourceDir",
                                                  "Ljava/lang/String;");
            if (src_fid != NULL) {
                jstring src = (jstring)(*env)->GetObjectField(env, ai, src_fid);
                const char *path = (*env)->GetStringUTFChars(env, src, NULL);
                if (path != NULL) {
                    if (!hy_verify_dex_hashes(path)) {
                        hy_react("dexhash");
                    }
                    (*env)->ReleaseStringUTFChars(env, src, path);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /* guard thread */
    if (pthread_create(&g_guard_thread, NULL, hy_guard_main, NULL) == 0) {
        pthread_detach(g_guard_thread);
    }
}

#ifdef __ANDROID__
static jobject native_bridge_asset(JNIEnv *env, jclass cls, jstring name) {
    (void)cls;
    hyjni_set_env(env);
    return hy_open_asset(env, name);
}
#endif

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* shared entry for generated VMP thunks                               */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

static uint32_t hy_pay_le32_local(const uint8_t *p) {
    return (uint32_t)p[0] | ((uint32_t)p[1] << 8) | ((uint32_t)p[2] << 16) | ((uint32_t)p[3] << 24);
}

/* Finds the program for a method in the VMPROG section. Layout per entry:
 * method_idx u32 | program_len u32 | program bytes. */
static const uint8_t *hy_find_program(uint32_t method_idx, uint32_t *len) {
    uint32_t sec_len = 0;
    const uint8_t *sec = hy_payload_find(&g_payload, HY_SEC_VMPROG, &sec_len);
    if (sec == NULL || sec_len < 4u) return NULL;
    {
        uint32_t count = hy_pay_le32(sec);
        uint32_t off = 4u;
        uint32_t i;
        for (i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            uint32_t mid, plen;
            if (off + 8u > sec_len) return NULL;
            mid = hy_pay_le32(sec + off);
            plen = hy_pay_le32(sec + off + 4);
            off += 8u;
            if ((uint64_t)off + plen > (uint64_t)sec_len) return NULL;
            if (mid == method_idx) {
                *len = plen;
                return sec + off;
            }
            off += plen;
        }
    }
    return NULL;
}

/* Runs the VMP program bound to a method index. Marshals arguments from the
 * generated thunk. */
static int hy_run_vm_program(JNIEnv *env, uint32_t method_idx,
                              const hyvm_val *args, uint8_t argc, hyvm_val *ret) {
    uint32_t plen = 0;
    const uint8_t *prog = hy_find_program(method_idx, &plen);
    hyvm_run_spec spec;
    uint8_t lib_secret[32];
    unsigned i;
    int rc;

    if (prog == NULL) return -1;
    for (i = 0; i < 32; i++) {
        lib_secret[i] = (uint8_t)(hyapk_lib_secret[i] ^ (HY_SECRET_MASK + i));
    }
    memset(&spec, 0, sizeof(spec));
    spec.lib_secret = lib_secret;
    spec.bridge = &hyjni_bridge;
    spec.program = prog;
    spec.program_size = plen;
    spec.args = args;
    spec.argc = argc;
    spec.ret = ret;
    rc = hyvm_run(&spec);
    hy_secure_zero(lib_secret, sizeof(lib_secret));
    return rc;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* generated-code API (hy_d2c_api.h)                                   */

#include "jni/hy_d2c_api.h"

void hy_d2c_set_env(JNIEnv *env) {
    hyjni_set_env(env);
}

long long hy_d2c_d2i(double d) {
    if (d != d) return 0;
    if (d >= 9.2233720368547758e18) return INT64_MAX;
    if (d <= -9.2233720368547758e18) return INT64_MIN;
    return (long long)d;
}

void hy_d2c_throw_arithmetic(void) {
    JNIEnv *env = t_env;
    if (env == NULL) return;
    {
        jclass cls = (*env)->FindClass(env, "java/lang/ArithmeticException");
        if (cls != NULL) {
            (*env)->ThrowNew(env, cls, "divide by zero");
        } else {
            (*env)->ExceptionClear(env);
        }
    }
}

hyvm_val hy_d2c_invoke(unsigned int method_idx, unsigned char kind, void *self,
                       const hyvm_val *args, unsigned char argc, int *ok) {
    hyvm_val ret;
    char pending = 0;
    hyvm_ctx stub;
    memset(&stub, 0, sizeof(stub));
    ret.i = 0;
    *ok = 1;
    if (jni_invoke(&stub, method_idx, kind, self, args, argc, &ret, &pending) != 0) {
        *ok = 0;
    } else if (pending) {
        *ok = 0; /* exception propagates at the JNI boundary */
    }
    return ret;
}

void *hy_d2c_const_string(unsigned int string_id, int *ok) {
    void *out = NULL;
    hyvm_ctx stub;
    memset(&stub, 0, sizeof(stub));
    *ok = jni_const_string(&stub, string_id, &out) == 0;
    return out;
}

void *hy_d2c_new_instance(unsigned int type_id, int *ok) {
    void *out = NULL;
    hyvm_ctx stub;
    memset(&stub, 0, sizeof(stub));
    *ok = jni_new_instance(&stub, type_id, &out) == 0;
    return out;
}
```
