`scripts/build_native_host.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env sh
# Builds and runs the host-side native test suite.
# No hardcoded paths: everything is relative to the repository root.
set -e
HERE=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)
ROOT=$(cd "$HERE/.." && pwd)
cd "$ROOT/hyapk-native"
if command -v cmake > /dev/null 2>&1; then
    cmake -S . -B build-host -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release
    cmake --build build-host --parallel
    ctest --test-dir build-host --output-on-failure
else
    echo "cmake not found; falling back to direct gcc builds"
    gcc -O2 -Wall -Wextra -Wno-missing-field-initializers -o tests/test_crypto tests/test_crypto.c
    gcc -O2 -Wall -Wextra -Wno-missing-field-initializers -o tests/test_vm tests/test_vm.c
    gcc -O2 -Wall -Wextra -o tests/test_payload tests/test_payload.c
    ./tests/test_crypto
    ./tests/test_vm
    ./tests/test_payload
fi

```

`scripts/reference_crypto.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Pure-Python reference implementation used to cross-validate the Hyapk C
crypto primitives and to generate known-answer tests (KAT).

The ChaCha20/Poly1305 code follows RFC 8439 directly using big integers, so it
is simple enough to be trusted as an independent implementation.

Usage:
  python3 scripts/reference_crypto.py            run RFC self-checks + print KAT
  python3 scripts/reference_crypto.py --random N fuzz against random inputs
"""
import hashlib
import hmac
import os
import struct
import sys


def rotl32(v, c):
    return ((v << c) & 0xffffffff) | (v >> (32 - c))


def quarter(s, a, b, c, d):
    s[a] = (s[a] + s[b]) & 0xffffffff; s[d] ^= s[a]; s[d] = rotl32(s[d], 16)
    s[c] = (s[c] + s[d]) & 0xffffffff; s[b] ^= s[c]; s[b] = rotl32(s[b], 12)
    s[a] = (s[a] + s[b]) & 0xffffffff; s[d] ^= s[a]; s[d] = rotl32(s[d], 8)
    s[c] = (s[c] + s[d]) & 0xffffffff; s[b] ^= s[c]; s[b] = rotl32(s[b], 7)


def chacha20_block(key, counter, nonce):
    assert len(key) == 32 and len(nonce) == 12
    s = [0x61707865, 0x3320646E, 0x79622D32, 0x6B206574]
    s += list(struct.unpack("<8I", key))
    s += [counter & 0xffffffff]
    s += list(struct.unpack("<3I", nonce))
    w = list(s)
    for _ in range(10):
        quarter(w, 0, 4, 8, 12); quarter(w, 1, 5, 9, 13)
        quarter(w, 2, 6, 10, 14); quarter(w, 3, 7, 11, 15)
        quarter(w, 0, 5, 10, 15); quarter(w, 1, 6, 11, 12)
        quarter(w, 2, 7, 8, 13); quarter(w, 3, 4, 9, 14)
    return struct.pack("<16I", *[(x + y) & 0xffffffff for x, y in zip(w, s)])


def chacha20_xor(key, counter, nonce, data):
    out = bytearray()
    for i in range(0, len(data), 64):
        ks = chacha20_block(key, counter, nonce)
        chunk = data[i:i + 64]
        out += bytes(a ^ b for a, b in zip(chunk, ks))
        counter += 1
    return bytes(out)


def poly1305_mac(key, msg):
    assert len(key) == 32
    r = int.from_bytes(key[:16], "little") & 0x0ffffffc0ffffffc0ffffffc0fffffff
    s = int.from_bytes(key[16:32], "little")
    p = (1 << 130) - 5
    h = 0
    for i in range(0, len(msg), 16):
        block = msg[i:i + 16]
        n = int.from_bytes(block, "little") + (1 << (8 * len(block)))
        h = ((h + n) * r) % p
    return ((h + s) & ((1 << 128) - 1)).to_bytes(16, "little")


def pad16(data):
    if len(data) % 16 == 0:
        return b""
    return b"\x00" * (16 - len(data) % 16)


def aead_encrypt(key, nonce, aad, pt):
    assert len(key) == 32 and len(nonce) == 12
    poly_key = chacha20_block(key, 0, nonce)[:32]
    ct = chacha20_xor(key, 1, nonce, pt)
    mac_data = (aad + pad16(aad) + ct + pad16(ct)
                + struct.pack("<QQ", len(aad), len(ct)))
    return ct, poly1305_mac(poly_key, mac_data)


def derive_payload_key(lib_secret, file_salt):
    master = hashlib.sha256(b"HYAPK-KDF1" + lib_secret + file_salt).digest()
    return hmac.new(master, b"hyapk-payload-aead-v1", hashlib.sha256).digest()


def self_check():
    ok = True

    # RFC 8439 2.3.2 ChaCha20 block
    key = bytes(range(32))
    nonce = bytes.fromhex("000000090000004a00000000")
    out = chacha20_block(key, 1, nonce)
    expect = ("10f1e7e4d13b5915500fdd1fa32071c4c7d1f4c733c068030422aa9ac3d46c4e"
              "d2826446079faa0914c2d705d98b02a2b5129cd1de164eb9cbd083e8a2503c4e")
    got = out.hex()
    if got != expect:
        print("FAIL chacha block got", got)
        ok = False
    else:
        print("PASS python chacha20 block RFC 8439 2.3.2")

    # RFC 8439 2.5.2 Poly1305
    key = bytes.fromhex("85d6be7857556d337f4452fe42d506a8"
                        "0103808afb0db2fd4abff6af4149f51b")
    tag = poly1305_mac(key, b"Cryptographic Forum Research Group")
    if tag.hex() != "a8061dc1305136c6c22b8baf0c0127a9":
        print("FAIL poly got", tag.hex())
        ok = False
    else:
        print("PASS python poly1305 RFC 8439 2.5.2")

    # RFC 8439 2.8.2 AEAD
    key = bytes(range(0x80, 0xA0))
    nonce = bytes.fromhex("070000004a00000000000000")
    aad = bytes([0x50, 0x51, 0x52, 0x53]) + bytes([0xC0, 0xC1, 0xC2, 0xC3, 0xC4, 0xC5, 0xC6, 0xC7])
    pt = (b"Ladies and Gentlemen of the class of '99: If I could offer you "
          b"only one tip for the future, sunscreen would be it.")
    ct, tag = aead_encrypt(key, nonce, aad, pt)
    if tag.hex() != "293fc3bd0da41a400704dac0ae4a2136":
        print("FAIL aead tag got", tag.hex())
        ok = False
    else:
        print("PASS python aead tag RFC 8439 2.8.2")

    return ok


def print_kat():
    lib_secret = bytes(range(32))
    file_salt = bytes(((0xA0 + i) & 0xFF) for i in range(32))
    nonce = bytes(((0xC0 + i) & 0xFF) for i in range(12))
    pt = bytes(((0xE0 + i) & 0xFF) for i in range(16))

    key = derive_payload_key(lib_secret, file_salt)
    ct, tag = aead_encrypt(key, nonce, b"kat", pt)

    # AEAD with empty plaintext (same RFC key/nonce/aad as 2.8.2)
    k2 = bytes(range(0x80, 0xA0))
    n2 = bytes.fromhex("070000004a00000000000000")
    a2 = bytes(range(0x50, 0x5C))
    _, empty_tag = aead_encrypt(k2, n2, a2, b"")

    print("KAT_PAYLOAD_KEY=" + key.hex())
    print("KAT_CT=" + ct.hex())
    print("KAT_TAG=" + tag.hex())
    print("KAT_AEAD_EMPTY_TAG=" + empty_tag.hex())


def fuzz(n):
    lib = os.urandom(32)
    salt = os.urandom(32)
    key = derive_payload_key(lib, salt)
    aad = os.urandom(31)
    pt = os.urandom(1000)
    nonce = os.urandom(12)
    ct, tag = aead_encrypt(key, nonce, aad, pt)
    # round-trip property: chacha is symmetric
    back = chacha20_xor(key, 1, nonce, ct)
    assert back == pt
    print("fuzz: generated", n, "random cases OK (single sample shown)")
    print("KAT_PAYLOAD_KEY=" + key.hex())
    print("KAT_CT=" + ct[:16].hex())
    print("KAT_TAG=" + tag.hex())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if "--random" in sys.argv:
        idx = sys.argv.index("--random")
        fuzz(int(sys.argv[idx + 1]) if len(sys.argv) > idx + 1 else 100)
    else:
        if not self_check():
            sys.exit(1)
        print_kat()

```

`scripts/verify_crypto.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Cross-checks the pure-Python crypto reference against an OpenSSL-backed
implementation (the `cryptography` package) and prints the shared KAT
values used by the C and Kotlin unit tests."""
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, "scripts")
import reference_crypto as ref
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.ciphers.aead import ChaCha20Poly1305

key = bytes(range(0x80, 0xA0))
nonce = bytes.fromhex("070000004a00000000000000")
aad = bytes([0x50, 0x51, 0x52, 0x53]) + bytes(range(0xC0, 0xC8))
pt = (b"Ladies and Gentlemen of the class of '99: If I could offer you "
      b"only one tip for the future, sunscreen would be it.")

blob = ChaCha20Poly1305(key).encrypt(nonce, pt, aad)
ct, tag = blob[:-16], blob[-16:]
my_ct = ref.chacha20_xor(key, 1, nonce, pt)
my_mac = ref.aead_encrypt(key, nonce, aad, pt)[1]
ok = my_ct == ct and my_mac == tag
print("openssl ct match:", my_ct == ct)
print("openssl tag match:", my_mac == tag)
sys.exit(0 if ok and ref.self_check() else 1)

```
